*William Lehn-Schiøler,Magnus Ruud Kjær,Rahul Thapa,Magnus Guldberg Pedersen,Anton Storgaard Mosquera,Nick Williams,Radu Gatej,Tue Lehn-Schiøler,Sándor Beniczky,Sadasivan Puthusserypady,James Zou,Lars Kai Hansen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过稀疏自编码器（SAEs）揭示了 EEG 基础模型的内部黑盒，实现了临床特征的精准操控与解释，并建立了评估模型安全性与偏见的分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG 基础模型虽然性能卓越，但内部计算过程黑盒化，缺乏可解释性，阻碍了其在临床环境中的信任与可靠应用。

Method: 使用 TopK 稀疏自编码器 (SAEs) 提取三种 EEG Transformer（SleepFM、REVE、LaBraM）的稀疏特征字典；通过临床分类学（异常、年龄、性别、药物）进行特征基础化；提出“目标 vs 非目标”探针以量化控制选择性；引入谱解码器将潜在空间操作映射回生理可解释的频谱特征。

Result: 成功在三种模型中提取了单语义特征；识别出三种操作机制（可选、纠缠、未编码）；量化了特定干预（如抑制病理慢波、恢复 α 波）对模型性能的影响，并暴露了模型中存在的临床概念耦合问题。

Conclusion: SAEs 可以有效解析 EEG 基础模型的内部表征，不仅提升了模型的可解释性，还揭示了模型存在的临床表征偏差（如年龄-病理混淆）和“破坏球”式干预风险。该工作为临床安全应用 EEG 基础模型提供了可解释性基准和操控工具。

Abstract: EEG foundation models achieve state-of-the-art clinical performance, yet the internal computations driving their predictions remain opaque: a barrier to clinical trust. We apply TopK Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) across three architecturally distinct EEG transformers: SleepFM, REVE, and LaBraM to extract sparse feature dictionaries from their embeddings. By grounding these features in a clinical taxonomy (abnormality, age, sex, and medication), we benchmark monosemanticity and entanglement across architectures. A single hyperparameter procedure, driven by an intrinsic dictionary health audit, transfers robustly across all three architectures. Via concept steering, we introduce a "target vs. off-target" probe area metric to quantify steering selectivity and reveal three operational regimes: selectively steerable, encoded but entangled, and non-encoded. This framework exposes critical representational failures: "wrecking-ball" interventions that collapse global model performance, and clinical entanglements, such as age-pathology confounding, where it is impossible to suppress one concept without corrupting the other. Finally, a spectral decoder maps these interventions back to the amplitude spectrum, translating latent manipulations into physiologically interpretable frequency signatures, such as pathological slow-wave suppression and $α$-band restoration.

</details>


### [150] [Rethinking Molecular OOD Generalization via Target-Aware Source Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13932)
*Zhuohao Lin,Kun Li,Jiameng Chen,Jiajun Yu,Duanhua Cao,Yizhen Zheng,Wenbin Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文旨在解决 AI 药物研发中极端分布外预测能力不足的问题，提出了严苛的基准评估 SCOPE-BENCH 以及基于强化学习的域适应框架 POMA，显著提升了分子属性预测的泛化准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究存在两点局限：1. 现有的支架划分（scaffold-splitting）无法过滤微观语义重叠，导致模型产生捷径学习，高估外推能力；2. 传统域适应方法在面对极端结构漂移时存在负迁移现象，导致模型性能下降。

Method: 1. 构建了 SCOPE-BENCH 基准：基于物理化学描述空间的聚类划分，以更严苛地评估 OOD 性能。 2. 提出了 POMA 框架：一种 Retrieve-Compose-Adapt 管道，利用强化学习策略动态选择最优源数据子集，并进行宏观拓扑与微观药效团尺度的双尺度域适应。

Result: 1. SCOPE-BENCH 评估显示，现有最先进模型在极度 OOD 下预测误差激增（最高 8.0 倍）；2. POMA 在多种骨干模型上实现了平均绝对误差（MAE）最高 11.2% 的降低，平均相对性能提升 6.2%。

Conclusion: SCOPE-BENCH 揭示了现有模型在极度分布外（OOD）场景下的脆弱性。提出的 POMA 框架通过检索、组合与自适应策略，有效缓解了负迁移问题，在 3D 分子属性预测任务中显著提升了泛化性能。

Abstract: Robust prediction of molecular properties under extreme out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios is a pivotal bottleneck in AI-driven drug discovery. Current scaffold-splitting protocols fail to obstruct microscopic semantic overlap, predisposing models to shortcut learning and overestimating their true extrapolation capability; meanwhile, conventional domain adaptation paradigms suffer under extreme structural shifts, as blindly aligning heterogeneous source libraries injects topological noise and triggers negative transfer. To address these two challenges, scaffold-cluster out-of-distribution performance evaluation benchmark (SCOPE-BENCH), a benchmark built on cluster-level partitioning in an explicit physicochemical descriptor space, is proposed alongside policy optimization for multi-source adaptation (POMA), a framework that formulates knowledge transfer as a retrieve-compose-adapt pipeline: labeled source scaffolds structurally close to the unlabeled target are first identified as proxy targets; a reinforcement learning policy then adaptively selects the optimal source subset from an exponentially large candidate pool; and dual-scale domain adaptation is finally performed at macroscopic topological and microscopic pharmacophore scales. Evaluations show that prediction errors of state-of-the-art 3D molecular models surge by up to 8.0x on SCOPE-BENCH with a mean of 5.9x, while POMA achieves up to an 11.2% reduction in mean absolute error with an average relative improvement of 6.2% across diverse backbone architectures. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Molecular-OOD-Code-73F6.

</details>


### [151] [Unsupervised learning of acquisition variability in structural connectomes via hybrid latent space modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13933)
*Gaurav Rudravaram,Lianrui Zuo,Karthik Ramadass,Elyssa McMaster,Jongyeon Yoon,Aravind R. Krishnan,Adam M. Saunders,Chenyu Gao,Nancy R. Newlin,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Lori L. Beason Held,Murat Bilgel,Laura A. Barquero,Micah DArchangel,Tin Q. Nguyen,Laurie B. Cutting,Derek Archer,Timothy J. Hohman,Daniel C. Moyer,Bennett A. Landman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型混合潜空间VAE，通过“架构退火”技术，在无需人工调参的情况下，成功从复杂的dMRI结构连接组中实现了采集效应（非生物变异）与生物特征的有效拆分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dMRI数据在不同站点、扫描仪和采集协议间的变异性严重影响结构连接组分析；传统降维方法无法有效区分采集噪声与生物学特征；现有混合潜空间模型通常需要繁琐的人工超参数调节。

Method: 提出了一种结合离散与连续成分的混合变分自编码器（Hybrid VAE），核心创新在于“架构退火”机制：通过在编码器输出端引入自适应退火过程，模型在训练期间能自动平衡离散和连续潜变量，而非依赖传统的损失函数加权手动调节。

Result: 在包含7,416例样本（覆盖跨度极广的年龄段及多种病理状态）的数据集上，该方法在站点识别任务中达到ARI=0.53，显著优于标准VAE、PCA+k-means及基于损失函数退火的混合模型，成功实现了采集效应的自动化解耦。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于架构退火（Architectural Annealing）的非监督学习框架，能够有效将弥散磁共振成像（dMRI）结构连接组数据中的采集效应（站点/扫描仪差异）与生物学变异分离开，避免了传统模型对离散/连续变量比例的手动调节需求。

Abstract: Acquisition differences across sites, scanners, and protocols in dMRI introduce variability that complicates structural connectome analysis. This motivates deep learning models that can represent high-dimensional connectomes in a low-dimensional space while explicitly separating acquisition-related effects from biological variation. Conventional dimensionality reduction methods model all variance as continuous, so acquisition effects often get absorbed into a continuous latent space. Recent hybrid latent-space models combine discrete and continuous components to address this, but typically require manual capacity tuning to ensure the discrete component captures the intended variability. We introduce an unsupervised framework that removes this manual tuning by architecturally annealing encoder outputs before decoding, allowing the model to adaptively balance discrete and continuous latent variables during training. To evaluate it, we curated a dataset of N=7,416 structural connectomes derived from dMRI, spanning ages 2 to 102 and 13 studies with 25 unique acquisition-parameter combinations. Of these, 5,900 are cognitively unimpaired, 877 have mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and 639 have Alzheimer's disease (AD). We compare against a standard VAE, PCA with k-means clustering, and hybrid models that anneal only through the loss function. Our architectural annealing produces stronger site learning (ARI=0.53, p<0.05) than these baselines. Results show that a hybrid continuous-discrete latent space, with architectural rather than loss-based annealing, provides a useful unsupervised mechanism for capturing acquisition variability in dMRI: by jointly modeling smooth and categorical structure, the Joint-VAE recovers clusters aligned with scanner and protocol differences.

</details>


### [152] [Towards the Next Frontier of LLMs, Training on Private Data: A Cross-Domain Benchmark for Federated Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13936)
*Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez,Enrique Zuazua,Georgios Kellaris,Joaquin del Rio,Oleksii Sliusarenko,Xabi Uribe-Etxebarria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在隐私保护和数据分布不均（non-IID）的情况下，如何通过联邦学习（Federated Learning）和参数高效微调（PEFT）技术实现对私有机构数据的 LLM 适配。研究表明，该框架能实现媲美中心化训练的效果，是利用受监管领域高价值私有数据的可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着高质量公共数据耗尽，挖掘医疗、金融等受监管领域受限于隐私和法规的私有数据成为提升 LLM 性能的关键。然而，这些数据存在分布式存储、无法共享以及严重的非独立同分布（non-IID）特征，需要一种无需数据交换的联邦协作式微调方法。

Method: 提出并实施了一个基于 Sherpa.ai 平台的联邦学习框架，在不交换私有数据的情况下进行 LLM 的联合微调。实验对比了 LoRA、QLoRA 和 IA3 三种 PEFT 策略，涵盖医疗（MedQA, MedMCQA）和金融（FPB, FiQA-SA）领域的四个数据集，并针对 institutional 非独立同分布（non-IID）特性进行了评估。

Result: 联邦微调的效果非常接近中心化训练，显著优于单个机构孤立地进行微调。特别是在能效方面，QLoRA 和 IA3 方法在保持较高精度的同时，大幅提升了资源利用效率，适应了受限的联邦环境。

Conclusion: 联邦参数高效微调（Federated PEFT）是一种在保护数据隐私的前提下，利用分布式非独立同分布（non-IID）数据提升大语言模型（LLM）领域性能的有效方案。该方法在性能上接近中心化训练，且优于孤立学习，并具备良好的能效表现（Green AI）。

Abstract: The recent success of large language models (LLMs) has been largely driven by vast public datasets. However, the next frontier for LLM development lies beyond public data. Much of the world's most valuable information is private, especially in highly regulated sectors such as healthcare and finance, where data include patient histories or customer communications. Unlocking this data could represent a major leap forward, enabling LLMs with deeper domain expertise and stronger real-world utility. Yet, these data cannot be shared because they are distributed across institutions and constrained by privacy, regulatory, and organizational barriers. Moreover, institutional datasets are typically non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID), differing across sites in population characteristics, data modalities, documentation patterns, and task-specific label distributions.
  In this paper, we demonstrate a practical approach to unlocking private and distributed institutional data for LLM adaptation through federated collaboration across data silos. Built on the Sherpa.ai Federated Learning platform, our framework enables nodes to jointly fine-tune a shared LLM without exchanging private data. We evaluate this approach through a cross-domain benchmark in healthcare and finance, using four closed-ended question answering and classification datasets: MedQA, MedMCQA, FPB, and FiQA-SA. We compare three parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) strategies-LoRA, QLoRA, and IA3-across pretrained backbones under non-IID settings reflecting institutional data heterogeneity. Our results show that federated fine-tuning performs close to centralized training and outperforms isolated single-institution learning. From a Green AI perspective, QLoRA and IA3 improve efficiency with limited accuracy degradation, supporting federated PEFT as a viable approach for adapting LLMs where data cannot be shared.

</details>


### [153] [EvolveMem:Self-Evolving Memory Architecture via AutoResearch for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13941)
*Jiaqi Liu,Xinyu Ye,Peng Xia,Zeyu Zheng,Cihang Xie,Mingyu Ding,Huaxiu Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvolveMem 是一种 LLM 记忆架构，它允许检索系统随存储内容自动进化，通过 LLM 自主诊断并调整检索策略，从而摆脱手动调优，显著提升了跨会话任务的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 长期记忆系统仅在数据层面动态演变，而检索机制、融合策略和生成策略通常是固定的。为了实现真正的自适应记忆，需要使存储知识与检索机制实现协同演化。

Method: EvolveMem 架构：将检索配置定义为结构化行动空间；引入 LLM 驱动的诊断模块，通过分析失败日志（failure logs）提出配置调整；配备带有自动回滚（revert-on-regression）和探索（explore-on-stagnation）机制的监控模块，实现闭环自进化（AutoResearch）。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 基准测试上，EvolveMem 较最强基线提升 25.7%，较初始基线提升 78.0%；在 MemBench 上较最强基线提升 18.9%。进化后的策略在跨基准测试中表现出正向迁移性。

Conclusion: EvolveMem 通过闭环自进化机制证明了检索系统不仅可以存储动态知识，其内部策略（即检索架构）也能自主优化，从而超越了人工调优的性能上限。所学习的检索配置具有良好的通用性，表明该方法捕捉到了通用的检索原则。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LLM agents that operate across multiple sessions, yet existing memory systems treat retrieval infrastructure as fixed: stored content evolves while scoring functions, fusion strategies, and answer-generation policies remain frozen at deployment. We argue that truly adaptive memory requires co-evolution at two levels: the stored knowledge and the retrieval mechanism that queries it. We present EvolveMem, a self-evolving memory architecture that exposes its full retrieval configuration as a structured action space optimized by an LLM-powered diagnosis module. In each evolution round, the module reads per-question failure logs, identifies root causes, and proposes targeted configuration adjustments; a guarded meta-analyzer applies them with automatic revert-on-regression and explore-on-stagnation safeguards. This closed-loop self-evolution realizes an AutoResearch process: the system autonomously conducts iterative research cycles on its own architecture, replacing manual configuration tuning. Starting from a minimal baseline, the process converges autonomously, discovering effective retrieval strategies including entirely new configuration dimensions not present in the original action space. On LoCoMo, EvolveMem outperforms the strongest baseline by 25.7% relative and achieves a 78.0% relative improvement over the minimal baseline. On MemBench, EvolveMem exceeds the strongest baseline by 18.9% relative. Evolved configurations transfer across benchmarks with positive rather than catastrophic transfer, indicating that the self-evolution process captures universal retrieval principles rather than benchmark-specific heuristics. Code is available at https://github.com/aiming-lab/SimpleMem.

</details>


### [154] [A Unified Geometric Framework for Weighted Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13943)
*Raphael Vock,Edouard Duchesnay,Benoit Dufumier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过距离几何视角揭示了对比学习中加权InfoNCE目标的几何属性，解决了监督分类中的类不平衡偏移和连续标签下的几何失配问题，为设计具有良好几何特性的对比目标提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习通过相似度图学习表征，但所得嵌入的几何结构缺乏理论理解，特别是在类不平衡和连续标签场景下的表现机制尚不明确。

Method: 利用距离几何问题（Distance Geometry Problems）的视角，将加权InfoNCE目标转化为对表征几何结构的定义，并分析了监督学习（SupCon/Soft SupCon）和连续标签设置下的最优解特性。

Result: 1. 在监督分类中，SupCon在类不平衡时会导致类间相似度不均匀，而Soft SupCon能保持正规单纯形（regular simplex）几何结构；2. 在连续标签设置下，y-Aware CL因标签和潜空间相似度的几何失配（球面与欧氏空间）导致无法达到最优；3. 提出了几何一致性的加权选择方案（如Euclidean-Euclidean weighting）。

Conclusion: 对比学习中加权方案的选择直接决定了表征空间几何结构的形成、退化或不一致性。该研究提供了一个原则性的框架，通过距离几何（Distance Geometry）视角指导对比学习目标的构建。

Abstract: Contrastive learning (CL) aims to preserve relational structure between samples by learning representations that reflect a similarity graph. Yet, the geometry of the resulting embeddings remains poorly understood. Here we show that weighted InfoNCE objectives can be interpreted as Distance Geometry Problems, where the weighting scheme specifies the target geometry to be realized by the representation. This viewpoint yields exact characterizations of the optimal embeddings for several supervised and weakly supervised objectives. In supervised classification, both SupCon and Soft SupCon (a dense relaxation of it where pairs from distinct classes have small non-zero similarity) collapse samples within each class to a single prototype. However, while balanced SupCon recovers the classical regular simplex geometry, class imbalance breaks this symmetry: SupCon induces non-uniform inter-class similarities depending on class sizes, whereas Soft SupCon preserves a regular simplex geometry regardless of class imbalance. In continuous-label settings, our framework reveals a different failure mode: y-Aware CL generally cannot attain its entropic optimum unless the labels lie on a hypersphere, exposing a mismatch between Euclidean label weights and spherical latent similarity. By contrast, geometrically consistent choices such as Euclidean-Euclidean weighting or X-CLR admit unique optimal embeddings. Our results show that the choice of weighting scheme determines whether contrastive learning is geometrically realizable, degenerate, or inconsistent, providing a principled framework for designing contrastive objectives.

</details>


### [155] [WarmPrior: Straightening Flow-Matching Policies with Temporal Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13959)
*Sinjae Kang,Chanyoung Kim,Kaixin Wang,Li Zhao,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文引入 WarmPrior，通过利用动作历史优化生成式机器人的源分布，简化了概率路径，从而提升了控制任务的成功率与强化学习的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前基于扩散和流匹配的生成式策略在机器人控制中可能存在的训练效率和推理路径优化问题，探索源分布对生成策略的影响。

Method: 提出了一种名为 WarmPrior 的方法，将标准的高斯源分布替换为基于近期动作历史的临时接地先验（temporally grounded prior），并将其应用于行为克隆和先验空间强化学习中。

Result: WarmPrior 显著提升了机器人操作任务的成功率；通过产生更直的概率路径（类似于 Rectified Flow 中的最优传输耦合）增强了生成效果；同时在强化学习中提高了采样效率和最终性能。

Conclusion: 源分布的设计是生成式机器人控制中一个重要但未被充分探索的维度；WarmPrior通过利用短期动作历史简化了生成路径，显著提升了机器人策略的性能。

Abstract: Generative policies based on diffusion and flow matching have become a dominant paradigm for visuomotor robotic control. We show that replacing the standard Gaussian source distribution with WarmPrior, a simple temporally grounded prior constructed from readily available recent action history, consistently improves success rates on robotic manipulation tasks. We trace this gain to markedly straighter probability paths, echoing the effect of optimal-transport couplings in Rectified Flow. Beyond standard behavior cloning, WarmPrior also reshapes the exploration distribution in prior-space reinforcement learning, improving both sample efficiency and final performance. Collectively, these results identify the source distribution as an important and underexplored design axis in generative robot control.

</details>


### [156] [Towards Resource-Efficient LLMs: End-to-End Energy Accounting of Distillation Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13981)
*Katherine Lambert,Sasha Luccioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对大型语言模型蒸馏过程进行了全面的生命周期能耗审计，指出学术界以往忽略了教师模型的计算开销，并提出了基于能效-性能权衡的蒸馏优化建议与开源评估工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示了当前模型蒸馏研究普遍忽略了完整的端到端能源成本（特别是教师侧的工作负载），导致看似“高效”的模型实则在全生命周期中可能并不环保。

Method: 提出了一个全阶段能量核算框架，通过阶段性追踪GPU设备功耗，对经典的基于Logit的知识蒸馏和合成数据监督微调进行对比分析，并构建了能量-质量（Energy-Quality）帕累托前沿。

Result: 量化了蒸馏管道中常常被忽视的隐形成本，揭示了蒸馏方法及其超参数不仅影响模型性能，还显著影响整体碳足迹。实验结果表明，在考虑完整生命周期能耗后，蒸馏的性价比存在明显的权衡取舍。

Conclusion: 蒸馏并非总是节能的；必须将教师模型的计算成本（数据生成、缓存、评估）纳入全生命周期能耗考量。研究通过测量构建了能效-性能帕累托前沿，提供了指导蒸馏任务选择的模型配置原则，并开源了评估工具以推动研究的可持续性。

Abstract: The rise in deployment of large language models has driven a surge in GPU demand and datacenter scaling, raising concerns about electricity use, grid stress, and the impacts of modern AI workloads. Distillation is often promoted as one of the most effective paths to obtain cheaper, more efficient models, yet these claims rarely account for the full end-to-end energy and resource costs, including crucial teacher-side workloads such as data generation, logit caching, and evaluation. We present a comprehensive energy accounting framework that measures the complete computational cost of distillation pipelines via detailed stage-wise tracking of GPU device power consumption. In our experiments, we separate and log empirical energy use across distinct phases and systematically measure the energy and emissions of two common distillation methods: the classic logit-based knowledge distillation and synthetic-data supervised fine-tuning, constructing energy-quality Pareto frontiers that expose the previously ignored costs. From these measurements and analyses, we derive practical design rules for selecting distillation methods and hyperparameters under energy and budget constraints, and release an open-source measurement harness and accounting protocol to provide a standardized foundation for comparable, reproducible distillation research, explicitly accountable for complete pipeline energy impact.

</details>


### [157] [Finite Sample Bounds for Learning with Score Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14168)
*Devin Smedira,Abhijith Jayakumar,Sidhant Misra,Marc Vuffray,Andrey Y. Lokhov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次推导了多项式指数族模型在分数匹配下的非渐近样本复杂度界，证明了该方法在高维统计中的多项式收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管分数匹配在学习连续指数族分布方面计算效率高，但其统计特性的理论研究尚不充分；既往研究多仅限于渐近分析，缺乏针对高维情境下的非渐近统计保证。

Method: 利用分数匹配（Score Matching）方法对多项式指数族分布进行学习，并对其进行非渐近（non-asymptotic）样本复杂度分析。

Result: 导出了多项式指数族模型在分数匹配下的非渐近样本复杂度边界，该边界表明样本复杂度与模型维数呈多项式关系。

Conclusion: 本文首次为多项式指数族模型的分数匹配估计提供了非渐近样本复杂度边界，证明了该方法在处理高维连续分布时的理论有效性。

Abstract: Learning of continuous exponential family distributions with unbounded support remains an important area of research for both theory and applications in high-dimensional statistics. In recent years, score matching has become a widely used method for learning exponential families with continuous variables due to its computational ease when compared against maximum likelihood estimation. However, theoretical understanding of the statistical properties of score matching is still lacking. In this work, we provide a non-asymptotic sample complexity analysis for learning the structure of exponential families of polynomials with score matching. The derived sample bounds show a polynomial dependence on the model dimension. These bounds are the first of its kind, as all prior work has shown only asymptotic bounds on the sample complexity.

</details>


### [158] [TabPFN-3: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13986)
*Léo Grinsztajn,Klemens Flöge,Oscar Key,Felix Birkel,Philipp Jund,Brendan Roof,Mihir Manium,Shi Bin,Hoo,Magnus Bühler,Anurag Garg,Dominik Safaric,Jake Robertson,Benjamin Jäger,Simone Alessi,Adrian Hayler,Vladyslav Moroshan,Lennart Purucker,Philipp Singer,Alan Arazi,Julien Siems,Jan Hendrik Metzen,Georg Grab,Nick Erickson,Siyuan Guo,Eliott Kalfon,Simon Bing,David Salinas,Clara Cornu,Lilly Charlotte Wehrhahn,Diana Kriuchkova,Kursat Kaya,Lydia Sidhoum,Marie Salmon,Jerry Chen,Madelon Hulsebos,Yann LeCun,Samuel Müller,Bernhard Schölkopf,Sauraj Gambhir,Noah Hollmann,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN-3 是一款高性能表格基模型，通过测试时间计算伸缩和架构优化，在处理 100 万行规模数据集时实现了极高预测性能与推理速度，超越了传统的集成学习与 AutoML 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展 TabPFN 的能力以应对更大规模（1M 行）的表格数据集，同时提升推理速度，并拓宽其在时间序列、关系数据及含文本表格数据中的通用性。

Method: 提出 TabPFN-3 架构，采用仅在合成数据上预训练的方法，引入行分块（row-chunking）技术和减少 KV 缓存来处理高达 100 万行的数据，并开发了 TabPFN-3-Plus（Thinking）API 以利用测试时间计算伸缩提升 Elo 评分。

Result: 在 TabArena 基准测试中大幅超越所有基线模型（包括 AutoGluon），在 RelBenchV1 和 TabSTAR 上达到 SOTA；推理速度相比 TabPFN-2.5 提升 20 倍；支持处理 1M 行数据且在 8 小时微调的梯度提升树基线上表现更优。

Conclusion: TabPFN-3 标志着表格数据基模型（Tabular Foundation Models）的重大进步，通过引入测试时间计算伸缩（test-time compute scaling）和更高效的架构，在处理大规模、多模态（时间序列、关系型、文本）表格数据时，在性能与速度上均优于现有的集成学习和自动机器学习（AutoML）方案。

Abstract: Tabular data underpins most high-value prediction problems in science and industry, and TabPFN has driven the foundation model revolution for this modality. Designed with feedback from our users, TabPFN-3 builds on this foundation to scale state-of-the-art performance to datasets with 1M training rows and substantially reduce training and inference time. Pretrained exclusively on synthetic data from our prior, TabPFN-3 dramatically pushes the frontier of tabular prediction and brings substantial gains on time series, relational, and tabular-text data. On the standard tabular benchmark TabArena, a forward pass of TabPFN-3 outperforms all other models, including tuned and ensembled baselines, by a significant margin, and pareto-dominates the speed/performance frontier. On more diverse datasets, TabPFN-3 ranks first on datasets with many classes, and beats 8-hour-tuned gradient-boosted-tree baselines on datasets up to 1M training rows and 200 features. TabPFN-3 introduces test-time compute scaling to tabular foundation models. Our API offering TabPFN-3-Plus (Thinking) exploits this to beat all non-TabPFN models by over 200 Elo on TabArena, rising to 420 Elo on the largest data subset, and outperforms AutoGluon 1.5 extreme while being 10x faster, without using LLMs, real data, internet search or any other model besides TabPFN. TabPFN-3 extends the capabilities of our models, enabling SOTA prediction on relational data (new SOTA foundation model on RelBenchV1) and tabular-text data (SOTA on TabSTAR via TabPFN-3-Plus); and improves existing integrations: a specialized checkpoint, TabPFN-TS-3, ranks 2nd on the time-series benchmark fev-bench, and SHAP-value computation is up to 120x faster. TabPFN-3 achieves this performance while being up to 20x faster than TabPFN-2.5. In addition, a reduced KV cache and row-chunking scale to 1M rows on one H100 with fast inference speed.

</details>


### [159] [Support Before Frequency in Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13999)
*Adrian Müller,Antoine Gonon,Zebang Shen,Ya-Ping Hsieh,Niao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究揭示离散扩散模型在训练时存在“先学结构/有效性，后学频率/分布”的层级机制，并通过理论与实验证实了不同扩散机制在这一学习过程中的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管离散扩散模型在语言建模中表现出色，但其去噪目标如何组织学习过程（即学习的优先级）尚不明确。

Method: 从理论维度对均匀扩散（uniform diffusion）和吸收扩散（absorbing diffusion/masking）的逆向去噪过程进行数学推导，证明了小噪声机制下逆向编辑分解为“主导尺度”（数据支持集/语法性）和“细粒度系数”（频率分布），并通过语言模型实验和合成正则语言任务进行验证。

Result: 证明了在最终去噪步骤的小噪声机制下，逆向编辑可解耦；发现支持集定位的收敛速度早于频率排名；揭示了吸收扩散模型比均匀扩散模型更倾向于优先执行提升有效性的更新，且实验结果与理论预测高度吻合。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型在学习过程中存在阶段性，即模型会先学习数据支持集（数据的结构/语法有效性），随后才学习数据频率分布（细粒度概率分布）。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are increasingly competitive for language modeling, yet it remains unclear how their denoising objectives organize learning. Although these objectives target the full data distribution, we show that the exact reverse process induces a hierarchy between coarse support information and finer frequency information. For uniform and absorbing (a.k.a. masking) diffusion, we prove that, in the small-noise regime of the final denoising steps, each single-token reverse edit decomposes into a leading scale, determined by whether it moves toward the data support (e.g., grammatically valid sentences), and a finer coefficient, determining relative probabilities within the same scale. Thus, recovering validity structure only requires learning the correct order of magnitude of reverse probabilities, whereas recovering data frequencies requires coefficient-level estimation. The separation is mechanism-dependent: uniform diffusion exhibits a trichotomy into validity-improving, validity-preserving, and validity-worsening edits, while absorbing diffusion places its leading-order mass on validity-improving moves. Experiments on a masked language diffusion model and synthetic regular-language tasks support these predictions: support-localization emerges earlier than within-support frequency ranking, and the contrast between uniform and absorbing diffusion matches the predicted rate separation. Together, our results suggest that discrete diffusion models learn data support before data frequencies.

</details>


### [160] [Dywave: Event-Aligned Dynamic Tokenization for Heterogeneous IoT Sensing Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14014)
*Tomoyoshi Kimura,Denizhan Kara,Jinyang Li,Hongjue Zhao,Yigong Hu,Yizhuo Chen,Xiaomin Ouyang,Shengzhong Liu,Tarek Abdelzaher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Dywave是一种针对IoT感知信号的动态分词框架，通过结合小波分解与事件检测，在大幅压缩输入长度的前提下提升了IoT智能任务的精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT系统中的感知信号具有典型的非平稳性（non-stationary）和多尺度（multi-scale）特性，现有的通用分词技术难以有效处理这些信号以支持智能应用。

Method: 提出了Dywave动态分词框架：1. 利用基于小波的层级分解技术（wavelet-based hierarchical decomposition）；2. 识别与物理事件相对应的动态时间边界；3. 自适应地压缩冗余时间区间，同时保持时间连贯性。

Result: 在5个真实IoT感知数据集上进行测试，准确率最高提升12%，计算效率提升显著（输入token长度减少高达75%），并展现出更好的域自适应能力与序列长度鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Dywave通过基于小波的动态分词方法，有效解决了IoT感知信号非平稳及多尺度带来的挑战，在提升模型性能的同时显著降低了计算开销，为IoT序列建模提供了一种高效、稳健的表示学习范式。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) systems continuously collect heterogeneous sensing signals from ubiquitous sensors to support intelligent applications such as human activity analysis, emotion monitoring, and environmental perception. These signals are inherently non-stationary and multi-scale, posing unique challenges for standard tokenization techniques. This paper proposes Dywave, a dynamic tokenization framework for IoT sensing signals that constructs compact input representations aligned with intrinsic temporal structures and underlying physical events. Dywave leverages wavelet-based hierarchical decomposition, identifies meaningful temporal boundaries corresponding to underlying semantic events, and adaptively compresses redundant intervals while preserving temporal coherence. Extensive evaluations on five real-world IoT sensing datasets across activity recognition, stress assessment, and nearby object detection demonstrate that Dywave outperforms state-of-the-art methods by up to 12% in accuracy, while improving computational efficiency by reducing input token lengths by up to 75% across mainstream sequence models. Moreover, Dywave exhibits improved robustness to domain shifts and varying sequence lengths.

</details>


### [161] [R2R2: Robust Representation for Intensive Experience Reuse via Redundancy Reduction in Self-Predictive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14026)
*Sanghyeob Song,Donghyeok Lee,Jinsik Kim,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对强化学习在高频率数据复用下的过拟合难题，本文提出R2R2正则化方法，通过优化自预测学习（SPL）中的表示学习冲突，显著提升了机器人在数据稀缺环境下的训练效率与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人等数据稀缺领域，通过高频率更新（高UTD）来提高数据利用率会导致模型在自预测学习（SPL）中产生表示层的不稳定性及过拟合。现有研究多关注于Critic偏差，而缺乏对表示层稳定性的深入探讨。

Method: 提出了“通过冗余减少实现鲁棒表征”（R2R2）方法。核心设计包括：识别并纠正了标准零中心化处理与SPL谱属性之间的冲突，设计了非中心化的优化目标，并将其整合至TD7及SimbaV2-SPL框架中。

Result: 在11项连续控制任务中，当UTD比率为20时，R2R2将TD7的性能提升了约22%；同时，R2R2与SimbaV2-SPL结合后取得了新的SOTA效果。

Conclusion: R2R2通过抑制自预测学习机制下的过拟合，有效提升了机器人在高数据回放频率（UTD）环境下的强化学习性能，并证明了其作为正则化方法的通用性和互补性。

Abstract: For reinforcement learning in data-scarce domains like real-world robotics, intensive data reuse enhances efficiency but induces overfitting. While prior works focus on critic bias, representation-level instability in Self-Predictive Learning (SPL) under high Update-to-Data (UTD) regimes remains underexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose Robust Representation via Redundancy Reduction (R2R2), a regularization method within SPL. We theoretically identify that standard zero-centering conflicts with SPL's spectral properties and design a non-centered objective accordingly. We verify R2R2 on SPL-native algorithms like TD7. Furthermore, to demonstrate its orthogonality to prior advancements, we extend the state-of-the-art SimbaV2, which originally lacks SPL, by integrating a tailored SPL module, termed SimbaV2-SPL. Experiments across 11 continuous control tasks confirm that R2R2 effectively mitigates overfitting; specifically, at a UTD ratio of 20, it improves TD7 by ~22% and provides additional gains on top of SimbaV2-SPL, which itself establishes a new state-of-the-art. The code can be found at: https://github.com/songsang7/R2R2

</details>


### [162] [Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention: Learning When to Write by Predicting Future Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14037)
*Gergely Szilvasy,Manuel Faysse,Maria Lomeli,Matthijs Douze,Pierre-Emmanuel Mazaré,Loïc Cabannes,Wen-tau Yih,Hervé Jégou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 SP-KV 机制，通过轻量化预测器动态剪枝 KV 缓存，在实现 3-10 倍存储压缩的同时维持了模型性能，有效解决了长序列推理中的内存瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大模型处理长序列时，Transformer 的 KV 缓存占用巨大的内存带宽和存储空间，限制了高效生成。

Method: 引入“自剪枝键值注意力”（SP-KV）机制：使用轻量级效用预测器评估每个 KV 对的价值，保留近期 KV 缓存，对旧 KV 进行基于阈值的动态稀疏化，并与 LLM 进行端到端的联合训练。

Result: 在保持性能几乎无损的前提下，将 KV 缓存大小减少了 3 到 10 倍，提升了解码速度和内存效率，且在长序列上表现出更高压缩率。

Conclusion: SP-KV 是一种有效的动态 KV 缓存压缩机制，能够在保持模型性能的同时显著降低内存占用和提升推理速度，并揭示了模型内部的稀疏性分布模式。

Abstract: Under modern test-time compute and agentic paradigms, language models process ever-longer sequences. Efficient text generation with transformer architectures is increasingly constrained by the Key-Value cache memory footprint and bandwidth. To address this limitation, we introduce Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention (SP-KV), a mechanism designed to predict future KV utility in order to reduce the size of the long-term KV cache. This strategy operates at a fine granularity: a lightweight utility predictor scores each key-value pair, and while recent KVs are always available via a local window, older pairs are written in the cache and used in global attention only if their predicted utility surpasses a given threshold. The LLM and the utility predictor are trained jointly end-to-end exclusively through next-token prediction loss, and are adapted from pretrained LLM checkpoints.
  Rather than enforcing a fixed compression ratio, SP-KV performs dynamic sparsification: the mechanism adapts to the input and typically reduces the KV cache size by a factor of $3$ to $10\times$, longer sequences often being more compressible. This leads to vast improvements in memory usage and decoding speed, with little to no degradation of validation loss nor performance on a broad set of downstream tasks. Beyond serving as an effective KV-cache reduction mechanism, our method reveals structured layer- and head-specific sparsity patterns that we can use to guide the design of hybrid local-global attention architectures.

</details>


### [163] [Reliability-Gated Source Anchoring for Continual Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14063)
*Vikash Singh,Debargha Ganguly,Weicong Chen,Sabyasachi Sahoo,Sreehari Sankar,Biyao Zhang,Mohsen Harir,Shouren Wang,Osama Zafar,Christian Gagné,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 CTTA 中源模型不可靠导致的“盲目锚定”问题，RMemSafe 通过熵门控机制实时动态调整对源模型的依赖，在源模型失效时平滑切换至无源回退策略，大幅提升了模型在持续腐蚀环境下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在持续测试时间适应（CTTA）中，现有的源锚定方法在源模型本身性能极差（如严重的分布偏移）时仍强行依赖该锚点，导致“盲目锚定”失效，严重影响模型适应效果。

Method: 提出 RMemSafe 算法，基于冻结源模型的归一化预测熵构建可靠性门控。当源模型因分布偏移变得不可靠（预测熵接近均匀分布）时，自动减弱甚至消除对源锚点（source anchor）及一致性过滤器的依赖，切换至无源依赖的回退机制（包含基础损失与边缘校准）。

Result: 在 CCC-Hard 数据集及多个交叉验证设定下，RMemSafe 在 9 个测试单元中的 8 个取得了最低误差；相比 ROID+ASR，在 ResNet-50 和 ViT-B/16 上分别提升了 1.05 pp 和 0.48 pp 的性能，并表现出更优的抗源退化能力。

Conclusion: RMemSafe 通过引入基于源模型预测熵的可靠性门控机制，有效解决了 CTTA 在源模型失效时的“盲目锚定”问题，在多种持续腐蚀场景下显著提升了模型的稳健性与适应性能。

Abstract: Continual test-time adaptation (CTTA) updates a pretrained model online on an unlabeled, non-stationary stream while anchoring it to a frozen source checkpoint. This anchor is useful only when the source remains reliable. On CCC-Hard, however, a ResNet-50 source falls to approximately $1.3\%$ top-$1$ accuracy, while existing source-anchored CTTA methods continue applying the same anchor strength. We call this failure mode blind anchoring and propose RMemSafe, a reliability-gated extension of ROID that uses the frozen source's normalized predictive entropy to attenuate all explicit source-coupled uses in the objective. When the source posterior approaches uniformity, the gate closes: the source anchor and agreement filter vanish, and the objective reduces to a source-agnostic fallback comprising ROID's base losses plus marginal calibration. Combined with ASR, RMemSafe achieves the lowest error on $8$ of $9$ matched-split continual-corruption cells and is the best reset-based method on all $9$, improving ROID+ASR by $1.05$~pp on ResNet-50 and $0.48$~pp on ViT-B/16. A controlled source-degradation sweep shows a $1.13{\times}$ shallower harm slope than ROID+ASR, consistent with the graceful-decay prediction. The entropy gate detects high-entropy source collapse, not confidently wrong low-entropy sources; this scope is explicitly evaluated and discussed.

</details>


### [164] [Policy Optimization in Hybrid Discrete-Continuous Action Spaces via Mixed Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14297)
*Matias Alvo,Daniel Russo,Yash Kanoria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对混合离散-连续动作空间的强化学习，提出了一种结合路径导数和分数函数梯度的混合梯度估计方法（HPO），有效解决了既有方法在信用分配和梯度质量上的不足，并在多个基准测试中表现出超越PPO的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在离散+连续动作的混合空间中，标准强化学习方法（如SF估计器）在高维下存在严重的信用分配问题；而微分模拟方法在处理离散动作或非平滑动力学时会产生偏差或无用梯度。

Method: 提出混合策略优化（Hybrid Policy Optimization, HPO），结合路径导数（pathwise gradients）与分数函数（SF）梯度，实现了一种无偏的混合梯度估计器；同时允许在存在动作不连续的场景下进行重构以适用该方法。

Result: 在库存控制和切换线性二次调节（LQR）问题中，HPO性能大幅超过PPO，且随着连续动作维度的增加，性能优势愈发明显。

Conclusion: HPO通过混合梯度估计器有效解决了混合动作空间中基于分数函数估计带来的高方差和基于微分模拟带来的非平稳问题，实现了更优的样本效率和性能，且理论上揭示了优化趋于最优时离散与连续动作可近似解耦的特性。

Abstract: We study reinforcement learning in hybrid discrete-continuous action spaces, such as settings where the discrete component selects a regime (or index) and the continuous component optimizes within it -- a structure common in robotics, control, and operations problems. Standard model-free policy gradient methods rely on score-function (SF) estimators and suffer from severe credit-assignment issues in high-dimensional settings, leading to poor gradient quality. On the other hand, differentiable simulation largely sidesteps these issues by backpropagating through a simulator, but the presence of discrete actions or non-smooth dynamics yields biased or uninformative gradients. To address this, we propose Hybrid Policy Optimization (HPO), which backpropagates through the simulator wherever smoothness permits, using a mixed gradient estimator that combines pathwise and SF gradients while maintaining unbiasedness. We also show how problems with action discontinuities can be reformulated in hybrid form, further broadening its applicability. Empirically, HPO substantially outperforms PPO on inventory control and switched linear-quadratic regulator problems, with performance gaps increasing as the continuous action dimension grows. Finally, we characterize the structure of the mixed gradient, showing that its cross term -- which captures how continuous actions influence future discrete decisions -- becomes negligible near a discrete best response, thereby enabling approximate decentralized updates of the continuous and discrete components and reducing variance near optimality. All resources are available at github.com/MatiasAlvo/hybrid-rl.

</details>


### [165] [SurF: A Generative Model for Multivariate Irregular Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14069)
*Mohammad R. Rezaei,Tejas Balaji,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对非规则多变量事件流生成建模难题，本文提出 SurF 模型。该模型通过学习型双射（TRT）将事件流映射为指数噪声，实现了高效的跨数据集训练与长序列建模，在多个基准测试中超越了现有基线，为异步事件流领域的基础模型研究奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成建模方法在非规则采样多变量事件流任务上表现不佳：基于分词的方法无法处理跨度极大的时间间隔；神经时序点过程（NTPP）则受限于计算复杂的窗口级数值积分（numerical quadrature）。

Method: 1. 利用时间重缩放定理（Time Rescaling Theorem, TRT）构建事件序列与 i.i.d. 单位速率指数噪声之间的学习型双射；2. 提出了三种高效的累积强度参数化方法以支持长序列扩展；3. 设计了基于 Transformer 的编码器，支持多数据集预训练。

Result: 在 6 个真实基准数据集上，SurF 在 3 个数据集上达到了最优时间 RMSE；在留一法验证中，其预训练检查点在 5/6 的数据集上优于经典及神经自回归基线，在 Amazon 和 Earthquake 上表现最优。

Conclusion: SurF 是异步事件流生成建模的重大进展，通过引入学习型双射实现了跨数据集的统一建模，并展示了作为异步事件流基础模型（Foundation Model）的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Irregularly sampled multivariate event streams remain a stubbornly difficult modality for generative modeling: tokenization-based approaches break down when inter-event intervals vary by orders of magnitude, and neural temporal point processes are bottlenecked by window-level numerical quadrature. We (i) propose SurF, a generative model that uses the Time Rescaling Theorem (TRT) as a learnable bijection between event sequences and i.i.d.\ unit-rate exponential noise, enabling a single model to be trained across heterogeneous event-stream datasets; (ii) three efficient parameterizations of the cumulative intensity that scale to long sequences; and (iii) a Transformer-based encoder for multi-dataset pretraining. On six real-world benchmarks, SurF achieves the best reported time RMSE on Earthquake, Retweet, and Taobao, and is within trial-level noise of the strongest specialist on the remaining three. Under a strict leave-one-out protocol, the held-out checkpoint beats every classical and neural-autoregressive baseline on 5/6 datasets and beats every baseline on Amazon and Earthquake, an initial step toward foundation models over asynchronous event streams.

</details>


### [166] [Language-Induced Priors for Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14301)
*Qiyuan Chen,Jiayu Zhou,Raed Al Kontar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对冷启动领域适应中的负迁移难题，本文提出利用LLM将目标域文本描述转化为语义先验（LIP），通过EM算法引导源域选择，实现了小样本下的高效适应与大样本下的稳健收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在领域适应的冷启动阶段，由于目标数据匮乏，现有统计方法难以区分相关源领域，极易发生负迁移，而往往被忽视的专家文本描述可作为缓解此问题的有效语义信息。

Method: 提出一种概率框架，通过预训练LLM将目标域的专家文本描述转化为“语言诱导先验”（LIP）；将该先验集成至期望最大化（EM）算法中，用于动态识别并加权相关源领域，该方法兼容任何具有似然函数的参数模型。

Result: 理论上证明了该估计器在先验正确时接近预言机（Oracle）的冷启动均方误差，并在先验不准确时依然保持渐进一致性；实验在描述性（高斯估计）、预测性（C-MAPSS）及规定性（MuJoCo）任务中验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的“语言诱导先验”（LIP）框架有效解决了冷启动场景下领域适应的负迁移问题，证明了利用LLM语义信息可作为有效先验，兼顾了冷启动阶段的指导性和大样本下的渐进一致性。

Abstract: Domain adaptation faces a fundamental paradox in the cold-start regime. When target data is scarce, statistical methods fail to distinguish relevant source domains from irrelevant ones, which often leads to negative transfer. In this paper, we address this challenge by leveraging expert textual descriptions of the target domain, a resource that is often available but overlooked. We propose a probabilistic framework that translates these semantic descriptions into a choice model, namely a Language-Induced Prior (LIP), that learns the preferences from a pretrained Large Language Model (LLM). The LIP is then integrated into an Expectation-Maximization algorithm to identify source relevance. Methodologically, this framework is compatible with any parametric model where a likelihood is available. It allows the LIP to guide the selection of sources when target signals are weak, while gradually refining these choices as samples accumulate. Theoretically, we prove that the estimator roughly matches an oracle cold-start MSE under a correct prior, while remaining asymptotically consistent regardless of the quality of the LIP. Empirically, we validated the framework on a descriptive (Gaussian estimation), a predictive (C-MAPSS dataset), and a prescriptive task (MuJoCo hopper).

</details>


### [167] [Nearest-Neighbor Radii under Dependent Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14343)
*Yuanyuan Gao,Yilong Hou,Zhexiao Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了强混合依赖数据下最近邻半径的几何属性，证明了即使在数据存在依赖性的情况下，最近邻方法依然稳健，并给出了基于局部内在维度的非渐近误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最近邻方法多基于独立同分布（i.i.d.）假设，缺乏对依赖型数据（如时间序列）几何结构的理论支撑，需探讨依赖性是否会重构最近邻邻域的尺度。

Method: 研究了强混合时间序列下的最近邻半径几何，建立了多项式混合下的几乎确定（almost sure）收敛性，以及几何混合下的夏普（sharp）非渐近矩界。

Result: 理论结果表明，即便在依赖采样下，最近邻半径的度量仍能反映局部数据密度；推导出的非渐近矩界受局部内在维度制约，而非环境维度；实验验证了该理论在合成数据及真实时间序列中的适用性。

Conclusion: 在强混合依赖关系下，最近邻半径的几何属性与独立采样下的行为一致，且收敛速度与局部内在维度相关，证明了最近邻方法在依赖型数据（如时间序列）中依然有效。

Abstract: Nearest-neighbor methods are fundamental to classical and modern machine learning, yet their geometric properties are typically analyzed under independent sampling. In this paper, we study the nearest-neighbor radii under dependent sampling. We consider strong mixing dependent observations and ask whether dependence changes the scale of nearest-neighbor neighborhoods. We establish distribution-free almost sure convergence under polynomial mixing and sharp non-asymptotic moment bounds under geometric mixing. The moment bounds depend on the local intrinsic dimension rather than the ambient dimension, making the results applicable to high-dimensional data concentrated near lower-dimensional manifolds. Synthetic experiments and real-world time-series benchmarks support the theory, showing that nearest-neighbor geometry remains informative under dependence sampling.

</details>


### [168] [Fair and Calibrated Toxicity Detection with Robust Training and Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14074)
*Mokshit Surana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对毒性分类系统中排序、校准和弃权三个轴向的分析，指出目前主流的训练和后处理方法在解决公平性时存在深层的权衡与失效。研究证实，单一指标无法衡量系统公平性，需要通过多轴框架重新评估模型在实际场景中的真实风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的毒性分类公平性评估往往是割裂的。训练干预与后处理机制并非独立，论文旨在揭示这三维度（排序、校准、弃权）之间复杂的相互作用及其对系统公平性的隐含影响。

Method: 对比了三种训练策略（ERM、实例加权、Group DRO）与三种后处理/决策机制（温度缩放、置信度弃权、人均阈值优化）；使用子群AUC、BPSN/BNSP指标、误差差距及子群预期校准误差（ECE）进行全面评估。

Result: 1. 校准差异是隐性不公平点；2. 训练干预无法完全消除不公平，往往会导致排序与校准目标的权衡；3. 后处理手段受限于训练产生的偏差，难以起到预期修正作用；4. 现有的置信度弃权策略本质上是不公平的，对不同内容类型的干预效果存在偏差。

Conclusion: 开发公平的毒性分类系统需要采用一个多轴（ranking, calibration, abstention）综合评估框架，因为单一的训练或后处理干预无法同时解决所有公平性维度，且不同方法会产生截然不同的失效模式。

Abstract: Fairness in toxicity classification involves three integrated axes: ranking, calibration, and abstention. Training-time interventions and post-hoc safety mechanisms cannot be evaluated independently because the former determines the efficacy of the latter. We compare Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM), instance-level reweighting, and Group DRO across these axes, combined with temperature scaling, confidence-based abstention, and per-identity threshold optimization. Evaluation uses subgroup AUC, BPSN/BNSP AUC, error gaps, and per-subgroup Expected Calibration Error (ECE) with bootstrap CIs ($n = 1000$).
  We report four findings. (1) Calibration disparity is a hidden fairness violation. ERM has near-perfect aggregate calibration ($0.013$) but is significantly miscalibrated across all identity subgroups ($+0.029$ to $+0.134$). (2) Training interventions reshape rather than eliminate disparity. Reweighted ERM improves ranking (BPSN AUC $+0.06$ to $+0.12$) but worsens the calibration-fairness gap by up to $+0.232$. Group DRO eliminates calibration disparity but only by becoming uniformly miscalibrated globally (ECE $0.118$). (3) Post-hoc methods inherit training failure modes. Temperature scaling fails because miscalibration is non-uniform. Confidence-based abstention works under ERM but breaks under DRO, where the risk-coverage curve rises with deferral. (4) Abstention itself is unfair. Confidence-based deferral helps background content far more than identity-mentioning content. We argue that SRAI fairness requires a multi-axis framework: methods that differ only in aggregate ranking can differ sharply in failure modes that determine real-world harm.

</details>


### [169] [Fast Rates for Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14599)
*Andreas Schlaginhaufen,Maryam Kamgarpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为熵正则化Min-Max-IRL在Borel空间MDP中建立了强有力的统计保证，证明了其参数估计可达到O(1/n)的快速收敛率，并确立了其与MLE的结构联系，且无需执行探索性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决熵正则化Min-Max逆强化学习（IRL）在复杂MDP环境下的统计效率与理论保证问题，特别是在需要处理Borel空间和解决模型误设定情况下的理论缺失。

Method: 利用最大似然估计（MLE）与Min-Max-IRL在总体水平和确定性动力学下的等价性证明；利用Min-Max-IRL损失函数的伪自共轭性（pseudo-self-concordance）进行收敛性分析；通过求导分析软最优值函数（soft-optimal value function）。

Result: 1. 在总体水平证明了MLE与Min-Max-IRL的等价性；2. 在经验水平（确定性动力学下）证明了二者等价；3. 证明了轨迹级KL散度和参数估计误差具有O(1/n)的快速收敛速率；4. 扩展了奖励可辨识性理论至通用Borel空间。

Conclusion: 证明了熵正则化Min-Max-IRL在有限时段Borel空间MDP下的结构等价性与统计收敛性，确立了其在参数估计上的高效率，并将其适用范围扩展至模型误设定的情况。

Abstract: We establish novel structural and statistical results for entropy-regularized min-max inverse reinforcement learning (Min-Max-IRL) with linear reward classes in finite-horizon MDPs with Borel state and action spaces. On the structural side, we show that maximum likelihood estimation (MLE) and Min-Max-IRL are equivalent at the population level, and at the empirical level under deterministic dynamics. On the statistical side, exploiting pseudo-self-concordance of the Min-Max-IRL loss, we prove that both the trajectory-level KL divergence and the squared parameter error in the Hessian norm decay at the fast rate $\mathcal{O}(n^{-1})$, where $n$ is the number of expert trajectories. Our guarantees apply under misspecification and require no exploration assumptions. We further extend reward-identifiability results to general Borel spaces and derive novel results on the derivatives of the soft-optimal value function with respect to reward parameters.

</details>


### [170] [In-Context Learning for Data-Driven Censored Inventory Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14840)
*Sohom Mukherjee,Anh-Duy Pham,Richard Pibernik,Yunbei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对决策相关的审查库存问题，提出了一种基于离线元训练与在线上下文生成的新框架 (ICGPS)，解决了传统 TS 在先验错误下的脆弱性，实现了高性能的在线库存控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的参数化 Thompson 采样 (TS) 在先验失配下表现脆弱，而离线填补方法难以直接迁移到在线学习，需寻求一种稳健的决策方法。

Method: 提出了一种上下文内生成后验采样 (ICGPS) 方法；利用预训练的生成模型 (ChronosFlow) 在线生成潜在需求的补全，并基于这些补全采取决策，将审查反馈问题转化为强盗凸优化问题。

Result: 在理论上证明了 ICGPS 的贝叶斯遗憾上界；在实验中，该方法不仅匹配了理想情况下的 TS 性能，且在先验失配、分布偏移及重度审查的真实数据集（SuperStore）下显著优于现有基准模型。

Conclusion: ICGPS 框架通过离线训练的生成模型在在线任务中实现了稳健的库存控制，弥合了离线预测质量与在线决策性能之间的鸿沟，在重度审查场景下表现优异。

Abstract: We study inventory control with decision-dependent censoring, focusing on the censored or repeated newsvendor (R-NV), where each order quantity determines whether demand is fully observed or censored by sales. Existing approaches based on parametric Thompson sampling (TS) can be brittle under prior mismatch, while offline imputation methods need not transfer to online learning. Motivated by the predictive view of decision making, we combine these ideas by taking oracle actions on learned completions of latent demand. We propose in-context generative posterior sampling (ICGPS), which uses modern generative models that are meta-trained offline and deployed online by in-context autoregressive generation. Theoretically, we show that the Bayesian regret of ICGPS with a learned completion kernel is bounded by the Bayesian regret of a TS benchmark with the ideal completion kernel plus a deployment penalty scaling as $\sqrt{T}$ times the square root of the completion mismatch. This yields a plug-in template for operational problems with known TS regret bounds. For R-NV, we derive sublinear Bayesian regret by reducing censored feedback to bandit convex optimization feedback. We also show that, under reasonable coverage and stability assumptions, the online completion mismatch is controlled by the offline censored predictive mismatch, so offline predictive quality transfers to online performance. Practically, we instantiate ICGPS with ChronosFlow, which combines a frozen time-series transformer backbone with a trainable conditional normalizing-flow head for fast censoring-consistent sampling. In benchmark experiments, ChronosFlow-ICGPS matches correctly specified TS, outperforms myopic and UCB-style baselines, and is robust to prior mismatch and distribution shift. ChronosFlow-ICGPS also performs well for the real-world SuperStore dataset, especially under heavy censoring.

</details>


### [171] [Mini-JEPA Foundation Model Fleet Enables Agentic Hydrologic Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14120)
*Mashrekur Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 Mini-JEPAs，这是一组轻量级的、针对特定传感器的领域基础模型，通过路由智能体协同工作。相比单一的大型通用模型，该方法能以更小的计算代价在水文学等专业任务中实现更优的精准度和解释力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型通用地理空间基础模型（如 AlphaEarth）虽然具备宏观特征表征能力，但在处理特定精细领域（如水文）信号时表现欠佳，且存在计算开销巨大、模型不透明及难以部署等问题。

Method: 提出了 Mini-JEPAs 架构：使用 5 个各 22M 参数的专用 JEPA 模型，分别处理不同模态（光学、SAR、热红外、物候、地形/土壤）的数据；引入了一个路由智能体（Router LLM）来匹配用户问题与最合适的传感器特征；在轻量化模型的基础上，结合 LLM 实现多模型联合查询。

Result: Mini-JEPAs 在单任务重构上表现出色（$R^2$ 高达 0.97）；相比单纯使用 AlphaEarth，结合 Mini-JEPAs 的多模态路由方案在水文指标预测上提升了 $ΔR^2$ 达 0.031，并在物理相关问题评估中表现出显著的性能提升（Cohen's d=1.10）。

Conclusion: Mini-JEPAs 提供了一种轻量级、模块化且高效的替代通用巨型地理空间模型方案，能够通过路由机制在特定领域（尤其是水文学）实现性能优化，且极大降低了计算资源需求。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models compress multispectral observations into dense embeddings increasingly used in natural-language environmental reasoning systems. A single planetary-scale model, e.g. Google AlphaEarth, handles broad characterization well but may compromise on specialized hydrologic signals. Such generalist models are also often inaccessible, expensive, and require large-scale compute. We propose Mini-JEPAs: a fleet of small sensor-specialized Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) foundation models consulted by a routing agent for specialized questions. We pretrained five 22M-parameter Mini-JEPAs sharing an identical Vision Transformer backbone, JEPA recipe, and 64-d output space, using Sentinel-2 optical, Sentinel-1 SAR, MODIS thermal, multi-temporal Sentinel-2 phenology, and a topography-soil stack. Each Mini-JEPA reconstructs the variable matched to its sensor, with cross-validated $R^2$ reaching 0.97 for elevation, 0.97 for temperature, and 0.81 for precipitation. The five manifolds differ in geometric structure, with global participation ratios from 8.9 to 20.2 and local intrinsic dimensionalities from 2.3 to 9.0. Joint topography-soil and phenology models add predictive value beyond AlphaEarth alone for soil moisture, aridity, and precipitation ($ΔR^2$ up to 0.031). A router LLM reads per-modality references and selects appropriate sensors with a perfect hit rate over a curated question set. In paired LLM-as-Judge evaluation, dual retrieval over AlphaEarth and the routed fleet outperforms AlphaEarth alone on physics-matched questions (Cohen's $d = 1.10$, $p = 0.031$). Locally-trained Mini-JEPAs can be operationalized for hydrologic intelligence with modest compute.

</details>


### [172] [InfoSFT: Learn More and Forget Less with Information-Aware Token Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14967)
*Mahdi Sabbaghi,George Pappas,Adel Javanmard,Hamed Hassani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: InfoSFT 是一种简单、低成本的 SFT 加权策略，通过聚焦中等置信度的 token 来平衡新行为学习与原有能力保持，有效避免了过拟合和灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 SFT 会对所有训练样本进行等权重拟合，导致模型容易对低概率样本过拟合，引发策略偏移（policy shifts），从而损害模型的原有通用能力。现有方法（如过滤或降权）可能会抑制模型需要学习的新行为。

Method: 提出 InfoSFT 方法，对标准的 token-wise 损失函数进行加权。该方法聚焦于“中等置信度”的 token（即模型并非完全确定，但也不至于造成不稳定性的样本），通过简单的加权机制过滤掉模型已掌握的简单样本和可能导致过拟合的极端样本。

Result: 在数学、代码和思维链（CoT）等任务的广泛实验证明，InfoSFT 相比标准 SFT 和概率加权基线，在提升泛化能力和保留原有领域知识方面均表现更佳。

Conclusion: InfoSFT 提供了一种简单且有效的 SFT 加权方案，通过关注“中等置信度”的 token 来优化模型表现，在提升新任务性能的同时有效缓解了灾难性遗忘。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) provides the standard approach for teaching LLMs new behaviors from offline expert demonstrations. However, standard SFT uniformly fits all samples -- including those with low likelihood under the base model -- which can disproportionately drive training updates toward overfitting specific samples rather than learning the target behavior. Moreover, adapting to these unlikely samples induces substantial policy shifts that degrade prior capabilities. Existing methods mitigate this by filtering, regenerating, or down-weighting low-likelihood data. In doing so, they often suppress precisely the novel behaviors the base model has yet to learn.
  We propose InfoSFT, a principled weighting scheme for the SFT objective that concentrates learning signals on maximally informative, medium-confidence tokens -- those neither overly familiar to the base model nor too unlikely to cause instability. Requiring only a one-line modification to the standard token-wise loss, InfoSFT demonstrably improves generalization over vanilla SFT and likelihood-weighted baselines across math, code, and chain-of-thought tasks with diverse model families, while better preserving pre-existing capabilities.

</details>


### [173] [bde: A Python Package for Bayesian Deep Ensembles via MILE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14146)
*Vyron Arvanitis,Angelos Aslanidis,Emanuel Sommer,David Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: bde 是一个基于 JAX 和 MILE 算法的 Python 包，专为表格数据的贝叶斯深度集成设计，提供快速训练和不确定性量化功能，且兼容 scikit-learn。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了简化表格数据中贝叶斯深度集成的实现过程，解决传统方法在训练效率和不确定性量化方面的挑战。

Method: 基于 JAX 实现的微正则朗之万集成（MILE）采样推理方法，并提供与 scikit-learn 兼容的 API 接口。

Result: 提供了一个支持回归和分类任务的工具包，能够实现高效的 MCMC 采样与不确定性量化。

Conclusion: bde 是一个高效、用户友好且兼容 scikit-learn 的 Python 包，为处理表格数据进行贝叶斯深度集成提供了一种实用的解决方案，能够实现快速训练、采样和不确定性估计。

Abstract: bde is a user-friendly Python package for Bayesian Deep Ensembles with a particular focus on tabular data. Built on an efficient JAX implementation of the sampling-based inference method Microcanonical Langevin Ensembles (MILE), it provides scikit-learn compatible estimators for fast training, efficient Markov Chain Monte Carlo sampling, and uncertainty quantification in both regression and classification tasks.

</details>


### [174] [Uncovering Trajectory and Topological Signatures in Multimodal Pediatric Sleep Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14156)
*Scott Ye,Harlin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探索了儿科睡眠分析中深度学习嵌入背后的潜在结构，证明了结合几何拓扑特征与临床数据可显著增强睡眠事件检测的诊断性能与模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前生成模型在儿科睡眠分析中的应用尚处于起步阶段，且其多模态嵌入的潜在结构特征（如几何与拓扑信息）在诊断中的作用机制尚不明确。

Method: 使用多模态掩码自编码器（Multimodal MAE）提取睡眠分期嵌入；引入PHATE降维坐标、整夜运动描述符、持久同调特征及EHR信息；采用线性模型和MLP进行特征融合与分类。

Result: 在处理严重的样本不平衡问题时，融合几何、拓扑及临床特征的模型在四个二分类任务（血氧饱和度下降、EEG觉醒、低通气、呼吸暂停）中表现稳健，AUPRC显著提升，且完整融合模型在Brier score和校准误差（ECE）方面表现最优。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，在儿科睡眠分析中，利用潜在空间的几何与拓扑属性（PHATE坐标、持久同调）结合电子健康记录（EHR）可以提供除基础嵌入之外的补充性诊断信号，显著提升模型性能并改善校准效果。

Abstract: While generative models have shown promise in pediatric sleep analysis, the latent structure of their multimodal embeddings remains poorly understood. This work investigates session-wide diagnostic information contained in the sequences of 30-second pediatric PSG epochs embedded by a multimodal masked autoencoder. We test whether augmenting embeddings with PHATE-derived per-epoch coordinates and whole-night movement descriptors, persistent homology summaries of the embedding cloud, and EHR yields task-relevant signals. Simple linear and MLP models, chosen for interpretability rather than state-of-the-art performance, show that geometric, topological, and clinical features each provide complementary gains. For binary predictions, feature importance is task-dependent, and more expressive late-fusion models generally perform better, with AUPRC improving from 0.26 to 0.34 for desaturation, 0.31 to 0.48 for EEG arousal, 0.09 to 0.22 for hypopnea, and 0.05 to 0.14 for apnea. We also report Brier score and Expected Calibration Error, where the full fusion model yields the best calibration across all four binary tasks. Our study reveals that latent geometry/topology and EHR offer complementary, interpretable signals beyond embeddings, improving calibration and robustness under extreme imbalance.

</details>


### [175] [Slot-MPC: Goal-Conditioned Model Predictive Control with Object-Centric Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14937)
*Jonathan Spieler,Angel Villar-Corrales,Sven Behnke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Slot-MPC通过学习基于槽的对象表示并结合可微分的动态模型，实现了端到端的基于梯度的MPC，显著提升了机器人在复杂任务中的规划效率与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大多数对象中心世界模型和强化学习方法依赖于推理时固定的反应式策略，难以推广到新颖场景。为了解决这一问题，需要一种能通过MPC进行规划、具备适应未见场景能力的模型框架。

Method: 提出了Slot-MPC框架：1. 使用视觉编码器学习基于槽（slot-based）的各个对象表示；2. 学习动作条件下的对象中心动态模型；3. 在推理阶段利用模型的可微分性，通过基于梯度的模型预测控制（MPC）直接优化行动，而非传统的无梯度采样法。

Result: 在模拟机器人操作任务中，Slot-MPC在任务性能和规划效率上均超过了非对象中心的世界模型基准；在具备有限状态-动作覆盖的离线设置下，基于梯度的MPC展现出了比梯度无关采样法更好的性能。

Conclusion: Slot-MPC证明了显式的、以对象为中心的表示形式为可控且具有通用性的决策提供了强大的归纳偏置，并且在该领域中，基于梯度的MPC在处理受限的离线数据时优于基于样本的MPC。

Abstract: Predictive world models enable agents to model scene dynamics and reason about the consequences of their actions. Inspired by human perception, object-centric world models capture scene dynamics using object-level representations, which can be used for downstream applications such as action planning. However, most object-centric world models and reinforcement learning (RL) approaches learn reactive policies that are fixed at inference time, limiting generalization to novel situations. We propose Slot-MPC, an object-centric world modeling framework that enables planning through Model Predictive Control (MPC). Slot-MPC leverages vision encoders to learn slot-based representations, which encode individual objects in the scene, and uses these structured representations to learn an action-conditioned object-centric dynamics model. At inference time, the learned dynamics model enables action planning via MPC, allowing agents to adapt to previously unseen situations. Since the learned world model is differentiable, we can use gradient-based MPC to directly optimize actions, which is computationally more efficient than relying on gradient-free, sampling-based MPC methods. Experiments on simulated robotic manipulation tasks show that Slot-MPC improves both task performance and planning efficiency compared to non-object-centric world model baselines. In the considered offline setting with limited state-action coverage, we find that gradient-based MPC performs better than gradient-free, sampling-based MPC. Our results demonstrate that explicitly structured, object-centric representations provide a strong inductive bias for controllable and generalizable decision-making. Code and additional results are available at https://slot-mpc.github.io.

</details>


### [176] [LLMs Know When They Know, but Do Not Act on It: A Metacognitive Harness for Test-time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14186)
*Qi Cao,Yufan Wang,Peijia Qin,Shuhao Zhang,Pengtao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出一种元认知控制框架，通过将 LLM 的自我监控信号转化为显式推理控制指令（FOK 和 JOL），在不调整模型参数的前提下，显著增强了模型在文本、代码及多模态推理任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型能够评估自身解决问题的可能性及答案的正确性（自我监控信号），但这些信号通常被孤立处理，未能有效转化为指导推理过程的控制机制。

Method: 受认知心理学中 Nelson-Narens 理论启发，提出了“元认知控制框架（Metacognitive Harness）”。该框架在推理过程中引入：推理前评估（Feeling-of-Knowing, FOK）和推理后判断（Judgment-of-Learning, JOL），将这些信号转化为控制指令，通过“信任当前解”、“带反馈重试”或“多尝试聚合”等策略引导推理流程。

Result: 在无需参数更新或针对性微调的情况下，使 Claude Sonnet-4.6 的基准测试汇总准确率从 48.3% 提升至 56.9%，并在 HLE-Verified、LiveCodeBench v6 和 R-Bench-V 等多个核心基准测试中超过了现有最佳排行榜记录。

Conclusion: 大语言模型本身具备潜在的元认知能力（如对任务难度和自身表现的评估），通过将其显式化并整合进推理控制流程，无需参数微调即可显著提升推理性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often expose useful signals of self-monitoring: before solving a problem, they can estimate whether they are likely to succeed, and after solving it, they can judge whether their answer is likely to be correct. However, these signals are typically measured or elicited in isolation, rather than used to control inference. In this work, we ask whether LLMs possess latent metacognitive ability that can be turned into effective test-time control. Inspired by the Nelson--Narens theory from cognitive psychology, we propose a metacognitive harness that separates monitoring from reasoning. For each problem, the model first reports a pre-solve feeling-of-knowing (FOK) signal; after each solve attempt, it reports a post-solve judgment-of-learning (JOL) signal. Rather than treating these signals as passive confidence estimates, the harness turns them into an explicit control interface for reasoning: it decides when to trust the current solution, when to retry with compact metacognitive feedback, and when to pass multiple attempts to a final aggregator. Across text, code, and multimodal reasoning benchmarks, our harness substantially improves a fixed Claude Sonnet-4.6 base model without parameter updates or benchmark-specific fine-tuning. On the evaluated public benchmark snapshots, it raises pooled accuracy from 48.3 to 56.9 and exceeds the strongest listed leaderboard entries on the three primary evaluation settings: HLE-Verified, LiveCodeBench v6, and R-Bench-V. These results suggest that strong LLMs may already possess useful metacognitive ability, but require an explicit control harness to act on it during reasoning.

</details>


### [177] [Towards Fine-Grained and Verifiable Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14210)
*Yingying Fang,Haijie Xu,Shuang Wu,Mariathasan Anish,Guang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种细粒度 CBM 框架，通过强制将预测的概念关联至局部的视觉证据，解决了传统 CBM 无法验证概念对应性的缺陷，在保持性能的同时大幅提升了模型的可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的概念瓶颈模型（CBM）虽然具备初步的可解释性，但无法验证所预测的概念是否真的基于正确的视觉特征，容易产生虚假相关性（spurious correlations），难以满足高可靠性场景（如医疗）的信任需求。

Method: 提出了一种细粒度（fine-grained）CBM 框架，在预测最终结果前，通过机制将每个概念直接定位（grounding）在局部的视觉证据上，强制模型学习概念与具体图像区域的对应关系。

Result: 在医疗影像基准测试中，该模型在保持与标准 CBM 相当的预测性能的同时，实现了信息完整性，并显著增强了模型决策过程的透明度与可验证性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过引入细粒度的视觉定位机制，成功解决了概念瓶颈模型（CBM）中“预测概念与视觉证据脱节”的可靠性问题，显著提升了模型的可解释性与可验证性，为医疗等高风险领域提供了更具实战价值的信任机制。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) offer interpretable alternatives to black-box predictors by introducing human-relatable concepts before the final output. However, existing CBMs struggle to verify whether predicted concepts correspond to the correct visual evidence, limiting their reliability. We propose a fine-grained CBM framework that grounds each concept in localized visual evidence, enabling direct inspection of where and how concepts are encoded. This design allows users to interpret predictions and verify that the model learns intended concepts rather than spurious correlations. Experiments on medical imaging benchmarks show that our learned concept space is information-complete and achieves predictive performance comparable to standard CBMs, while substantially improving transparency. Unlike post-hoc attribution methods, our framework validates both the presence and correctness of concept representations, bridging interpretability with verifiability. Our approach enhances the trustworthiness of CBMs and establishes a principled mechanism for human-model interaction at the concept level, paving the way toward more reliable and clinically actionable concept-based learning systems.

</details>


### [178] [PreFT: Prefill-only finetuning for efficient inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14217)
*Andrew Lanpouthakoun,Aryaman Arora,Zhengxuan Wu,Dhruv Pai,Ben Keigwin,Dan Jurafsky,Christopher Potts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PreFT方法，通过仅在预填充阶段（而非解码阶段）应用个性化适配器，显著提升了多用户场景下LLM的推理吞吐量，同时保持了接近传统PEFT的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的参数高效微调（PEFT）方法在多用户服务场景下，因解码阶段处理多个适配器导致吞吐量大幅下降，且现有优化侧重于参数规模而非服务吞吐量。

Method: 提出PreFT（Prefill-only Finetuning）方法；仅将适配器应用于预填充阶段，解码阶段则弃用；在vLLM推理引擎上实现了预填充专用版的LoRA和ReFT。

Result: 在Llama 3.1 70B上，并发服务512个适配器时，PreFT的吞吐量是传统PEFT的1.9倍；尽管在监督微调（SFT）任务上评估损失略高，但可通过增加秩（Rank）补偿且几乎不影响吞吐量；在强化学习（RL）任务上，PreFT性能与标准PEFT基本持平。

Conclusion: 证实了仅在预填充（prefill）阶段应用适配器（PreFT）是一种在大规模个性化服务中，比传统全阶段适配器更优的精度-吞吐量权衡方案。

Abstract: Large language models can now be personalised efficiently at scale using parameter efficient finetuning methods (PEFTs), but serving user-specific PEFTs harms throughput, even with specialised kernels and memory management techniques. This is because, theoretically and empirically, a mismatch exists between prefill (processing a large number of tokens at once) and decode (generating a single token autoregressively): the latter has far lower throughput when serving multiple adapters. Rather than optimising performance relative to parameter count, for efficient multi-adapter serving, we instead ought to optimise performance relative to serving throughput. We therefore propose PreFT (Prefill-only Finetuning), wherein we only apply the adapter to prefill tokens and discard it afterwards. PreFT significantly increases throughput with minimal effect on performance. We develop and release an efficient implementation of two prefill-only PEFTs, LoRA and ReFT, on the vLLM inference engine. We first show that serving multi-user PreFTs is more efficient than traditional PEFTs ($1.9\times$ the throughput when serving $512$ adapters on Llama 3.1 70B). Then, we compare the performance of prefill-only vs. all-token adapters on a variety of supervised finetuning and reinforcement learning tasks with LMs at varying scales. On SFT, we observe that the evaluation loss of PreFTs is higher than PEFTs, but can be compensated by increasing rank with nearly no reduction in throughput. On RL, we consistently find that PreFTs approach parity with standard PEFTs. Together, this work validates prefill-only adaptation of LLMs as a more favourable accuracy-throughput tradeoff than existing PEFTs for personalised serving.

</details>


### [179] [Diagnosing Training Inference Mismatch in LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14220)
*Tianle Zhong,Neiwen Ling,Yifan Pi,Zijun Wei,Tianshu Yu,Geoffrey Fox,Peng Wu,Xiao Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了LLM强化学习中“训练-推理不匹配”（TIM）问题，指出其是导致训练崩溃的系统性原因而非随机噪声，并提出了相应的诊断方法与缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM强化学习系统中，生成阶段（Rollout）与优化阶段（Policy Optimization）在处理相同模型权重和序列时产生的token概率存在不一致，这种“训练-推理不匹配”（TIM）会导致训练不稳定且难以排查。

Method: 通过提出的VeXact诊断设置（零失配环境）来隔离TIM，分析其对优化过程的影响，并识别出缓解TIM的系统性补救措施。

Result: 研究发现，仅微小的token级数值差异即足以引发训练崩溃；TIM会改变实际的优化目标，从而对模型训练产生实质性负面影响。

Conclusion: 训练-推理不匹配（TIM）并非无害的数值噪声，而是导致LLM强化学习训练崩溃的一阶系统性扰动，必须在分析模型稳定性时予以重视。

Abstract: Modern LLM RL systems separate rollout generation from policy optimization. These two stages are expected to produce token probabilities that match exactly. However, implementation differences can make them assign different values to the same sequence under the same model weights, inducing Training-Inference Mismatch (TIM). TIM is difficult to inspect because it is entangled with off-policy drift and common stabilization mechanisms. In this work, we isolate TIM in a zero-mismatch diagnostic setting (VeXact), and show that small token-level numerical disagreements can independently cause training collapse. We further show that TIM changes the effective optimization problem, and identify a set of remedies that could mitigate TIM. Our results suggest that TIM is not benign numerical noise, but a systems-level perturbation that should be treated as a first-order factor in analyzing LLM RL stability.

</details>


### [180] [DT-Transformer: A Foundation Model for Disease Trajectory Prediction on a Real-world Health System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14227)
*Yunying Zhu,Andrew R Weckstein,Kueiyu Joshua Lin,Jie Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过利用大规模跨机构电子健康记录（EHR）训练了“DT-Transformer”模型，显著提升了真实临床环境中疾病轨迹预测的准确性，证明了利用大规模多中心医疗数据构建基础模型对临床预测的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有疾病轨迹预测模型多基于单一医院数据集，难以反映临床真实部署环境的复杂性，旨在利用大规模、多中心健康系统数据提升模型的泛化能力与预测准确性。

Method: 开发了DT-Transformer基础模型。该模型基于Mass General Brigham (MGB) 医疗系统中涵盖11家医院和众多门诊的170万患者的5710万条结构化电子健康记录（EHR）进行训练。

Result: DT-Transformer在留出集及前瞻性验证中表现出色，在896个疾病类别的下一次事件预测任务中，中位年龄与性别分层AUC达到0.871，所有类别的AUC均高于0.5。

Conclusion: 基于多中心、大规模健康系统数据训练的DT-Transformer验证了其作为临床预测基础模型的潜力，证明了在真实世界临床复杂性下实现高精度预测的可行性。

Abstract: Accurate disease trajectory prediction is critical for early intervention, resource allocation, and improving long-term outcomes. While electronic health records (EHRs) provide a rich longitudinal view of patient health in clinical environments, models trained on curated research cohorts may not reflect routine deployment settings, and those trained on single-hospital datasets capture only fragments of each patient's trajectory. This highlights the importance of leveraging large, multi-hospital health systems for training and validation to better reflect real-world clinical complexity. In this work, we develop DT-Transformer, a foundation model trained on 57.1M structured EHR entries over 1.7M patients from Mass General Brigham (MGB), spanning 11 hospitals and a broad network of outpatient clinics. DT-Transformer achieves strong discrimination in both held-out and prospective validation settings. Next-event prediction achieves a median age- and sex-stratified AUC of 0.871 across 896 disease categories, with all categories exceeding AUC 0.5. These results support health system-scale training as a path toward foundation models suited to real-world clinical forecasting.

</details>


### [181] [AudioMosaic: Contrastive Masked Audio Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14231)
*Hanxun Huang,Qizhou Wang,Xingjun Ma,Cihang Xie,Christopher Leckie,Sarah Erfani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AudioMosaic 通过创新的时频掩码增强策略，解决了对比学习在音频领域的计算需求难题，显著提升了音频表征的质量与通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频自监督学习领域虽然由生成式重建目标主导，但对比学习方法因数据增强策略复杂及对大 Batch Size 的高要求而受限，亟需一种更高效的对比学习途径来提升音频表示的判别能力。

Method: 提出 AudioMosaic 模型，利用结构化的时频掩码（structured time-frequency masking）在声谱图块上构建正样本对，从而降低显存消耗并支持大规模批次训练。

Result: 在多个标准音频基准测试中，线性探测（linear probing）和微调（fine-tuning）效果均达到行业最优（SOTA）；此外，该编码器在音频-语言模型中表现出更强的迁移能力。

Conclusion: AudioMosaic 是一个高效的对比学习框架，通过结构化的时频掩码技术实现了优异的音频表征学习，且在音频理解及音频-语言任务中表现出色，优于现有的生成式方法。

Abstract: Audio self-supervised learning (SSL) aims to learn general-purpose representations from large-scale unlabeled audio data. While recent advances have been driven mainly by generative reconstruction objectives, contrastive approaches remain less explored, partly due to the difficulty of designing effective audio augmentations and the large batch sizes required for contrastive pre-training. We introduce \textbf{AudioMosaic}, a contrastive learning-based audio encoder for general audio understanding. During pre-training, AudioMosaic constructs positive pairs by applying structured time-frequency masking to spectrogram patches, which reduces memory usage and enables efficient large-batch training. Compared with generative approaches, the AudioMosaic encoder learns more discriminative utterance-level representations that demonstrate strong transferability across datasets, domains, and acoustic conditions. Extensive experiments show that AudioMosaic achieves state-of-the-art performance on several standard audio benchmarks under both linear probing and fine-tuning. We further show that integrating the pretrained AudioMosaic encoder into audio-language models improves performance on audio-language tasks. The code is publicly available in our \href{https://github.com/HanxunH/AudioMosaic}{GitHub repository}.

</details>


### [182] [Quantum Advantage in Multi Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14235)
*Simranjeet Singh Dahia,Claudia Szabo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过CHSH博弈和协同导航任务，证实了量子纠缠是提升QMARL智能体协同能力的核心，并证明了量子-经典混合架构的性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前QMARL研究普遍缺乏严谨的对比基准，导致难以区分所谓的“量子优势”是源于量子特性还是算法巧合，需要通过实验验证纠缠在智能体协同中的实际作用。

Method: 提出了一个基于变分量子电路（VQC）且共享纠缠态的去中心化QMARL框架；通过CHSH博弈（具备严格数学性能上限的基准）验证量子优势；并在CoopNav游戏中对比了经典MAA2C与不同配置的QMARL的表现。

Result: 在CHSH博弈中，纠缠QMARL智能体达到了0.854的Tsirelson极限，超越了0.75的经典上限；去纠缠量子电路仅能达到经典水平；在CoopNav任务中，QMARL的成功率约为经典MAA2C的两倍。

Conclusion: 研究确认了量子纠缠是实现QMARL协同优势的关键机制，且量子-经典混合架构（量子Actor+经典中心化Critic）在复杂任务中表现最优。纠缠结构的优选对性能至关重要。

Abstract: We present an empirical evaluation of quantum entanglement in agent coordination within quantum multi agent reinforcement learning (QMARL). While QMARL has attracted growing interest recently, most prior work evaluates quantum policies without provable baselines, making it impossible to rigorously distinguish quantum advantage from algorithmic coincidence. We address this directly by evaluating a decentralized QMARL framework with variational quantum circuit (VQC) actors with shared entangled states. In the CHSH game, which has a mathematically proven classical performance ceiling of 0.75 win rate, we show that entangled QMARL agents approach the Tsirelson limit of 0.854, providing clear evidence of their quantum advantage. We show that unentangled quantum circuits match the classical baseline, confirming that entanglement and not the quantum circuit itself is the active coordination mechanism. We also explore the effect of specific entanglement structures, as some Bell states enable coordination gains while others actively harm performance. On cooperative navigation (CoopNav), QMARL without entanglement achieves $\sim2\times$ improvement in success rate over classical MAA2C ($\sim$0.85 versus $\sim$0.40), with the hybrid configuration, quantum actor paired with a classical centralised critic, outperforming both fully classical and fully quantum solutions. We present our experimental analysis and discuss future work.

</details>


### [183] [Active Learners as Efficient PRP Rerankers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14236)
*Jeremías Figueiredo Paschmann,Juan Kaplan,Francisco Nattero Santiago Mauricio Barron Bucolo,Juan Wisznia,Luciano del Corro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大模型成对排序中判断噪声与调用成本的矛盾，本文提出一种基于有噪成对比较主动学习的重排序框架，通过消除位置偏差并优化采样策略，以更少的调用次数获得更精准的排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的PRP方法将LLM的成对判断视为确定性数据，并依赖经典排序算法，但LLM的判断往往存在噪声、顺序敏感且不满足传递性，导致在预算约束下的top-K排名不准确（截断误差）。

Method: 1. 将PRP重排序任务定义为“从有噪成对比较中进行主动学习”；2. 使用主动学习排序器代替传统排序算法（如冒泡排序、快速排序）；3. 引入“随机方向预言机（randomized-direction oracle）”，通过单次LLM调用将系统性位置偏差转化为零均值噪声。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在调用约束（call-constrained）环境下，相较于传统方法能获得更高的NDCG@10性能。

Conclusion: 将成对排名提示（PRP）重构为主动学习问题，并结合随机化方向机制，能有效解决传统排序算法在处理大模型（LLM）有噪偏好判断时的局限性，在有限的调用预算下显著提升排名性能。

Abstract: Pairwise Ranking Prompting (PRP) elicits pairwise preference judgments from an LLM, which are then aggregated into a ranking, usually via classical sorting algorithms. However, judgments are noisy, order-sensitive, and sometimes intransitive, so sorting assumptions do not match the setting. Because sorting aims to recover a full permutation, truncating it to meet a call budget does not produce a dependable top-K. We thus reframe PRP reranking as active learning from noisy pairwise comparisons and show that active rankers are drop-in replacements that improve NDCG@10 per call in the call-constrained regime. Our noise-robust framework also introduces a randomized-direction oracle that uses a single LLM call per pair. This approach converts systematic position bias into zero-mean noise, enabling unbiased aggregate ranking without the cost of bidirectional calls.

</details>


### [184] [Paraphrasing Attack Resilience of Various AI-Generated Text Detection Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14240)
*Andrii Shportko,Inessa Verbitsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多种AI生成文本检测方法在改写攻击下的抗干扰能力，揭示了高性能检测器往往在面对攻击时更为脆弱的“性能与韧性”权衡困境，挑战了现有检测技术的可靠性认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）的广泛应用带来了剽窃和虚假信息传播等问题，加上AI检测规避工具的兴起，提升机器生成文本检测的可靠性已成为迫切需求。

Method: 本文通过评估三种主流机器生成文本检测方法（微调RoBERTa、Binoculars以及基于文本特征的分析），并利用随机森林分类器对这些方法进行集成，测试了它们在面对改写攻击时的韧性。

Result: 研究发现，包含Binoculars的集成模型拥有最强的原始检测性能，但同时在面对改写攻击时性能下降最为显著。

Conclusion: AI文本检测领域存在“性能与韧性（Performance vs. Resilience）”的二分法难题，即当前最先进的检测技术虽然表现优异，但在面对改写攻击时往往表现脆弱，这使得这些技术的可靠性受到质疑。

Abstract: The recent large-scale emergence of LLMs has left an open space for dealing with their consequences, such as plagiarism or the spread of false information on the Internet. Coupling this with the rise of AI detector bypassing tools, reliable machine-generated text detection is in increasingly high demand. We investigate the paraphrasing attack resilience of various machine-generated text detection methods, evaluating three approaches: fine-tuned RoBERTa, Binoculars, and text feature analysis, along with their ensembles using Random Forest classifiers. We discovered that Binoculars-inclusive ensembles yield the strongest results, but they also suffer the most significant losses during attacks. In this paper, we present the dichotomy of performance versus resilience in the world of AI text detection, which complicates the current perception of reliability among state-of-the-art techniques.

</details>


### [185] [Artificial Intelligence-Assistant Cardiotocography: Unified Model for Signal Reconstruction, Fetal Heart Rate Analysis, and Variability Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14242)
*Xiaohua Wang,Kai Yu,XuXiao Liang,Liang Wang,Chao Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究开发了一种高鲁棒性的AI胎心监测模型，通过先进的信号还原与分类（IOL）技术，显著提升了胎心监护中减速与加速事件的识别精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统胎心率监测方法受设备性能、数据传输以及医生主观评估差异的限制，容易导致 fetal compromise（胎儿窘迫）等不良预后，亟需更客观、精准的自动化监测手段。

Method: 1. 数据预处理：利用55万余条无标签数据进行预训练，并结合7,266条专家标注数据进行微调；2. 模型架构：开发了专门用于胎心率（FHR）监测的FHrCTG模型，具备去噪和信号重构能力；3. 验证方法：提出“交集重叠标签”（IOL）方法，将连续频率分析转化为分类判别。

Result: 模型在检测减速（灵敏度89.13%，特异度87.78%）和加速（灵敏度62.5%，特异度92.04%）方面表现稳健；基于Fischer标准，在验证周期性（AUC 0.7214）和变异幅度（AUC 0.9643）方面均取得了高分。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的基于AI的FHrCTG模型在胎心率监测和异常识别方面展现了高性能，证明了其在改善传统胎心监护局限性、降低人为误判风险方面的临床应用潜力和有效性。

Abstract: The monitoring of fetal heart rate (FHR) and the assessment of its variability are crucial for preventing fetal compromise and adverse outcomes. However, traditional methods encounter limitations arising from equipment performance, data transmission, and subjective assessments by doctors. We have developed a tailored AI-based FHrCTG model specifically for FHR monitoring, which effectively mitigates noise interference and precisely reconstructs signals. Our model was pre-trained on a massive dataset consisting of 558,412 unlabeled data points and further refined using 7,266 expert-reviewed entries. To validate FHR, we introduced the Intersection Overlapping Labels (IOL) approach, which transforms rate analysis into categorical judgments. Testing revealed that our model demonstrates high sensitivity and specificity in detecting critical FHR decelerations (89.13% and 87.78%, respectively) and accelerations (62.5% and 92.04%, respectively).
