Across multiple model families and hazard benchmarks, MANSU is the first method to jointly satisfy all four properties with margin on each (meaningful forgetting, retain preservation, non-positive PTQ gap, and structural erasure), while gradient-based baselines recover up to +0.05 accuracy under compression.

</details>


### [224] [Self-Distilled Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15155)
*Zhengxi Lu,Zhiyuan Yao,Zhuowen Han,Zi-Han Wang,Jinyang Wu,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 智能体强化学习中奖励信号稀疏及多轮蒸馏不稳定的问题，SDAR 提出了一种带门控的自我蒸馏辅助优化机制，有效提升了复杂智能体任务的训练效率与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的强化学习（RL）在处理 LLM 智能体的长视界任务时，仅提供粗粒度的轨迹级奖励；而直接引入 OPSD 在多轮对话场景中容易引发不稳定性，且在处理技能条件下的特权上下文引导时缺乏差异化处理。

Method: 提出了 SDAR（Self-Distilled Agentic Reinforcement Learning），核心是将 OPSD（On-Policy Self-Distillation）作为带门控机制的辅助目标：通过 Sigmoid 门控映射将离散的 token 级反馈信号进行调节，对“教师”模型认定的正向偏差进行强化学习蒸馏，对不确定或负向的投射则进行软性衰减，确保 RL 依然作为主优化骨干。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5 和 Qwen3 系列模型上，通过 ALFWorld、WebShop 和 Search-QA 测试，SDAR 较 GRPO 基准有显著提升（ALFWorld +9.4%, Search-QA +7.0%, WebShop-Acc +10.2%），并有效克服了传统 GRPO+OPSD 的不稳定性。

Conclusion: SDAR 通过将 OPSD 视为带门控的辅助目标，成功解决了多轮智能体训练中自我蒸馏的不稳定性问题，显著提升了 LLM 智能体在长视界任务中的性能，成为一种比单一强化学习更优的优化策略。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a central paradigm for post-training LLM agents, yet its trajectory-level reward signal provides only coarse supervision for long-horizon interaction. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) complements RL by introducing dense token-level guidance from a teacher branch augmented with privileged context. However, transferring OPSD to multi-turn agents proves problematic: compounding multi-turn instability destabilizes supervision, while skill-conditioned privileged guidance requires asymmetric treatment for negative teacher rejections may arise from imperfect skills retrieval or utilization. We introduce SDAR (Self-Distilled Agentic Reinforcement Learning), which treats OPSD as a gated auxiliary objective while keeping RL as the primary optimization backbone. SDAR maps detached token-level signals into a sigmoid gate, strengthening distillation on teacher-endorsed positive-gap tokens and softly attenuating negative teacher rejections. Across the Qwen2.5 and Qwen3 families on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search-QA, SDAR substantially improves over GRPO (+9.4% on ALFWorld, +7.0% on Search-QA, +10.2% on WebShop-Acc), avoids the instability of naive GRPO+OPSD, and consistently outperforms hybrid RL--OPSD baselines across model scales.

</details>


### [225] [FutureSim: Replaying World Events to Evaluate Adaptive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15188)
*Shashwat Goel,Nikhil Chandak,Arvindh Arun,Ameya Prabhu,Steffen Staab,Moritz Hardt,Maksym Andriushchenko,Jonas Geiping*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FutureSim 是一款评估 AI 智能体在动态、现实世界中进行长期事件预测能力的基准测试，实验发现当前前沿模型在处理随时间发展的信息时表现欠佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 AI 评估方法难以衡量智能体在动态、开放式环境中对新信息的长期适应能力；需要一个能够模拟真实世界演进、评估长期预测和推理能力的基准。

Method: 构建了名为 FutureSim 的仿真环境，通过按时间顺序重播真实世界事件（新闻文章和事件结果），要求 AI 智能体在具备知识截止日期后的时间轴上，对未来事件进行预测和推理。

Result: 对前沿智能体的评估结果显示，模型性能差距明显，最佳准确率仅为 25%，且许多模型的预测质量（Brier score）甚至不如随机或基准预测。消融实验证明了该基准在研究长周期测试时适应、搜索、记忆和不确定性推理方面的有效性。

Conclusion: FutureSim 提供了一个现实的基准测试，能够有效衡量 AI 智能体在处理长期、动态、开放式现实世界任务时的适应能力，揭示了当前顶尖模型在此类任务中仍存在较大的性能差距。

Abstract: AI agents are being increasingly deployed in dynamic, open-ended environments that require adapting to new information as it arrives. To efficiently measure this capability for realistic use-cases, we propose building grounded simulations that replay real-world events in the order they occurred. We build FutureSim, where agents forecast world events beyond their knowledge cutoff while interacting with a chronological replay of the world: real news articles arriving and questions resolving over the simulated period. We evaluate frontier agents in their native harness, testing their ability to predict world events over a three-month period from January to March 2026. FutureSim reveals a clear separation in their capabilities, with the best agent's accuracy being 25%, and many having worse Brier skill score than making no prediction at all. Through careful ablations, we show how FutureSim offers a realistic setting to study emerging research directions like long-horizon test-time adaptation, search, memory, and reasoning about uncertainty. Overall, we hope our benchmark design paves the way to measure AI progress on open-ended adaptation spanning long time-horizons in the real world.

</details>


### [226] [Unbiased and Second-Order-Free Training for High-Dimensional PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14643)
*Jaemin Seo,Surin Lee,Jae Yong Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文解决了基于BSDE求解PDE时由EM离散化引起的训练偏差问题，提出了一种无需二阶导数且无偏差的训练框架，在保持高效性的同时提升了求解精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于BSDE的深度学习方法在求解高维PDE时，常用的Euler-Maruyama时间离散化方法会引入内在的训练损失偏差；而高阶离散方案（如Heun）虽能消除偏差，但会引入二阶导数计算，导致计算开销大幅增加。

Method: 对Euler-Maruyama（EM）诱导的损失偏差进行原则分析，并在无需二阶空间导数的前提下，构建了一个修正后的无偏差BSDE训练框架。

Result: 成功实现了消除EM诱导偏差且无需二阶导数计算的目标，维持了BSDE方法在高维PDE求解中避免维度灾难的计算效率优势。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种无偏差、无需二阶导数的BSDE求解框架，证明了在保持BSDE方法计算优势的同时，可以有效克服传统Euler-Maruyama离散化带来的损失函数偏差问题。

Abstract: Deep learning methods based on backward stochastic differential equations (BSDEs) have emerged as competitive alternatives to physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) for solving high-dimensional partial differential equations (PDEs). By leveraging probabilistic representations, BSDE approaches can avoid the curse of dimensionality and often admit second-order-free training objectives that do not require explicit Hessian evaluations. It has recently been established that the commonly used Euler-Maruyama (EM) time discretization induces an intrinsic bias in BSDE training losses. While high-order schemes such as Heun can fully eliminate this bias, such schemes re-introduce second-order spatial derivatives and incur substantial computational overhead. In this work, we provide a principled analysis of EM-induced loss bias and propose an unbiased, second-order-free training framework that preserves the computational advantages of BSDE methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/seojaemin22/Un-EM-BSDE.

</details>


### [227] [Slower Generalization, Faster Memorization: A Sweet Spot in Algorithmic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14659)
*Shin So,Kyelim Lee,Albert No*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现“更多数据即更快收敛”的直觉在结构化输出任务（如NW算法）中并不总是成立，存在最优数据集规模“甜点”，超过该规模反而会增加收敛所需的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨“数据量临界值”理论——即更多数据是否总是能加速验证集的收敛，并揭示了该直觉在特定结构化任务中失效的现象及原因。

Method: 在Needleman–Wunsch (NW) 矩阵生成任务中，对比不同数据集规模下小模型（Transformers）的验证准确率收敛速度及计算开销，并与乘法运算任务基线进行对比。

Result: 在NW任务中，中等数据集规模下模型达到最高验证准确率的速度最快，而非最大规模数据集；在达到泛化阈值后，更多数据反而需要更多梯度更新；但在初步掌握规则阶段，大数据集能加速训练拟合。

Conclusion: 在结构化输出任务中，存在“数据量甜点”，学习规则所需的关键数据量与优化收敛速度所需的数据量并不一致；学习规则与实现精确拟合之间存在差异。

Abstract: Critical-data-size accounts of grokking suggest a natural post-threshold intuition: once training data is sufficient to identify the underlying rule, additional data should accelerate validation convergence. We show that this intuition can fail in a controlled structured-output task. In Needleman--Wunsch (NW) matrix generation, small Transformers reach high validation exact-match accuracy fastest at an intermediate dataset size, not at the largest one. Past this dataset-size sweet spot, generalization remains achievable but requires more gradient updates. Conversely, in the regime where partial validation competence first appears, larger datasets can require fewer updates to reach high training accuracy, suggesting that emerging rule structure can accelerate fitting beyond example-wise memorization. A multiplication baseline does not show the same post-threshold slowdown. These results separate the critical data size for the onset of generalization from the dataset size that optimizes update-based convergence, and identify structured-output tasks where learning the rule and completing exact-fitting can diverge.

</details>


### [228] [AQKA: Active Quantum Kernel Acquisition Under a Shot Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14672)
*Jian Xu,Chao Li,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对量子核学习中测量次数分配不合理的问题，提出了一种基于核矩阵项对模型敏感度进行自适应分配的策略AQKA，通过闭式理论推导实现了最优Shot配置，在模拟和真实量子硬件上展现了显著的精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在近期的量子硬件上，估计 $N \times N$ 量子核矩阵需要 $\Theta(N^2 S)$ 的测量次数，这是主要的部署瓶颈。现有的预算节省方法（如Nyström-QKE等）虽然对测量项进行了采样，但在所选子集内仍使用均匀的测量分配，忽视了不同核矩阵项对分类性能的贡献差异。

Method: 1. 提出了AQKA（Adaptive Quantum Kernel Alignment）方法：根据下游分类算法的敏感度，动态分配测量次数（shots）；
2. 导出了闭式配对级获取策略：Shot分配策略公式 $s_{ij}^{\star} \propto |g_{ij}|\sqrt{K_{ij}(1-K_{ij})}$，适用于核岭回归（KRR）和支持向量机（SVM）；
3. 构建了预算分配制度分解模型；
4. 进行了多种子、多平台的真实量子硬件在线自适应分配实验。

Result: 1. 在预算受限条件下（$B \lesssim 16 n_{\mathrm{pairs}}$），AQKA显著优于均匀分配方法，性能提升随规模扩大而增长（$N=1000$时提升25个百分点）；
2. 在IBM 156量子比特硬件上，性能提升达26-32个百分点；
3. 将理论 regret 边界与核矩阵稀疏性关联，提供了更精确的复杂性界限；
4. 实现在线自适应分配，在多个量子硬件平台上获得稳定的精度收益（+14至+17个百分点）。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了AQKA算法，证明了在预算受限的量子核学习任务中，非均匀的测量次数分配优于现有的均匀分配方法。文章构建的闭式解析理论为自适应分配策略提供了严谨的数学支撑，并在真实量子硬件上验证了该方法的显著性能增益。

Abstract: Estimating an $N \times N$ quantum kernel from circuit fidelities requires $Θ(N^2 S)$ measurement shots, the dominant bottleneck for deployment on near-term hardware. Existing budget-saving methods (Nyström-QKE, ShoFaR, kernel-target alignment) sub-sample \emph{which} entries to measure but allocate shots \emph{uniformly} within their chosen subset, ignoring how much each entry drives the downstream classifier. We close this gap with two contributions. \textbf{First, a complete regime decomposition} for shot-budgeted quantum kernel learning: a principled menu of when each allocator wins. Our method, \emph{AQKA}, dominates the budget-limited regime ($B \lesssim 16 n_{\mathrm{pairs}}$) on sparse-sensitivity KRR, with the gap \emph{growing} from $+8$ to $+25$ pts over uniform as $N$ scales $225{\to}1000$ and reaching $+26$--$32$ pts on an \texttt{ibm\_pittsburgh} (156-qubit Heron) hardware kernel; Nyström-QKE wins at saturating budgets on planted-sparse via low-rank reconstruction; ShoFaR is competitive only at extreme low budgets. \textbf{Second, a closed-form pair-level acquisition theory}: $s_{ij}^{\star} \propto |g_{ij}|\sqrt{K_{ij}(1-K_{ij})}$ with explicit gradient $g_{ij}$ for KRR (Lemma~1, $|β_iα_j+β_jα_i|\sqrt{K_{ij}(1-K_{ij})}$) and SVM via the envelope theorem ($|η_i^*η_j^*|\sqrt{K_{ij}(1-K_{ij})}$); a \emph{corrected} sparsity-aware Cauchy--Schwarz rate $ρ\le 2m/N$ matching empirics (vs.\ the naive $m^2/N^2$); an explicit-constant plug-in regret bound (Theorem~2); and a tighter SVM ceiling $ρ^{\mathrm{SVM}} \le m_{\mathrm{sv}}^2/N^2$. We close with the first multi-seed live online adaptive shot allocation on quantum hardware: $+17.0 \pm 4.8$ pts at $N{=}20$ on \texttt{ibm\_aachen} ($3.5σ$, 5 seeds), with the advantage holding at $N{=}30$ at higher budget on \texttt{ibm\_berlin} ($+14.0 \pm 8.5$ pts, 5 seeds).

</details>


### [229] [ReMIA: a Powerful and Efficient Alternative to Membership Inference Attacks against Synthetic Data Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14686)
*Davide Scassola,Andrea Coser,Sebastiano Saccani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 ReMIA 的轻量级、实用性强的隐私评估指标，能够以较低的计算成本有效衡量合成数据中存在的成员推理风险，解决了传统 MIA 方法计算开销大和简单指标不敏感的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的成员推理攻击（MIA）虽然强大，但需要大量的影子模型训练和辅助数据，实用性差；而现有的快速度量指标（如 DCR）的敏感度有限。因此需要一种既高效又准确的实用隐私度量方法。

Method: 提出 ReMIA（相对成员推理攻击）指标，通过构建两个源数据集并生成两个合成数据集，训练分类器来判断样本源自哪个数据集，从而评估成员推理风险。仅需两次 SDG 训练运行，数据规模需求较小。

Result: 在多个表格数据集和 SDG 上的实验表明，ReMIA 具备与最先进方法相当的敏感度，同时显著降低了计算资源和辅助数据的要求。

Conclusion: ReMIA 是一种实用且高效的隐私评估指标，其敏感度与现有的攻击方法相当，但在实际应用中更具可行性。研究还强调了合成数据生成器（SDG）在实现隐私与效用平衡方面优于传统的基于噪声的匿名化方法。

Abstract: Tabular data sharing under privacy constraints is increasingly important for research and collaboration. Synthetic data generators (SDGs) are a promising solution, but synthetic data remains vulnerable to attacks, such as membership inference attacks (MIAs), which aim to determine whether a specific record was part of the training data. State-of-the-art MIAs are powerful but impractical: they rely on shadow modeling, requiring hundreds of SDG training runs, and need auxiliary data several times larger than the original training set. Fast proxy metrics like distance to closest record (DCR) are efficient but have limited sensitivity to MIA risk. We introduce ReMIA (Relative Membership Inference Attack), a practical privacy metric that requires only two SDG training runs and additional data no larger than the original training set. Rather than predicting whether a record was in the training set, ReMIA generates two synthetic datasets from two source datasets and measures whether a classifier can identify which source a record came from. Experiments across multiple tabular datasets and SDGs show that ReMIA has a sensitivity comparable to state-of-the-art MIAs while being substantially more practical. We further observe that SDGs can achieve privacy-utility trade-offs that traditional noise-based anonymization methods do not match. Code is available at https://github.com/aindo-com/remia.

</details>


### [230] [The Rate-Distortion-Polysemanticity Tradeoff in SAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14694)
*Tommaso Mencattini,Francesco Montagna,Francesco Locatello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SAE中的“速率-失真-多义性”权衡理论，指出多义性是数据分布（特别是特征共现）的固有属性，而非单纯的优化问题，并为评估SAE的可解释性提供了理论基础和指标基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的稀疏自动编码器（SAE）虽然在重构精确度（低失真）和特征稀疏性（低速率）上表现良好，但往往无法学习到具有高度可解释性的单义性（monosemantic）表征，限制了其在机理可解释性中的应用。

Method: 1. 提出“速率-失真-多义性”权衡（Rate-Distortion-Polysemanticity tradeoff）理论框架；2. 在玩具模型（toy-modeling）下进行 teorethical 和 empirical 分析；3. 探讨数据分布（特别是特征共现概率）对最优稀疏自动编码器（SAE）多义性的影响；4. 推导未知数据生成过程下的多义性度量必要条件，并对真实大语言模型（LLM）的SAE进行指标基准测试。

Result: 1. 证明了强制SAE实现单义性必然会导致重构失真增加及编码速率受限；2. 证实了最优SAE的多义性程度直接取决于训练数据的分布，特别是特征共现的频率；3. 评估并分析了现有衡量大语言模型SAE多义性指标的有效性与局限性。

Conclusion: 多义性（polysemanticity）本质上是一个数据分布问题。解决这一问题不能仅依赖架构优化或训练策略，必须将其与数据生成过程中的特征共现规律相结合。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) that can accurately reconstruct their input (minimizing distortion) by making efficient use of few features (minimizing the rate) often fail to learn monosemantic representations (highly interpretable), limiting their usefulness for mechanistic interpretability. In this paper, we characterise this tension in learning faithful, efficient, and interpretable explanations, introducing the Rate-Distortion-Polysemanticity tradeoff in SAEs. Under toy-modeling assumptions, we theoretically and empirically show that restricting the SAE to be monosemantic necessarily comes with an increase in rate and distortion. Assuming a generative model behind the input observations, we further demonstrate that the degree of polysemanticity of optimal SAEs is determined by the training data distribution, especially by the probability of features to co-occur. Finally, we extend the analysis to real-world settings by deriving necessary conditions that a polysemanticity measure should satisfy when the data-generating process is unknown, and we benchmark existing proxy metrics on SAEs trained on Large Language Models. Taken together, our findings show that polysemanticity is a data problem that should be accounted for when addressing it at the architectural and optimization level.

</details>


### [231] [NeuroAtlas: Benchmarking Foundation Models for Clinical EEG and Brain-Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14698)
*Konstantinos Kontras,Trui Osselaer,Stylianos G. Mouslech,Angeliki-Ilektra Karaiskou,Guido Gagliardi,Thomas Strypsteen,Mohammad Hossein Badiei,Anku Rani,Maarten Vanmarcke,Miguel Bhagubai,Chanakya Ekbote,Jaedong Hwang,Christos Chatzichristos,Paul Pu Liang,Maarten De Vos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布了覆盖面最广的EEG基准测试集NeuroAtlas，研究发现现有基础模型在处理EEG任务时并未表现出显著优势，且缺乏临床实用性，强调了开发新一代统一EEG模型的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型的研究在数据集、预处理方法和评价指标上缺乏统一标准，导致难以评估其临床实用性，且这些模型在EEG领域的实际效果尚不明确。

Method: 构建了迄今最大的EEG基准测试集NeuroAtlas，涵盖42个数据集和26万小时数据，涉及癫痫、睡眠医学、脑龄估计和脑机接口等多领域。通过定量评估与临床指标（如事件级决策质量、睡眠图特征、脑龄差距）对比，评估了EEG特定基础模型与通用时间序列基础模型在监督学习基线下的性能。

Result: 1. 特定于EEG的基础模型并未持续优于通用的时间序列模型。2. 传统的机器学习指标不足以反映临床效用；特定于任务的临床指标（如脑龄差距、睡眠图特征）对于评估更为重要。3. 不同模型在不同领域内的表现不稳定，排名变化较大。

Conclusion: 目前预训练模型（包括特定于EEG的和通用的时间序列模型）在统一EEG任务上表现平平，未能实现“开箱即用”的通用EEG模型目标。NeuroAtlas基准测试揭示了现有模型与临床需求之间的差距，并为未来模型开发提供了评估标准。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) promise to extract unified representations that generalize across downstream tasks. They have emerged across fields, including electroencephalography (EEG), but it is less clear how effective they are in this particular field. Published evaluations differ in datasets, in the EEG-specific preprocessing that might influence reported results, and in the reported metrics, frequently obscuring the clinical relevance in EEG. We introduce NeuroAtlas, the largest EEG benchmark to date: 42 datasets and 260k hours covering clinical EEG (epilepsy, sleep medicine, brain age estimation) and brain-computer interfaces, and include multiple datasets per task along with bespoke clinical evaluation metrics. Besides evaluating EEG-FMs with respect to supervised baselines, we present results from generic time-series FMs. We report three findings. First, EEG-specific FMs do not consistently outperform time-series FMs, which have neither EEG-focused architectures nor been pretrained on EEG. Second, standard machine learning metrics are insufficient to assess clinical utility: thus, we thoroughly evaluate more appropriate measures such as the quality of event-level decision-making, hypnogram-derived features, and the brain-age gap in the domains of epilepsy, sleep, and brain age, respectively. Third, model rankings and performance can vary substantially within domains. We conclude that pretrained models perform largely on par, with only narrow advantages for a few, and that current models do not yet deliver on the promise of an out-of-the-box unified EEG model. NeuroAtlas exposes this gap and provides the datasets and metrics for the next generation of unified EEG FMs.

</details>


### [232] [TAPIOCA: Why Task- Aware Pruning Improves OOD model Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14738)
*Krish Sharma,Omar Naim,Soumadeep Saha,Nicholas Asher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了任务感知剪枝的几何机制：剪枝并不改变ID性能，但通过移除导致OOD输入几何分布失真的特定层，使表示空间恢复正常，从而提升了模型在分布外数据上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究（如TALE）表明任务感知剪枝能提升模型特定任务表现，但其背后的机制尚不明确，即剪枝为何以及何时能带来性能提升。

Method: 通过控制变量法，从多项式回归任务到大型语言模型（LLM）进行实证分析；通过分析层级范数（layerwise norm）和成对距离（pairwise-distance）剖面来量化几何失真；并利用受控分布偏移和残差缩放干预提供因果证据。

Result: 实验显示：1. 剪枝对分布内（ID）数据性能无提升；2. 剪枝能显著提升OOD数据准确率；3. OOD输入会导致模型内部表示的几何结构（范数和距离关系）发生偏离，剪枝通过移除造成该失真的层，实现了表示空间的重对齐。

Conclusion: 任务感知剪枝通过移除放大分布外（OOD）失真的层，使数据表示重新对齐到模型适应的几何结构，从而提升了泛化性能。该研究揭示了剪枝的几何解释，为理解模型鲁棒性提供了新视角。

Abstract: Recent work has promoted task-aware layer pruning as a way to improve model performance on particular tasks, as shown by TALE. In this paper, we investigate when such improvements occur and why. We show first that, across controlled polynomial regression tasks and large language models, such pruning yields no benefit on in-distribution (ID) data but consistently improves out-of-distribution (OOD) accuracy. We further show empirically that OOD inputs induce layerwise norm and pairwise-distance profiles that deviate from the corresponding ID profiles. This leads to a geometric explanation of task-aware pruning: each task induces a task-adapted geometry, characterized empirically by the representation profiles observed on ID inputs. OOD inputs can introduce a distorted version of the task-adapted geometry. Task-aware pruning identifies layers that create or amplify this distortion; by removing them, it shifts OOD representational norms and pairwise distances toward those observed on the adapted distribution. This realigns OOD inputs with the model's task-adapted geometry and improves performance. We provide causal evidence through controlled distribution shifts and residual-scaling interventions, and demonstrate consistent behavior across model scales.

</details>


### [233] [Selective Safety Steering via Value-Filtered Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14746)
*Bat-Sheva Einbinder,Hen Davidov,Yee Whye Teh,Yarin Gal,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“价值过滤解码”的方法，用于优化 LLM 的安全引导，通过有条件的 Token 过滤显著减少了非必要的干预，在提升安全性的同时最大限度地保留了模型的原始能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的解码阶段安全引导方法存在过度干预的问题，即会修改原本就安全的回复，从而损害大型语言模型在有用性、流畅度、语言风格和连贯性等方面的性能。

Method: 提出了一种基于价值的解码时间（test-time）引导方法。该方法引入了一个基于价值的安全准则来过滤 Token，并为“错误干预”（false interventions）提供明确的概率界限。通过单一的阈值超参数，用户可以在安全性和性能干扰程度之间灵活调整。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在提升安全性的同时，比现有方法更好地保留了模型的原始性能特征，实现了安全、有用与模型质量之间更优的均衡。

Conclusion: 通过基于价值的过滤方法，本文提出的方法在保持大型语言模型原有性能（如有用性、流畅性等）与提升安全性之间取得了更好的平衡，减少了不必要的干预。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) are trained to align with human values, their generations may still violate safety constraints. A growing line of work addresses this problem by modifying the model's sampling policy at decoding time using a safety reward. However, existing decoding-time steering methods often intervene unnecessarily, modifying generations that would have been safe under the base model. Such unnecessary interventions are undesirable, as they can distort key properties of the base model such as helpfulness, fluency, style, and coherence. We propose a new test-time steering method designed to reduce such unnecessary interventions while improving the safety of unsafe responses. Our approach filters tokens using a value-based safety criterion and provides an explicit bound on the probability of false interventions. A single threshold hyperparameter controls this bound, allowing practitioners to trade off higher rates of unnecessary intervention for better output safety. Across multiple datasets and experiments, we show that our value-filtered decoding method outperforms existing baselines, achieving better trade-offs between safety, helpfulness, and similarity to the base model.

</details>


### [234] [Cognitive-Uncertainty Guided Knowledge Distillation for Accurate Classification of Student Misconceptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14752)
*Qirui Liu,Hao Chen,Weijie Shi,Jiajie Xu,Jia Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为解决教育误区诊断中的数据质量与模型部署难题，本文提出一种基于认知不确定性的知识蒸馏框架，通过精确筛选少量高价值样本并动态优化标签学习，实现了轻量级模型对超大规模模型的性能超越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对教育场景中学生误区识别存在的三大挑战：1. 数据稀疏及长尾分布导致的合成困难；2. 错误类别边界模糊及标注噪声严重；3. 大模型部署成本高且受预训练偏差影响，而小模型易过拟合。

Method: 提出了一个双阶段知识蒸馏框架：第一阶段进行标准蒸馏以获取任务基础能力；第二阶段引入基于认知不确定性的双层边际选择机制，识别并挖掘四类关键样本，并设计难度自适应机制调整硬标签与软标签的权重，以平衡类别关系学习与模糊错误类型的区分。

Result: 仅利用10.30%的筛选样本进行训练，在MAP-Charting数据集上MAP@3达到0.9585（提升17.8%）；在初中代数误区基准测试中，4B参数模型实现84.38%的准确率，远超SOTA大模型（67.73%）及72B精调模型（81.25%）。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的双阶段知识蒸馏框架，通过引入认知不确定性驱动的关键样本挖掘和难度自适应学习机制，有效解决了学生认知误区识别中数据稀疏、标注噪声及部署悖论的问题。轻量级模型在该方法下不仅超越了现有大模型的效果，且更适合端侧部署，为教育智能应用提供了高效且精准的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurately identifying student misconceptions is crucial for personalized education but faces three challenges: (1) data scarcity with long-tail distribution, where authentic student reasoning is difficult to synthesize; (2) fuzzy boundaries between error categories with high annotation noise; (3) deployment parado-large models overlook unconventional approaches due to pretraining bias and cannot be deployed on edge, while small models overfit to noise. Unlike traditional methods that increase diversity through large-scale data synthesis, we propose a two-stage knowledge distillation framework that mines high-value samples from existing data. The first stage performs standard distillation to transfer task capabilities. The second stage introduces a dual-layer marginal selection mechanism based on cognitive uncertainty, identifying four types of critical samples based on teacher model uncertainty and confidence differences. For different data subsets, we design difficulty-adaptive mechanism to balance hard/soft label contributions, enabling student models to inherit inter-class relationships from teacher soft labels while distinguishing ambiguous error types. Experiments show that with augmented training on only 10.30% of filtered samples, we achieve MAP@3 of 0.9585 (+17.8%) on the MAP-Charting dataset, and using only a 4B parameter model, we attain 84.38% accuracy on cross-topic tests of middle school algebra misconception benchmarks, significantly outperforming sota LLM (67.73%) and standard fine-tuned 72B models (81.25%). Our code is available at https://github.com/RoschildRui/acl2026_map.

</details>


### [235] [Crys-JEPA: Accelerating Crystal Discovery via Embedding Screening and Generative Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14759)
*Nian Liu,Nikita Kazeev,Stephen Gregory Dale,Artem Maevskiy,Yuwei Zeng,Ryoji Kubo,Pengru Huang,Thomas Laurent,Yann LeCun,Kostya S. Novoselov,Xavier Bresson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对晶体生成中稳定性和新颖性难以兼得的问题，提出 Crys-JEPA 架构，通过能量感知潜空间预测和迭代筛选精炼，实现了高质量新材料的生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的晶体生成模型存在明显的“稳定性-新颖性”权衡：过度拟合已知分布会导致新颖性不足，偏移该分布则会导致稳定性大幅下降；且基于似然的生成方法难以直接优化材料发现的关键指标。

Method: 提出了 Crys-JEPA（一种晶体联合嵌入预测架构），通过学习能够保留形成能差异的能量感知潜空间，将稳定性评估转化为基于嵌入的对比任务，并配合一套“筛选-精炼”流水线进行模型迭代优化。

Result: 在 MP-20 和 Alex-MP-20 数据集上，该方法在 V.S.U.N（稳定性与新颖性相关）指标上分别实现了最高 81.4% 和 82.6% 的性能提升。

Conclusion: Crys-JEPA 成功克服了晶体生成领域中稳定性与新颖性的权衡难题，通过能量感知潜空间和筛选精炼流水线，显著提升了生成既稳定又新颖材料的能力。

Abstract: De novo crystal generation seeks to discover materials that are not merely realistic, but also stable and novel. However, most existing generative models are trained to maximize the likelihood of observed crystals, which encourages samples to stay close to known materials yet not necessarily align with the criteria that matter in discovery. Through an empirical investigation, we show that current crystal generative models are caught in a pronounced stability--novelty trade-off: moving toward the observed distribution preserves stability but limits novelty, whereas moving away from it quickly destroys stability. This suggests that the useful region for discovering crystals that are both stable and novel is extremely narrow. To escape the trade-off, we introduce Crys-JEPA, a joint embedding predictive architecture for crystals that learns an energy-aware latent space preserving formation-energy differences. In this space, stability assessment can be reformulated as an embedding-based comparison against accessible training crystals, reducing the reliance on expensive energy evaluation and task-specific external references. Building on Crys-JEPA, we further develop a screening-and-refinement pipeline that identifies promising generated crystals and reintroduces them to refine the generative model. On MP-20 and Alex-MP-20 datasets, we achieve improvements over baselines up to 81.4% and 82.6% on V.S.U.N metric, respectively.

</details>


### [236] [Composable Crystals: Controllable Materials Discovery via Concept Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14769)
*Nian Liu,Yuwei Zeng,Ryoji Kubo,Nikita Kazeev,Stephen Gregory Dale,Artem Maevskiy,Pengru Huang,Thomas Laurent,Kostya S. Novoselov,Xavier Bresson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于概念组合的晶体生成框架，利用 VQ-VAE 学习并重组可解释的晶体构建块，实现了比传统随机采样更具可控性和更高新颖性的材料结构生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的材料晶体生成方法多依赖于黑盒随机采样，缺乏对结构生成过程的可控性，难以有效地探索训练分布之外的未知材料空间。

Method: 1. 使用矢量量化变分自编码器（VQ-VAE）从数据中自动发现可复用的晶体概念（涵盖局部原子环境与全局对称性）；2. 设计一个组合生成器（Composition Generator），通过迭代自我改进来优化概念的重组效率；3. 将组合后的概念作为条件，引导下游的晶体生成过程。

Result: 在 MP-20 和 Alex-MP-20 数据集上的实验结果表明，该框架在 V.S.U.N（有效性、稳定性、唯一性、新颖性）指标上分别提升了基准模型多达 53.2% 和 51.7%，特别是在新颖性方面表现突出。

Conclusion: 提出了一种概念驱动的组合生成框架，证明了通过学习和重组可复用的晶体概念，可以实现比传统黑盒随机采样更高效、更具可控性以及更高新颖性的材料发现。

Abstract: De novo crystal generation, a central task in materials discovery, aims to generate crystals that are simultaneously valid, stable, unique, and novel. Existing methods mainly rely on black-box stochastic sampling, providing limited control over how generated structures move beyond the observed distribution. In this paper, we introduce a concept-based compositional framework for crystal generation. We train a vector-quantized variational autoencoder to automatically discover a shared set of reusable crystal concepts, which serve as building blocks for guided generation. These learned concepts naturally exhibit interpretability from both local atomic environments and global symmetry patterns, and generalize to crystals from different distributions. By recombining such concepts, our framework enables controllable exploration of novel crystals beyond the training distribution, rather than relying solely on unconstrained random sampling. To further improve composition efficiency, we introduce a composition generator and iteratively refine it using high-quality samples generated by the model itself. The resulting concept compositions are then used to condition downstream crystal generation. Numerical experiments on MP-20 and Alex-MP-20 show that compositing concepts separately increase base model up to 53.2% and 51.7% on V.S.U.N metric, with particular gains in novelty.

</details>


### [237] [Beyond What to Select: A Plug-and-play Oscillatory Data-Volume Scheduling for Efficient Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14773)
*Suorong Yang,Hanqi Zhu,Hai Gan,Fangjian Su,Guang Li,Furao Shen,Soujanya Poria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PODS 是一种轻量级训练调度框架，通过在训练过程中动态震荡数据选择比率，在保持优化稳定性的同时最大化数据选择带来的正则化优势，实现了训练性能与效率的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据选择方法通常固定选择数据比率，忽略了选择比率对模型训练中隐含正则化效应的影响，导致无法在训练效率与模型性能之间达到最优平衡。

Method: 提出一个轻量级的“插入式”框架 PODS（Plug-and-play Oscillatory Data-volume Scheduling），它不改变采样标准，而是动态调度训练过程中的数据选择比率，交替执行低比率（强化正则化）和高比率（恢复优化保真度）阶段。

Result: PODS 在多种数据集、架构和任务中表现优异，例如将 ImageNet-1k 的训练成本降低了 50% 并提升了精度，同时使 LLM 指令微调速度加快超过 2 倍且无性能下降。

Conclusion: PODS 通过在数据选择过程中动态调节数据量（振荡机制），在确保优化稳定性的同时，有效地利用了数据选择带来的隐含正则化效应，显著提升了训练效率与模型泛化性能。

Abstract: Data selection accelerates training by identifying representative training data while preserving model performance. However, existing methods mainly focus on designing sample-importance criteria, i.e., deciding what to select, while typically fixing the selected data volume as the target ratio throughout training. Thus, they are often dynamic in sample identity but static in data volume. In this work, we revisit data selection from an optimization perspective and show that selected-data training induces an implicit regularization effect modulated by the instantaneous selection ratio. This reveals a key trade-off: lower ratios amplify selection-induced regularization, whereas higher ratios preserve data coverage and optimization fidelity. Motivated by this insight, we propose PODS, a Plug-and-play Oscillatory Data-volume Scheduling framework. Rather than introducing another sample-scoring metric, PODS serves as a lightweight module that dynamically schedules how much data to select over training. Under the target selection ratio, PODS alternates between low-ratio regularization phases and high-ratio recovery phases to exploit selection-induced regularization without sacrificing optimization stability. With its lightweight, ratio-level, and task-agnostic design, PODS is compatible with existing static and dynamic selection methods and broadly applicable across training paradigms. Experiments across various datasets, architectures, and tasks show that PODS consistently improves the efficiency-generalization trade-off, e.g., reducing ImageNet-1k training cost by 50% with improved accuracy and accelerating LLM instruction tuning by over 2x without performance degradation.

</details>


### [238] [Peng's Q($λ$) for Conservative Value Estimation in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14779)
*Byeongchan Kim,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CPQL 是一种新的离线多步强化学习算法，通过使用保守的 Peng's Q($λ$) 算子，在实现隐式行为正则化的同时，克服了传统单步保守算法的过悲观问题，实现了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的保守离线 RL 方法通常受限于单步 Bellman 算子，导致由于严重的过悲观估计而限制了性能上限，无法充分利用多步离线轨迹轨迹。

Method: 提出了一种基于保守 Peng's Q($λ$) (CPQL) 的无模型离线多步强化学习算法，利用 PQL 算子进行保守值估计，该算子的不动点更接近行为策略的价值函数，从而实现隐式行为正则化。

Result: 在 D4RL 基准测试中，CPQL 显著优于现有的单步离线 RL 基线；在离线转在线学习中，CPQL 预训练的 Q 函数能有效避免微调初期的性能下降。

Conclusion: CPQL 通过引入多步算子有效缓解了离线 RL 中的过悲观估计问题，并在离线和离线转在线（offline-to-online）场景中均展现出优越的性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: We propose a model-free offline multi-step reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm, Conservative Peng's Q($λ$) (CPQL). Our algorithm adapts the Peng's Q($λ$) (PQL) operator for conservative value estimation as an alternative to the Bellman operator. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work in offline RL to theoretically and empirically demonstrate the effectiveness of conservative value estimation with a \textit{multi-step} operator by fully leveraging offline trajectories. The fixed point of the PQL operator in offline RL lies closer to the value function of the behavior policy, thereby naturally inducing implicit behavior regularization. CPQL simultaneously mitigates over-pessimistic value estimation, achieves performance greater than (or equal to) that of the behavior policy, and provides near-optimal performance guarantees -- a milestone that previous conservative approaches could not achieve. Extensive numerical experiments on the D4RL benchmark demonstrate that CPQL consistently and significantly outperforms existing offline single-step baselines. In addition to the contributions of CPQL in offline RL, our proposed method also contributes to the offline-to-online learning framework. Using the Q-function pre-trained by CPQL in offline settings enables the online PQL agent to avoid the performance drop typically observed at the start of fine-tuning and to attain robust performance improvements. Our code is available at https://github.com/oh-lab/CPQL.

</details>


### [239] [GFMate: Empowering Graph Foundation Models with Test-time Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14809)
*Yan Jiang,Ruihong Qiu,Zi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GFMate，一种针对图基础模型（GFM）的测试时图提示微调方法。通过解耦提示与预训练过程、并利用测试阶段的无标注数据进行互补学习，显著提升了模型的泛化能力与应用效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图提示学习方法过度依赖源域信息，导致提示与预训练模型及特定源域高度耦合，限制了GFM在不同域间的泛化能力；且现有方法忽视了测试阶段无标签数据中蕴含的丰富信息。

Method: 提出了GFMate框架，包括：1. 与预训练无关的质心提示（centroid prompts）和层提示（layer prompts），避免了提示与特定源域或预训练结构的耦合；2. 设计了一种测试时互补学习目标（test-time complementary learning objective），同时利用标注和无标注的测试数据进行自适应优化。

Result: 在12个基准数据集上进行了广泛实验，结果显示GFMate表现优异，最高性能提升达30.63%。

Conclusion: GFMate通过引入与预训练无关的测试时图提示微调方法，有效解决了GFM在跨域和泛化能力上的限制，并利用无标签数据进一步优化了模型性能，证明了该范式的优越性。

Abstract: Graph prompt tuning has shown great potential in graph learning by introducing trainable prompts to enhance the model performance in conventional single-domain scenarios. Recent research has extended graph prompts to improve Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) by few-shot tuning auxiliary prompts. Despite their progress, most existing methods embed source-domain information into prompts, which serve either as input to GFMs or encoded during model pre-training. Such prompt entanglement with specific source domains and GFM pre-training strategy restricts their generalisability to other domains and different GFMs. Furthermore, existing GFM prompts merely rely on few-shot tuning for adaptation, neglecting the rich information in unlabelled target domain test data. Motivated by these insights, this paper aims to empower GFMs with pre-training-agnostic test-time graph prompt tuning, named GFMate. GFMate introduces centroid and layer prompts applied after pre-training on target domains, avoiding entanglement with specific source domains and model pre-training. In addition, a test-time complementary learning objective is devised to exploit both labelled and unlabelled target domain data for effective test-time prompt tuning. Extensive experiments on 12 benchmark datasets demonstrate the superior performance and efficiency of GFMate, achieving improvements of up to 30.63%. Code is available at https://github.com/YanJiangJerry/GFMate.

</details>


### [240] [ToMAToMP: Robust and Multi-Parameter Topological Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14824)
*Ludo Andrianirina,Mathieu Carrière*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ToMAToMP 算法，这是首个利用多参数持久同调理论实现的多函数拓扑聚类方法，不仅解决了传统方法对参数高度敏感和无法多函数处理的缺陷，还通过理论证明保证了其鲁棒性与高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的拓扑聚类算法 ToMATo 存在三大局限：1. 依赖用户手动调节图结构超参数；2. 对函数值的异常值敏感；3. 仅能处理单变量函数，无法应对多维度特征聚类的实际需求。

Method: 引入 ToMAToMP 算法，利用多参数持久同调中的 MMA（Minimal Module Approximation）分解技术，实现同时处理多个度量函数，并证明了其具备鲁棒性，同时利用该框架消除对图构造参数的依赖。

Result: 实验结果表明，ToMAToMP 在多个数据集上展现出高效的聚类性能，且在聚类质量上显著优于现有的拓扑分析及传统非拓扑聚类方法。

Conclusion: ToMAToMP 是首个能够处理多函数输入的拓扑聚类算法，不仅扩展了聚类应用的维度，还通过 MMA 分解实现了对参数设置的解耦和对异常值的鲁棒性，在多种数据集上均表现优于现有拓扑及非拓扑基准方法。

Abstract: Topological clustering, and its main algorithm ToMATo, is a clustering method from Topological Data Analysis (TDA) which has been applied successfully in several applications during the last few years. This is due to its high versatility, as clusters are detected from the persistent components in the sublevel sets of any user-defined function (gene expression, pixel values, etc), and efficiency, as topological clustering enjoys robustness guarantees. However, ToMATo is also limited in several ways. First, a graph on the data points needs to be provided as a hyper-parameter of the method (whose fine-tuning is left to the user). Second, ToMATo is known to be very sensitive to outlier values in the function range. Finally, and most importantly, ToMATo can only handle one function at a time, whereas it is critical to use several functions in various applications. In this article, we introduce ToMAToMP: the first topological clustering method able to handle several functions at the same time with theoretical guarantees. More specifically, we leverage a recent tool from multi-parameter persistent homology, called MMA decomposition, to design our clustering algorithm, and prove that it enjoys robustness properties. As corollaries, we show that it can be used to make ToMATo independent of graph tuning, and robust to outliers. Finally, we provide a set of numerical experiments showcasing the efficiency and quality of the clusterings produced by ToMAToMP, by showing strong improvement over non-topological and topological baselines for various datasets.

</details>


### [241] [GenAI for Energy-Efficient and Interference-Aware Compressed Sensing of GNSS Signals on a Google Edge TPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14839)
*Thorben Wegner,Lucas Heublein,Tobias Feigl,Felix Ott,Christopher Mutschler,Alexander Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于生成式AI（VAE）的GNSS干扰检测方案，通过在边缘TPU上进行模型量化与压缩，实现了低功耗、高压缩比（>42x）的实时干扰分类，有效降低了数据传输成本，同时保持了极高的分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GNSS干扰分类方法依赖于复杂的云端数据传输，难以满足功耗受限环境下的实时性需求，且传输成本高昂。

Method: 1. 构建基于变分自编码器（VAE）及其变体（如FactorVAE）的生成式AI模型进行数据压缩与特征提取；2. 针对Google Edge TPU进行模型架构适配与8-bit量化以降低功耗；3. 对原始IQ数据、FFT数据及手工特征进行处理与分类，并对比重建信号与原始信号的分类性能。

Result: 实现了超过42倍的数据压缩率；在重建信号上对约72种干扰类型的分类表现（F2-score 0.915）与原始信号表现（F2-score 0.923）极其接近。

Conclusion: 该方法在实现高效数据压缩（>42倍）的同时，保持了高精度的干扰分类能力（F2-score 0.915），证明了利用生成式AI（VAE）在边缘TPU上进行实时GNSS干扰检测和压缩的可行性与实用性。

Abstract: Traditional methods for classifying global navigation satellite system (GNSS) jamming signals typically involve post-processing raw or spectral data streams, requiring complex and costly data transmission to cloud-based interference classification systems. In contrast, our proposed approach efficiently compresses GNSS data streams directly at the hardware receiver while simultaneously classifying jamming and spoofing attacks in real time. Given the growing prevalence of GNSS jamming, there is a critical need for real-time solutions suitable for power-constrained environments. This paper introduces a novel method for compressing and classifying GNSS jamming threats using generative artificial intelligence (GenAI), specifically variational autoencoders (VAEs), deployed on Google Edge tensor processing units (TPUs). The study evaluates various autoencoder (AE) architectures to compress and reconstruct GNSS signals, focusing on preserving interference characteristics while minimizing data size near the receiver hardware. The pipeline adapts large-scale AE models for Google Edge TPUs through 8-bit quantization to ensure energy-efficient deployment. Tests on raw in-phase and quadrature-phase (IQ) data, Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) data, and handcrafted features show the system achieves significant compression (>42x) and accurate classification of approximately 72 interference types on reconstructed signals (F2-score 0.915), closely matching the original signals (F2-score 0.923). The hardware-centric GenAI approach also substantially reduces jammer signal transmission costs, offering a practical solution for interference mitigation. Ablation studies on conditional and factorized VAEs (i.e., FactorVAE) explore latent feature disentanglement for data generation, enhancing model interpretability and fostering trust in machine learning (ML) solutions for sensitive interference applications.

</details>


### [242] [GPart: End-to-End Isometric Fine-Tuning via Global Parameter Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14841)
*Paolo Mandica,Michał Brzozowski,Zuzanna Dubanowska,Neo Christopher Chung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GPart 提出了一种无需低秩矩阵约束、基于全局等距映射的高效微调方法，彻底解决了 LoRA 的距离扭曲问题，并实现了极高的参数效率与性能均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA 的双线性结构导致从参数到权重更新的映射并非距离保持（distance-preserving），从而扭曲了优化地形；现有方法未能解决这一核心瓶颈。

Method: 提出 GPart (Global Partition fine-tuning) 方法，放弃了 LoRA 的双线性结构，改用单一的等距分配矩阵（isometric partition matrix）将 $d$ 维训练向量直接映射到全权重空间，且仅需存储 $d+1$ 个值（向量及随机种子）。

Result: GPart 在自然语言理解、计算机视觉和数学推理任务中展现出优于或媲美现有 PEFT 方法的性能，实现了极致的参数效率与卓越的模型表现。

Conclusion: GPart 通过消除低秩假设，利用等距映射直接将低维参数映射到全参数空间，为高效微调（PEFT）提供了一种更简洁且性能优异的替代方案，确立了其在 PEFT 领域的先进地位。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has become the dominant paradigm for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) of large language models (LLMs). However, its bilinear structure introduces a critical limitation: the mapping from trainable parameters to weight updates is not distance-preserving, distorting the optimization landscape. Methods that project a low-dimensional vector into LoRA's parameter space, such as Uni-LoRA, improve parameter efficiency, but the subsequent bilinear LoRA map breaks end-to-end isometry, leaving the core distance-preservation problem unresolved. We propose GPart (Global Partition fine-tuning), a highly parameter-efficient fine-tuning method which removes the low-rank bottleneck entirely. Our method uses a single isometric partition matrix to map a $d$-dimensional trainable vector directly into the full weight space of the model. The result is an extremely minimal fine-tuning pipeline: one random projection, end-to-end isometric, with a single clean hyperparameter ($d$) and storage cost of $d+1$ values (the trainable vector plus a random seed). GPart builds on the theoretical premise that effective fine-tuning can emerge from random low-dimensional subspaces of the full weight space, without imposing low-rank matrix structure. We empirically demonstrate the superior or comparable performance of GPart to existing PEFT methods on natural language understanding, computer vision tasks, and mathematical reasoning. Overall, GPart achieves state-of-the-art efficiency and performance by removing structural constraints, offering a straightforward and elegant path to PEFT.

</details>


### [243] [Exploitation of Hidden Context in Dynamic Movement Forecasting: A Neural Network Journey from Recurrent to Graph Neural Networks and General Purpose Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14855)
*Lukas Schelenz,Shobha Rajanna,Denis Gosalci,Lucas Heublein,Jonas Pirkl,Jonathan Ott,Felix Ott,Christopher Mutschler,Tobias Feigl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了多种轨迹预测模型在NBA动态环境下的表现，发现增强了上下文信息的混合LSTM模型在精度与效率间取得了最佳平衡，并强调了在复杂动态场景下，针对具体任务定制模型架构的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体育领域具有高度的不可预测性与非线性复杂特征（如突发的变向与速度突变），传统统计模型无法捕捉这些复杂动态，现有的深度学习方法（如Transformer、GNN）也难以平衡时序依赖与上下文交互，且往往计算成本高昂。

Method: 评估了包括SARIMAX、KF、PF在内的传统线性预测模型，以及LSTM、GNN、TCNN、Transformer和作者提出的上下文增强型混合LSTM模型；通过实验对比了模型在不同预测时域、历史输入长度和上下文处理能力上的表现。

Result: 在长达2秒的预测时域内，机器学习方法显著优于线性模型。作者提出的上下文增强型混合LSTM模型表现最优，在FDE指标上达到1.51米，且在数据需求和训练效率上优于GAT和Transformer。

Conclusion: 在快节奏、动态的运动行为预测（如NBA轨迹预测）中，不存在通用的最优架构。任务的具体需求（如数据量、实时性、精度）决定了模型选择，且显式引入上下文信息对于提升预测表现至关重要。

Abstract: Forecasting within signal processing pipelines is crucial for mitigating delays, particularly in predicting the dynamic movements of objects such as NBA players. This task poses significant challenges due to the inherently interactive and unpredictable nature of sports, where abrupt changes in velocity and direction are prevalent. Traditional approaches, including (S)ARIMA(X), Kalman filters (KF), and Particle filters (PF), often struggle to model the non-linear dynamics present in such scenarios. Machine learning (ML) methods, such as long short-term memory (LSTM) networks, graph neural networks (GNNs), and Transformers, offer greater flexibility and accuracy but frequently fail to explicitly capture the interplay between temporal dependencies and contextual interactions, which are critical in chaotic sports environments. In this paper, we evaluate these models and assess their strengths and weaknesses. Experimental results reveal key performance trade-offs across input history length, generalizability, and the ability to incorporate contextual information. ML-based methods demonstrated substantial improvements over linear models across forecast horizons of up to 2s. Among the tested architectures, our hybrid LSTM augmented with contextual information achieved the lowest final displacement error (FDE) of 1.51m, outperforming temporal convolutional neural network (TCNN), graph attention network (GAT), and Transformers, while also requiring less data and training time compared to GAT and Transformers. Our findings indicate that no single architecture excels across all metrics, emphasizing the need for task-specific considerations in trajectory prediction for fast-paced, dynamic environments such as NBA gameplay.

</details>


### [244] [REALM: Retrospective Encoder Alignment for LFP Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14867)
*Peicheng Wu,Zhenyu Bu,Runze Ma,Lin Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REALM框架通过回顾性知识蒸馏技术，成功将离线双向LFP模型的表征能力迁移至轻量级因果模型，实现了高效、实时且高性能的行为神经解码，突破了LFP在BCI应用中的性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通道数微电极阵列和无线脑机接口（BCI）对传输带宽和功耗要求极高，传统的脉冲信号（Spike）虽精度高但开销大；而局部场电位（LFP）信号功耗低但精度通常不足，且现有高性能解码模型多采用非因果架构，无法支持实时应用。

Method: 提出名为REALM的框架，采用“离线到在线”的知识蒸馏策略：首先通过掩码自编码（MAE）目标预训练双向Mamba-2教师模型（离线），随后将其知识蒸馏至紧凑的因果（causal）学生模型（在线），并结合表征对齐与任务监督进行联合优化。

Result: 在行为解码任务中，REALM的表现优于现有的因果和非因果LFP状态下的最优方法（SOTA）；同时，相比基准模型，参数量减少了2倍，训练时间缩短了10倍。

Conclusion: REALM通过回顾性知识蒸馏证实了LFP信号可以实现高性能的实时神经解码，为下一代无线植入式BCI提供了一种可扩展、低功耗且高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Spike activity has been the dominant neural signal for behavior decoding due to its high spatial and temporal resolution. However, as brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) move toward high channel counts and wireless operation, the high sampling frequency of spike signals becomes a bottleneck due to high power and bandwidth requirements. Local field potentials (LFPs) represent a different spatial-temporal scale of brain activity compared to spikes, offering key advantages including improved long-term stability, reduced energy consumption, and lower bandwidth requirement. Despite these benefits, LFP-based decoding models typically show reduced accuracy and often rely on non-causal architectures that are unsuitable for real-time deployment. To address these challenges, we propose REALM: a retrospective distillation framework that enables causal LFP decoding. Inspired by offline-to-online distillation strategies in speech recognition, REALM transfers representational knowledge from a pretrained multi-session bidirectional LFP model to a causal version for real-time deployment. We first pretrain a bidirectional Mamba-2 teacher model using a masked autoencoding objective. We then distill this teacher model into a compact student model via a combined objective of representation alignment and task supervision. REALM consistently outperforms both causal and non-causal LFP-based SOTA methods for behavior decoding. Notably, our REALM improves decoding performance while achieving a $2\times$ reduction in parameter count and a $10\times$ reduction in training time. These results demonstrate that retrospective distillation effectively bridges the gap between offline and real-time neural decoding. REALM shows that LFP-only models can achieve competitive decoding performance without reliance on spike signals, offering a practical and scalable alternative for next-generation wireless implantable BCIs.

</details>


### [245] [TILBench: A Systematic Benchmark for Tabular Imbalanced Learning Across Data Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14915)
*Ruizhe Liu,Jiaqi Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 TILBench 大规模基准测试平台，通过 20 多万次实验全面评估了 40 多种表格数据不平衡学习算法，发现不存在“银弹”式方法，并为实际应用中的方法选择提供了系统的选型建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前针对表格数据不平衡学习缺乏系统性的实证研究；目前尚不清楚不同方法在预测性能、鲁棒性及计算可扩展性方面的优劣差异。

Method: 构建了大规模实证基准测试平台 TILBench，涵盖了 40 多种代表性算法和 57 个多样的表格数据集，执行了超过 20 万次受控实验。

Result: 通过大规模实验揭示了不平衡学习方法在不同数据特征下的表现规律，明确了方法选择对场景的依赖性。

Conclusion: 不存在一种在所有场景下均占优的通用方法；方法的有效性与数据集特征及计算约束高度相关；研究提供了基于数据特性的实际应用选型指南。

Abstract: Imbalanced learning remains a fundamental challenge in tabular data applications. Despite decades of research and numerous proposed algorithms, a systematic empirical understanding of how different imbalanced learning methods behave across diverse data characteristics is still lacking. In particular, it remains unclear how different method families compare in predictive performance, robustness under varying data characteristics, and computational scalability. In this work, we present Tabular Imbalanced Learning Benchmark (TILBench), a large-scale empirical benchmark for tabular imbalanced learning. TILBench evaluates more than 40 representative algorithms across 57 diverse tabular datasets, resulting in over 200000 controlled experiments across a wide range of data characteristics. Our findings show that no single method consistently dominates across all settings; instead, the effectiveness of imbalanced learning methods depends strongly on dataset characteristics and computational constraints. Based on these findings, we provide practical recommendations for selecting appropriate methods in real-world applications.

</details>


### [246] [Octopus: History-Free Gradient Orthogonalization for Continual Learning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14938)
*Yuehao Liu,Shanyan Guan,Weijia Zhang,Xuanming Shang,Yanhao Ge,Wei Li,Chao Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Octopus是一种无需历史数据的MLLM持续学习框架，通过梯度正交化（HiFGO）和两阶段解耦微调，在保障性能的同时解决了灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM持续学习方法存在局限性：架构扩展法计算开销大且泛化差；回放法存在隐私和存储瓶颈；单一的正则化法难以完全克服灾难性遗忘。

Method: 提出了一种名为Octopus的两阶段持续学习框架，核心技术包括：1. History-Free Gradient Orthogonalization (HiFGO)：在无需存储历史数据的前提下，通过梯度正交化机制减少参数干扰；2. 两阶段微调策略：将任务适应与正则化解耦。

Result: 在UCIT基准测试中，Octopus达到了性能领先（SOTA），在Avg（平均性能）和Last（最终任务性能）指标上分别超过了现有最优方法2.14%和6.82%。

Conclusion: Octopus框架通过HiFGO技术和两阶段微调策略，在无需历史数据的情况下，有效地平衡了MLLMs的持续学习稳定性与可塑性，并取得了优于现有SOTA方法的结果。

Abstract: Continual learning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) aims to sequentially acquire knowledge while mitigating catastrophic forgetting, yet existing methods face inherent limitations: architecture-based approaches incur additional computational overhead and often generalize poorly to new tasks, rehearsal-based methods rely on storing historical data, raising privacy and storage concerns, and conventional regularization-based strategies alone are insufficient to fully prevent parameter interference. We propose Octopus, a two-stage continual learning framework based on History-Free Gradient Orthogonalization (HiFGO), which enforces gradient-level orthogonality without historical task data. Our proposed two-stage finetuning strategy decouples task adaptation from regularization, achieving a principled balance between plasticity and stability. Experiments on UCIT show that Octopus establishes state-of-the-art performance, surpassing prior SOTA by 2.14% and 6.82% in terms of Avg and Last.

</details>


### [247] [Not All Symbols Are Equal: Importance-Aware Constellation Design for Semantic Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14940)
*Albert Shaju,Christo Kurisummoottil Thomas,Mayukh Roy Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种语义感知物理层框架，通过联合优化向量量化语义编码与自适应星座图映射，实现了对关键任务信息的动态保护，显著提升了通信系统的抗噪鲁棒性与语义传输效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语义通信系统通常将星座图设计与语义编码解耦，导致关键信息符号与非关键符号面临相同的信道误差风险，缺乏针对任务相关信息的差异化保护。

Method: 提出了一个联合语义-物理层框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于矢量量化变分自编码器（VQ-VAE）提取离散潜在概念；(2) 语义关键性指标（SCI），根据任务相关性对概念评分；(3) 深度强化学习（DRL）智能体，根据实时信道状况动态选择传输子集。此外，设计了一种语义感知M-QAM星座图，通过联合共现统计和SCI得分优化符号位置。引入了语义符号脆弱性（SSV）和语义保护概率（SPP）度量机制。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方案在4-QAM到1024-QAM调制阶数下实现了近100%的核心信息保护概率（SPP），优于标准星座图的50%。在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST和FSDD数据集上实现了21:1的压缩比，且语义质量保持在0.9以上，具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该文证明了在存在非均匀语义重要性和共现统计数据的场景下，传统的格雷码调制是次优的。提出的联合语义-物理层框架通过动态分配符号位置，显著提升了任务关键信息的鲁棒性（SPP近100%），并在高压缩率下保持了高质量的任务性能。

Abstract: Semantic communication systems for goal-oriented transmission must protect task-relevant information not only through source compression but also via physical layer mapping. Existing approaches decouple constellation design and semantic encoding, exposing critical symbols to channel errors at the same rate as irrelevant ones. Contrary to this, in this paper, a joint semantic-physical layer framework is proposed, which is composed of a vector quantized-variational autoencoder that extracts discrete latent concepts, a semantic criticality indicator (SCI) that scores each concept by task relevance, and a deep reinforcement learning agent that dynamically selects the transmission subset based on instantaneous channel conditions. At the physical layer, a learned semantic-aware M -QAM constellation assigns symbol positions according to joint co-occurrence statistics and SCI scores, departing from the uniform spacing and Gray coding of standard M -QAM which minimizes average BER without regard for semantic content. We introduce a novel semantic symbol vulnerability (SSV) metric and a semantic protection probability (SPP) to quantify the exposure of task-critical symbols to decoding errors, and prove that any Gray-coded constellation is strictly suboptimal in SCI-Weighted SSV whenever the source exhibits non-uniform semantic importance and co-occurrence statistics. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed constellation achieves near 100% SPP across modulation orders from 4-QAM to 1024-QAM versus 50% for standard constellations at high spectral efficiency, a 21:1 compression ratio with semantic quality above 0.9, generalizing across MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and FSDD without modification.

</details>


### [248] [Efficient Online Conformal Selection with Limited Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14953)
*Sreenivas Gollapudi,Kostas Kollias,Kamesh Munagala,Ali Sinop*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了在带宽（bandit）反馈受限的场景下，使用自适应共形推理（ACI）进行选择的方法，实现了在保障覆盖率有效性的同时，获得子集选择的高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对共形选择（Conformal Selection）问题，旨在解决在仅有有限反馈（bandit反馈）的情况下，如何在保证成功捕捉到至少一个“成功”目标的预设概率前提下，实现选择项子集最优化（即提高效率）。

Method: 利用李雅普诺夫函数（Lyapunov functions）分析框架，将ACI更新规则应用于适当的控制参数或对偶变量，以处理bandit和semi-bandit环境下的共形选择问题。

Result: 证明了ACI规则在对抗性环境下能够达到平均意义上的有效性（Valid），且在独立同分布（i.i.d.）输入下，能够针对随机基准达到亚线性的效率遗憾（sublinear efficiency regret）。

Conclusion: 通过自适应共形推理（ACI）框架，在极少反馈（bandit/semi-bandit）条件下，实现了对共形选择任务的平均有效性与渐进最优效率的理论保证，为在线学习与不确定性量化间架起了新桥梁。

Abstract: We address the problem of conformal selection, where an agent must select a minimal subset of options to ensure that at least one ``success'' is identified with a pre-specified target probability $φ$. While traditional online conformal prediction focuses on maintaining validity for the observed sequence, minimizing the resource cost (efficiency) of such selections, especially under limited feedback, remains a significant challenge. In this work, we consider settings with the most limited ``bandit'' feedback, and demonstrate that the simple Adaptive Conformal Inference (ACI) update rule, when applied to the appropriate control parameter or dual variable, is both adversarially valid, ensuring the success target is met on average for any input sequence (and hence under distribution shifts), and stochastically efficient, achieving sublinear efficiency regret for $i.i.d.$ inputs against an appropriate stochastic benchmark. We show such guarantees under canonical models capturing bandit and semi-bandit feedback to the agent via a unifying algorithmic technique, and analytic framework involving Lyapunov functions. Our approach handles more complex settings than prior work, while requiring significantly less feedback, and our results provide a new theoretical bridge between efficient online learning with limited feedback and distribution-free uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [249] [Distance-Matrix Wasserstein Statistics for Scalable Gromov--Wasserstein Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14981)
*Ao Xu,Tieru Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了解决 GW 距离计算复杂度高的问题，本文提出了 DMW 距离，通过比较采样点的距离矩阵分布来实现高效的度量空间结构比较，并提供了理论支撑和计算加速方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散 Gromov-Wasserstein (GW) 距离是对抗非凸二次优化问题，计算复杂度极高，难以在大规模数据集上应用。

Method: 提出距离矩阵 Wasserstein (DMW) 距离，通过采样计算点对距离矩阵并进行 Wasserstein 变换；采用切片（sliced）和多尺度技术以实现计算加速，并构建了基于多尺度 DMW 的正定核。

Result: 证明了 DMW 是 GW 的下界且是其松弛形式；建立了 GW-DMW 间隙随采样密集度收敛的理论，并给出了基于内在维数的有限样本复杂度界限；实验验证了其在分类与两样本检验任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: DMW 作为一个有效的 GW 距离替代方案，在处理大规模图和度量空间比较时表现优异，具备较好的计算扩展性和收敛理论保障。

Abstract: Gromov--Wasserstein (GW) distances compare graphs, shapes, and point clouds through internal distances, without requiring a common coordinate system. This invariance is powerful, but discrete GW is a nonconvex quadratic optimal transport problem and is difficult to estimate at scale. We propose \emph{Distance-Matrix Wasserstein} (DMW), a hierarchy of Wasserstein statistics comparing laws of random finite distance matrices. Rather than optimizing a global point-level alignment, DMW samples $n$ points from each space, records their pairwise distances, and transports the resulting matrix laws. We prove that DMW is a relaxation and lower bound of GW, and establish a reverse approximation inequality: the GW--DMW gap is controlled by the Wasserstein error of approximating each original measure with $n$ samples. Hence population DMW converges to GW as sampled subspaces become dense. We further give finite-sample bounds, including intrinsic-dimensional rates that depend on the data manifold rather than the ambient matrix dimension $\binom n2$. For scalable computation, we introduce sliced and multi-scale DMW; for $p=1$, the sliced multi-scale dissimilarity yields positive-definite exponential kernels. Experiments on synthetic metric spaces, scalability benchmarks, graph classification, and two-sample testing validate the theory and demonstrate an interpretable GW-style proxy for structural comparison.

</details>


### [250] [Second-Order Actor-Critic Methods for Discounted MDPs via Policy Hessian Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14982)
*Sanjeev Manivannan,Shuban V*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文克服了二阶优化在强化学习中计算困难的问题，通过双时间尺度框架实现了一种高效且稳定的二阶 Actor-Critic 算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的策略梯度方法多基于一阶更新，收敛较慢；而传统的二阶方法虽然收敛速度快，但由于 Hessian 矩阵估计的计算复杂性，在强化学习中的应用受限。

Method: 利用双时间尺度（two-timescale）Actor-Critic 框架，使 Critic 在更快的时间尺度上收敛从而作为准静态（quasi-stationary）函数；通过该近似，设计并实现了一种基于 Hessian-向量积（HVP）计算的二阶 Actor 更新方法。

Result: 提出了一种适用于折扣奖励设置的二阶 Actor-Critic 方法，该方法通过理论论证使得二阶近似在两时间尺度设定下变得合理，并实现了计算上的高效性。

Conclusion: 在双时间尺度框架下，作者成功推导并实现了基于 Hessian-向量积（HVP）的高效、稳定的二阶 Actor-Critic 方法，克服了传统二阶优化在 RL 中的计算瓶颈。

Abstract: We address the discounted reward setting in reinforcement learning (RL). To mitigate the value approximation challenges in policy gradient methods, actor-critic approaches have been developed and are known to converge to stationary points under suitable assumptions. However, these methods rely on first-order updates. In contrast, second-order optimization provides principled curvature-aware updates that are proven to accelerate convergence, but its application in RL is limited by the computational complexity of Hessian estimation. In this work, we analyze second-order approximations for the actor update that leverage the full curvature information of the objective as much as possible. A stable approximation requires treating the action-value function as locally constant with respect to policy parameters, which does not generally hold in policy gradient methods. We show that this approximation becomes well-justified under a two-timescale actor-critic framework, where the critic evolves on a faster timescale and can be treated as quasi-stationary during actor updates. Building on this insight, we formulate a second-order actor-critic method for the discounted reward setting that leverages Hessian-vector product (HVP) computations, resulting in a computationally efficient and stable second-order update.

</details>


### [251] [DeepTokenEEG Enhancing Mild Cognitive Impairment and Alzheimers Classification via Tokenized EEG Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15009)
*Thinh Nguyen-Quang,Minh Long Ngo,Ngoc-Son Nguyen,Nguyen Thanh Vinh,Huy-Dung Han,Bui Thanh Tung,Nguyen Quang Linh,Khuong Vo,Manoj Vishwanath,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种名为 DeepTokenEEG 的轻量化模型，通过空间与时间标记化技术提取 EEG 生物标志物，实现了高精度的阿尔茨海默病诊断，具有极佳的部署前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 EEG 分析面临数据获取困难、深度学习模型架构复杂以及人工解读耗时等挑战，需要开发一种高效、易于部署且准确的自动化诊断方法。

Method: 提出了一种名为 DeepTokenEEG 的模型，引入了空间（spatial）和时间（temporal）分词器（tokenizer），能够同时捕捉时间域和频率域的 AD 相关生物标志物，且参数量仅为 0.29M。

Result: 在包含 274 名受试者（180 名 AD 患者，94 名对照组）的数据集上，模型在特定频段达到了 100% 的最高分类准确率，较现有顶尖方法提升了 1.41%-15.35%。

Conclusion: DeepTokenEEG 是一种轻量级且高效的阿尔茨海默病诊断模型，凭借其紧凑的架构和优异的分类性能，在早期筛查和临床部署方面展现出巨大潜力。

Abstract: The detection of Alzheimers disease (AD) is considered crucial, as timely intervention can improve patient outcomes. Electroencephalogram (EEG)-based diagnosis has been recognized as a non-invasive, accessible, and cost-effective approach for AD detection; however, it faces challenges related to data availability, accuracy of modern deep learning methods, and the time-consuming nature of expert-based interpretation. In this study, a novel lightweight and high-performance model, DeepTokenEEG, was designed for the diagnosis of AD and the classification of EEG signals from AD patients, individuals with other neurological conditions, and healthy subjects. Unlike traditional heavy-weight models, DeepTokenEEG ultilizes spatial and temporal tokenizer that effectively captures AD-related biomarkers in both temporal and frequency domain with only 0.29 million paramaters. Trained in a combined dataset of 274 subjects, including 180 AD cases, and 94 healthy controls, the proposed method achieves a maximum recorded accuracy of 100% on specific frequency bands, representing an improvement of 1.41-15.35% over state-of-the-art methods on the same dataset. These results indicate the potential of DeepTokenEEG for early detection and screening of AD, with promising applicability for deployment due to its compact size.

</details>


### [252] [Generalized Priority-Aware Shapley Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15018)
*Kiljae Lee,Ziqi Liu,Weijing Tang,Yuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了广义优先级感知 Shapley 值（GPASV），通过允许在有向加权图中存在循环，克服了现有优先级感知评估方法的限制，并在 LLM 评估场景中验证了其灵活性与实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的优先级感知 Shapley 值方法要求优先级必须是二元且无环的（acyclic），这限制了其在处理如人类偏好聚合（常存在循环）和多准则比较等现实场景中的应用。

Method: 提出广义优先级感知 Shapley 值（GPASV），将其定义为任意有向加权优先级图上的随机序值；通过公理化表征定义 GPASV，开发相应的计算方法，并引入优先级扫描诊断工具。

Result: 提出的 GPASV 能够涵盖经典的优先级模型作为边界情况；在 LLM 集成评估任务中，GPASV 在 Chatbot Arena 循环偏好图上表现出有效性，证实了优先级权重平衡对评估结果的显著影响。

Conclusion: GPASV 通过引入带权重的有向优先级图，解决了传统优先级感知 Shapley 值对优先级必须是无环且二元这一限制，为处理循环偏好和复杂多准则排序提供了一种鲁棒且灵活的评估框架。

Abstract: Shapley value and its priority-aware extensions are widely used for valuation in machine learning, but existing methods require pairwise priority to be binary and acyclic, a restriction spectacularly violated in real-data examples such as aggregated human preferences and multi-criterion comparisons. We introduce the generalized priority-aware Shapley value (GPASV), a random order value defined on arbitrary directed weighted priority graphs, in which pairwise edges penalize rather than forbid order violations. GPASV covers a range of classical models as boundary cases. We establish GPASV through an axiomatic characterization, develop the associated computational methods, and introduce a priority sweeping diagnostic extending PASV's. We apply GPASV to LLM ensemble valuation on the cyclic Chatbot Arena preference graph, illustrating that priority-aware valuation is not a one-button operation: different balances of pairwise graph priority versus individual soft priority produce substantively different valuations of the same data.

</details>


### [253] [TopoPrimer: The Missing Topological Context in Forecasting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15035)
*Zara Zetlin,Kayhan Moharreri,Maria Safi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TopoPrimer 是一个将全局拓扑结构引入时序预测的框架，通过持久同调和谱层坐标提升了模型在复杂场景（特别是冷启动与季节性波动）下的预测精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时序预测模型在面对季节性突变和冷启动问题时往往表现不佳，该研究旨在通过引入额外的全局拓扑信息来补偿并增强预测系统的捕捉能力。

Method: 提出了 TopoPrimer 框架，通过持久同调（persistent homology）和谱层坐标（spectral sheaf coordinates）提取时序总体的全局拓扑结构，并将其作为嵌入输入整合到预测模型中（支持作为全训练模型的一部分或作为预训练模型的轻量级适配器）。

Result: 在 Chronos 和 TimesFM 等主流模型上的实验表明，TopoPrimer 在四个基准测试中持续提升预测精度（如 ECL 数据集 MSE 提升达 7.3%）；在季节性高峰期，模型性能衰减从 50% 降低至 10% 以内；在冷启动场景下，MAE 降低了 27%。

Conclusion: 将全局拓扑结构作为显式输入能有效增强时序预测模型的鲁棒性与准确性，特别是在季节性峰值和冷启动场景下表现优异，证明了拓扑特征与现有深度学习模型捕捉到的时序信号具有互补性。

Abstract: We introduce TopoPrimer, a framework that makes the global topological structure of the series population an explicit input
  to any forecasting model. TopoPrimer improves accuracy across diverse domains, stabilizes forecasts under seasonal demand
  spikes, and closes the cold-start gap. Precomputed once per domain via persistent homology and spectral sheaf coordinates,
  TopoPrimer deploys per token for fully-trained models and as a lightweight adapter for pre-trained backbones. Of these two
  components, sheaf coordinates are the primary accuracy driver. Across four public benchmarks on Chronos and TimesFM,
  TopoPrimer consistently improves forecasting accuracy, with gains of up to 7.3% MSE on ECL. The topology advantage persists
  with near-identical magnitude across zero-shot and fine-tuned backbones, suggesting topology and per-series training
  capture complementary signals. The gains are most pronounced in difficult regimes. Under peak seasonal demand, classical
  and zero-shot models degrade by up to 50%, while TopoPrimer stays within 10%. At cold start with no item history,
  TopoPrimer reduces MAE by 27% over a topology-free baseline.

</details>


### [254] [Separating Intrinsic Ambiguity from Estimation Uncertainty in Deep Generative Models for Linear Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15050)
*Yuxin Guo,Dongrui Deng,Pulkit Grover*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将深度生成模型的后验不确定性分解为内在模糊性与推理不确定性的方法，通过级联分析实现模型校准，帮助识别仅凭重建质量指标无法发现的隐藏故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度生成模型在处理逆问题时，虽然应用广泛，但其提供的后验不确定性难以解释，无法区分是源于数据本身的前向模糊性，还是推理过程带来的模型噪声，这在医疗等高风险应用中存在隐患。

Method: 提出了一种名为“结构分解（structural decomposition）”的后验不确定性分析方法。通过级联公式（cascade formulation）将后验不确定性分解为模型传播的不确定性与前向算子固有的模糊性，并结合仿真校准测试（simulation-based calibration）进行诊断。

Result: 通过在具有分析解的高斯模型上验证，证明了分解方法的正确性；在核磁共振成像（MRI）实验中展示了 decomposition 的效果；在脑电图（EEG）源成像任务中，利用该方法成功诊断出了现有模型仅靠重建质量指标难以发现的失效模式。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的后验不确定性结构分解方法，能够有效揭示后验分布中的内在模糊性，并为深度生成模型的标定与失效模式诊断提供了定量分析工具，对于高风险领域（如医疗影像）的模型验证具有重要意义。

Abstract: Recently, deep generative models have been used for posterior inference in inverse problems, including high-stakes applications in medical imaging and scientific discovery, where the uncertainty of a prediction can matter as much as the prediction itself. However, posterior uncertainty is difficult to interpret because it can mix ambiguity inherent to the forward operator with uncertainty propagated through inference. We introduce a structural decomposition of posterior uncertainty that isolates intrinsic ambiguity. A cascade formulation makes this ambiguity accessible for calibration analysis, enabling qualitative diagnostics and simulation-based calibration tests that reveal failure modes that remain hidden when models are selected by reconstruction quality alone. We first validate the approach on a Gaussian example with analytical posterior structure, then illustrate the decomposition on accelerated magnetic resonance imaging (MRI), and finally apply the calibration diagnostics to electroencephalography (EEG) source imaging.

</details>


### [255] [TFGN: Task-Free, Replay-Free Continual Pre-Training Without Catastrophic Forgetting at LLM Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15053)
*Anurup Ganguli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TFGN 是一种新型 Transformer 架构叠加层，通过将参数更新“读/写分解”到正交子空间，在无需重放数据或任务 ID 的情况下，解决了大模型持续预训练中的灾难性遗忘问题，并实现了高效的跨域迁移与元控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模语言模型中，在没有重放数据或任务识别标签的情况下，跨异构文本域进行持续预训练会导致灾难性遗忘，现有方法在规模化扩展上存在局限性。

Method: 提出 TFGN (Transformer Factorized Gradient Network) 架构叠加层；采用“读/写分解”策略：前向传播保持全稠密，利用构造化的参数更新机制确保不同领域的梯度更新在正交空间进行，防止覆盖先验领域知识。扩展包含闭环元控制层和算子级规划向量。

Result: 在 6 个异构领域的 3 种模型规模下测试，实现极低遗忘率（backward transfer -0.007），领域间梯度正交分离率达 99.59% 以上，同时展现出显著的正向跨领域迁移增益（如 JavaScript PPL 下降 26.8%~62.0%）。元控制扩展进一步减少 81% 遗忘，算子计划向量具有 99.96% 的余弦保真度。

Conclusion: TFGN 是一种能够有效解决大模型持续预训练中灾难性遗忘问题的架构方案，它在无需重放数据、无需任务标签的情况下，实现了领域间的正向迁移和极其优异的知识保持，并支持元控制和算子级规划。

Abstract: Continually pre-training a large language model on heterogeneous text domains, without replay or task labels, has remained an unsolved architectural problem at LLM scale. Existing methods rely on replay buffers, task identifiers, regularization penalties that scale poorly, or sentence-classification-scale evaluation.
  We introduce TFGN, an architectural overlay for transformer language models that produces input-conditioned, parameter-efficient updates while leaving the rest of the transformer unchanged. On six heterogeneous text domains (Prose, Python, Math, Biomedical, Chinese, JavaScript) at 1B tokens per phase across three model scales (~398M, ~739M, ~9B) and two regimes (From-Scratch and Retrofit), TFGN achieves backward transfer of -0.007 at LLaMA 3.1 8B Retrofit, HellaSwag retention 0.506/0.504/0.510, and >=99.59% L2-orthogonal gradient separation between domain pairs - with no replay, no task IDs, no Fisher penalty.
  The same matrices show positive cross-domain forward transfer: held-out JavaScript PPL drops 26.8% at LLaMA-8B Retrofit and 62.0% at GPT-2 Medium From-Scratch purely from Python training. Two extensions on the same substrate close further open problems. A closed-loop meta-control layer (Extension A) reduces forgetting by an additional 81% at ~398M, mapping onto the System A and System M roles of Dupoux et al. (arXiv:2603.15381). An operator-level plan vector (Extension B) reshapes forward-pass behavior at 99.96% cosine fidelity over 30 source->target pairs.
  The architectural insight is a Read/Write decomposition: the forward pass is fully dense, while cross-domain parameter updates are structured so prior-domain subspaces are not written to. To our knowledge, TFGN is the first architecture that simultaneously closes catastrophic forgetting at LLM scale, realizes a closed-loop autonomous-learning meta-controller, and carries an operator-level latent planner.

</details>


### [256] [DiffusionOPD: A Unified Perspective of On-Policy Distillation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15055)
*Quanhao Li,Junqiu Yu,Kaixun Jiang,Yujie Wei,Zhen Xing,Pandeng Li,Ruihang Chu,Shiwei Zhang,Yu Liu,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了解决扩散模型多任务强化学习中的干扰与遗忘问题，DiffusionOPD 提出了一种通过在线策略蒸馏（OPD）将多个独立教师模型整合至单一学生模型的新范式，并通过新的闭式逐步 KL 目标函数提升了训练效率与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于强化学习的扩散模型优化方法主要局限于单任务，而扩展至多任务时存在任务间干扰、不平衡以及级联训练带来的灾难性遗忘问题。

Method: 提出了一种“在线策略蒸馏（OPD）”训练范式：首先独立训练特定任务的教师模型，随后将其能力蒸馏至单一学生模型中。数学上，将 OPD 框架从离散标记扩展至连续状态马尔可夫过程，推导出统一的闭式逐步 KL 散度目标函数（通过均值匹配统一 SDE 与 ODE 精炼）。

Result: 实验证明该方法在多奖励强化学习和级联强化学习基准上均表现更优，具有更高的训练效率，并在所有评估指标上达成了 SOTA（当前最佳）水平。

Conclusion: DiffusionOPD 是一种高效的多任务扩散模型强化学习范式，能够有效解决多任务训练中的干扰、不平衡及遗忘问题，并实现了优于现有多任务强化学习方法的性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has emerged as a powerful tool for improving diffusion-based text-to-image models, but existing methods are largely limited to single-task optimization. Extending RL to multiple tasks is challenging: joint optimization suffers from cross-task interference and imbalance, while cascade RL is cumbersome and prone to catastrophic forgetting. We propose DiffusionOPD, a new multi-task training paradigm for diffusion models based on Online Policy Distillation (OPD). DiffusionOPD first trains task-specific teachers independently, then distills their capabilities into a unified student along the student own rollout trajectories. This decouples single-task exploration from multi-task integration and avoids the optimization burden of solving all tasks jointly from scratch. Theoretically, we lift the OPD framework from discrete tokens to continuous-state Markov processes, deriving a closed-form per-step KL objective that unifies both stochastic SDE and deterministic ODE refinement via mean-matching. We formally and empirically demonstrate that this analytic gradient provides lower variance and better generality compared to conventional PPO-style policy gradients. Extensive experiments show that DiffusionOPD consistently surpasses both multi-reward RL and cascade RL baselines in training efficiency and final performance, while achieving state-of-the-art results on all evaluated benchmarks.

</details>


### [257] [Learning from Language Feedback via Variational Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15113)
*Yang Li,Erik Nijkamp,Semih Yavuz,Shafiq Rayhan Joty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对强化学习中反馈利用率低及教师评估能力停滞的问题，本文提出变分策略蒸馏（VPD），通过 E-M 算法协同动态优化反馈教师与学生策略，突破了被动蒸馏的性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习从可验证奖励（RLVR）方法面临稀疏信号导致的探索瓶颈，而基于静态固定教师的反馈自蒸馏方法会随着学生模型进步而触及性能天花板。

Method: 提出变分策略蒸馏（VPD）框架，将学习过程形式化为变分期望最大化（EM）问题：在 E 步，通过自适应信赖域更新动态优化教师模型以提取更精准的 token 级反馈；在 M 步，学生模型通过在线策略产生的轨迹学习这些反馈。

Result: VPD 在科学推理和代码生成任务上显著优于标准 RLVR 和现有的自蒸馏基线方法。

Conclusion: VPD 通过协同优化教师与学生模型，解决了传统静态反馈蒸馏的局限性。实验表明，VPD 在科学推理和代码生成任务中表现优异，并揭示了基于反馈的自蒸馏与纯环境驱动强化学习之间的性能边界。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) suffers from sparse outcome signals, creating severe exploration bottlenecks on complex reasoning tasks. Recent on-policy self-distillation methods attempt to address this by utilizing language feedback to generate dense, token-level supervision. However, these approaches rely on a fixed, passive teacher to interpret the feedback. As the student policy improves, the teacher's zero-shot assessment capabilities plateau, ultimately halting further learning. To overcome this, we propose Variational Policy Distillation (VPD), a framework that formalizes learning from language feedback as a Variational Expectation-Maximization (EM) problem. VPD co-evolves both policies: in the E-step, the teacher is actively refined on trajectory outcomes via an adaptive trust-region update, translating textual feedback into a dynamically improved target token distribution. In the M-step, the student internalizes this dense distributional guidance on its own on-policy rollouts. By continuously improving the teacher's ability to extract actionable signals from textual critique, VPD overcomes the limitations of passive distillation. Evaluated across diverse sources of diagnostic feedback on scientific reasoning and code generation tasks, VPD consistently outperforms both standard RLVR and existing self-distillation baselines. Finally, by stress-testing our framework on rigid mathematical reasoning and cold-start regimes, we illuminate the fundamental bounds of feedback-driven self-distillation compared to pure environment-driven RL.

</details>


### [258] [Natural Synthesis: Outperforming Reactive Synthesis Tools with Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15131)
*Frederik Schmitt,Matthias Cosler,Niklas Metzger,Julian Siber,Vladimir Krsmanovic,Mohamed Ghanem,Bernd Finkbeiner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种神经符号合成方法，通过大模型与形式化验证相结合，解决了反应式合成的算法难题，并利用自动形式化技术实现了从自然语言直接合成硬件电路的工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应式合成（Reactive Synthesis）存在两大核心痛点：一是算法复杂度极高，难以自动构建硬件；二是用户编写形式化逻辑规范（Formal Specifications）难度极大。

Method: 提出了一种神经符号合成方法：利用大型推理模型（LLM）进行代码生成，结合模型检测器（Model Checker）提供可靠的符号反馈进行迭代修复；同时引入“自动形式化”（Autoformalization）步骤，将自然语言需求转化为形式化逻辑规范。

Result: 该研究在合成任务中超越了年度竞赛中的主流工具，并成功解决了参数化系统合成这一不可判定问题；同时，实验结果表明基于自然语言的合成性能与基于形式化规范的合成相当。

Conclusion: 该论文证明了神经符号合成方法在解决复杂反应式合成问题及实现自然语言到逻辑规范自动转化方面的有效性，确立了“自然语言到电路合成”作为一种切实可行的端到端工作流。

Abstract: Reactive synthesis, the problem of automatically constructing a hardware circuit from a logical specification, is a long-standing challenge in formal verification. It is elusive for two reasons: It is algorithmically hard, and writing formal specifications by hand is notoriously difficult. In this paper, we tackle both sides of the problem. For the algorithmic side, we present a neuro-symbolic approach to reactive synthesis that couples large reasoning models with model checkers to iteratively repair a synthesized Verilog implementation via sound symbolic feedback. Our approach solves more benchmarks than the best dedicated tools in the annual synthesis competition and extends to constructing parameterized systems, a problem known to be undecidable. On the specification side, we introduce an autoformalization step that shifts the specification task from temporal logic to natural language by introducing a hand-authored dataset of natural-language specifications for evaluation. We demonstrate performance comparable to that of starting from formal specifications, establishing natural synthesis as a viable end-to-end workflow.

</details>


### [259] [Causal Foundation Models with Continuous Treatments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15133)
*Christopher Stith,Medha Barath,Vahid Balazadeh,Jesse C. Cresswell,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个用于连续处理设置的因果基础模型，利用 Transformer 的上下文学习能力，在无需微调的情况下实现了跨任务的高精度因果效应曲线重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的因果推理多集中在二元处理（Binary treatment）设置，对于现实中更普遍且更复杂的连续处理设置（Continuous treatment）研究不足，缺乏能够跨任务泛化的通用建模方法。

Method: 1. 构建了一个包含连续处理变量的数据生成过程先验（Prior），用于生成大规模因果训练语料库；2. 采用基于 Transformer 的架构，通过上下文学习方式实现个体处理响应曲线（Treatment-response curves）的重建，从而摊销原本昂贵的贝叶斯后验推理过程。

Result: 该模型在个体处理响应曲线重建任务上达到最先进（SOTA）性能，具备强大的零样本迁移能力，无需额外训练或微调即可在未见任务中实现有效的因果推断。

Conclusion: 该研究证实了基础模型在因果推理领域的可行性，能够通过上下文学习（In-context learning）有效处理连续处理效应估计任务，且在零样本（Zero-shot）场景下的表现优于针对特定任务训练的传统模型。

Abstract: Causal inference, estimating causal effects from observational data, is a fundamental tool in many disciplines. Of particular importance across a variety of domains is the continuous treatment setting, where the variable of intervention has a continuous range. This setting is far less explored and represents a substantial shift from the binary treatment setting, with models needing to represent effects across a continuum of treatment values.
