In this paper, we present the first causal foundation model for the continuous treatment setting. Our model meta-learns the ability to predict causal effects across a wide variety of unseen tasks without additional training or fine-tuning. First, we design a novel prior over data-generating processes with continuous treatment variables in order to generate a rich causal training corpus. We then train a transformer to reconstruct individual treatment-response curves given only observational data, leveraging in-context learning to amortize expensive Bayesian posterior inference. Our model achieves state-of-the-art performance on individual treatment-response curve reconstruction tasks compared to causal models which are trained specifically for those tasks.

</details>


### [260] [Training ML Models with Predictable Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15134)
*Will Schwarzer,Scott Niekum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对机器学习模型部署规模下的失效预测问题，分析了现有外推估计器的偏差，并提出了一种“可预测性损失”微调方法，有效提升了对稀有、致命失效模式的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部署规模下，评估集往往不足以观察到关键的低概率失效，当前的各种失效外推方法存在预测偏差及在面对未知稀有失效模式时失效的问题。

Method: 1. 分析了基于最大k个失败分数的外推估计器的误差来源（有限k分解）；2. 发现该估计器存在安全倾向的偏置，但在缺失稀有失效模式时会失效；3. 提出了“可预测性损失”（forecastability loss）微调目标，以改善模型在极端情况下的可预测性。

Result: 在语言模型密码游戏和强化学习网格世界实验中，该方法显著降低了预测误差，同时保持了主任务性能，且安全性与监督基线相当。

Conclusion: 通过引入“可预测性损失”（forecastability loss）进行微调，能有效提升模型在极端失效情况下的预测精度，在保证主任务能力的同时，提高了大规模部署下的安全评估准确性。

Abstract: Estimating how often an ML model will fail at deployment scale is central to pre-deployment safety assessment, but a feasible evaluation set is rarely large enough to observe the failures that matter. Jones et al. (2025) address this by extrapolating from the largest k failure scores in an evaluation set to predict deployment-scale failure rates. We give a finite-k decomposition of this estimator's forecast error and show that it has a built-in bias toward over-prediction in the typical case, which is the safety-favorable direction. This bias is offset when the evaluation set misses a rare high-failure mode that the deployment set contains, leaving the forecast to under-predict at deployment scale. We propose a fine-tuning objective, the forecastability loss, that addresses this failure mode. In two proof-of-concept experiments, a language-model password game and an RL gridworld, fine-tuning substantially reduces held-out forecast error while preserving primary-task capability and achieving safety similar to that of supervised baselines.

</details>


### [261] [Widening the Gap: Exploiting LLM Quantization via Outlier Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15152)
*Xiaohua Zhan,Kazuki Egashira,Robin Staab,Mark Vero,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对主流大模型（LLM）量化方案的通用触发式攻击方法。通过植入特定异常值利用量化过程中的权重舍入逻辑实现受控的“权重坍塌”，成功在包括AWQ、GPTQ和GGUF在内的先进量化模型中植入恶意行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量化触发式攻击大多仅局限于简单的量化方法，在面对先进的量化方案（如AWQ、GPTQ、GGUF）时失效，研究旨在填补这一空白，验证主流复杂量化方案下的模型安全性。

Method: 提出了一种基于“权重坍塌（weight collapse）”的触发式植入攻击方案。通过将异常值（outliers）注入特定的权重块，利用现代量化算法将其他权重舍入为零的特性，精确控制模型在量化后的行为改变。

Result: 该攻击在三种攻击场景和多种LLM上进行了评估，在先前方法无法渗透的高级量化方案中，实现了极高的攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 该研究通过“权重坍塌”机制，首次证明了量化引发的安全风险不仅限于简单的量化方案，在复杂且广泛使用的量化方法（如AWQ、GPTQ、GGUF）中同样适用，揭示了LLM部署中严重的安全漏洞。

Abstract: LLM quantization has become essential for memory-efficient deployment. Recent work has shown that quantization schemes can pose critical security risks: an adversary may release a model that appears benign in full precision but exhibits malicious behavior once quantized by users. However, existing quantization-conditioned attacks have been limited to relatively simple quantization methods, where the attacker can estimate weight regions that remain invariant under the target quantization. Notably, prior attacks have consistently failed to compromise more popular and sophisticated schemes, limiting their practical impact. In this work, we introduce the first quantization-conditioned attack that consistently induces malicious behavior that can be triggered by a broad range of advanced quantization techniques, including AWQ, GPTQ, and GGUF I-quants. Our attack exploits a simple property shared by many modern quantization methods: large outliers can cause other weights to be rounded to zero. Consequently, by injecting outliers into specific weight blocks, an adversary can therefore induce a targeted, predictable weight collapse in the model. This effect can be used to craft seemingly benign full-precision models that exhibit a wide range of malicious behaviors after quantization. Through extensive evaluation across three attack scenarios and LLMs, we show that our attack achieves high success rates against a broad range of quantization methods on which prior attacks fail. Our results demonstrate, for the first time, that the security risks of quantization are not restricted to simpler schemes but are broadly relevant across complex, widely-used quantization methods.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [262] [Towards Robotic Dexterous Hand Intelligence: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13925)
*Weiguang Zhao,Xihao Guo,Tian Liang,Rui Zhang,Irwin King,Kaizhu Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于机器人灵巧手研究的全面综述。针对当前该领域标准不统一、研究碎片化的问题，文章系统分析了灵巧手的硬件设计、控制学习方法、数据资源及评估体系，旨在为未来研究提供结构化参考并指明关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人灵巧手研究在硬件形态、传感配置、任务设定及评估协议等方面缺乏统一标准，导致研究成果间难以进行系统性对比，阻碍了领域内技术路线图的清晰梳理。

Method: 本研究采用综述分析法（Holistic Review），从以下四个维度对灵巧手研究进行系统性梳理：1. 硬件分析（驱动、传感、设计权衡）；2. 方法论分类（控制与学习范式的演进）；3. 数据与评估（数据集、模态设计与评价协议）；4. 局限性与展望（总结瓶颈并提出未来研究方向）。

Result: 提供了机器人灵巧手研究的全面综述，明确了硬件设计中的核心性能权衡（如力量、柔顺性、系统复杂度），梳理了控制与学习算法的演进路径，并汇总了当前的数据资源与评估现状。

Conclusion: 本文通过整合硬件设计、控制方法、数据资源及评估协议，构建了机器人灵巧手研究的结构化框架，揭示了当前领域存在的碎片化问题，并指明了未来解决关键挑战的发展方向。

Abstract: Robotic dexterous hands are central to contact-rich manipulation, with rapid progress driven by advances in hardware, sensing, control, simulation, and data generation. However, existing studies are often developed under different assumptions regarding hand embodiments, sensory configurations, task settings, training data, and evaluation protocols, making systematic comparison difficult and obscuring the developmental trajectory of the field. This survey provides a holistic review of dexterous hand research from four complementary aspects. First, we present a hardware-level analysis covering actuation, transmission, perception, and representative hand designs, highlighting the key trade-offs in force capability, compliance, bandwidth, integration, and system complexity. Furthermore, we review control and learning methods for dexterous manipulation from a methodological perspective, grouping representative works by major paradigms and tracing their evolution in chronological order. In addition, we consolidate datasets, modality design, and evaluation practices, which enables methodological progress to be interpreted together with the ways in which it is trained, benchmarked, and assessed. Finally, we discuss the major limitations of current dexterous hand research and summarize the corresponding future directions. By connecting hardware analysis, methodological development, data resources, and evaluation, this survey aims to provide a structured understanding of dexterous hand research and to clarify the most important open challenges for future study.

</details>


### [263] [Ergodic Imitation for Adaptive Exploration around Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13996)
*Ziyi Xu,Cem Bilaloglu,Yiming Li,Sylvain Calinon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应遍历仿制方法，通过将演示轨迹转化为目标分布进行遍历控制，使机器人在保持模仿的同时具备自适应在线探索能力，解决环境失配导致的任务失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器人模仿学习中因训练与部署环境失配、观测或控制偏差导致的任务失败问题，需要一种既能遵循演示又能进行在线自适应探索的策略。

Method: 提出了一种自适应遍历仿制（Adaptive Ergodic Imitation）方法；通过检索示教轨迹构建目标分布；利用遍历控制（Ergodic Control）在跟踪示教轨迹与在线探索之间进行自适应插值。

Result: 证明了将遍历控制从传统的区域覆盖推广到基于示教的模仿学习任务中是可行的，并能使机器人在面对环境不确定性时更灵活地执行任务。

Conclusion: 通过将示教信息融入基于检索的滚动时域框架，该方法能够有效地将人体示教与自适应探索相结合，提升机器人在面对环境失配时的任务成功率与鲁棒性。

Abstract: In robotics, a common challenge in imitation learning is the mismatch between training and deployment conditions, caused, for example, by environmental changes or imperfect observation and control. When a robot follows a nominal trajectory under such mismatch, it may become stuck and fail to complete the task. This calls for adaptive online exploration strategies that remain grounded in demonstrations. To this end, we propose an adaptive ergodic imitation approach that constructs a target distribution from the geometry of the retrieved demonstrations and uses it to generate trajectories that adaptively interpolate between tracking and exploration. Our method extends ergodic control beyond its traditional role in area-coverage and search by incorporating demonstrations into a retrieval-based receding-horizon framework for adaptive imitation.

</details>


### [264] [Behavior Cloning for Active Perception with Low-Resolution Egocentric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14106)
*Anthony Bilic,Chen Chen,Ladislau Bölöni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过行为克隆实现了机械臂的主动感知，证明了无需复杂的任务规划，仅依靠低分辨率第一人称RGB视觉和动作预测，即可实现高效的目标对准与定位任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究行为克隆是否足以诱导机器人产生“主动感知”（active perception）能力，即通过一系列动作来改善后续观测，从而完成结构化对象查找和定位任务。

Method: 采用行为克隆方法，通过末端安装有RGB摄像头的低成本机械臂，实现闭环控制。模型直接从低分辨率RGB图像输入预测机器人关节的相对增量（delta）以实现目标避障与对准。

Result: 研究表明，在低分辨率自中心视觉下，系统能够可靠地完成定位植物的任务；实验证实，预测相对关节增量（relative joint deltas）比直接预测绝对关节位置具有更好的性能。

Conclusion: 行为克隆（Behavior Cloning）足以在结构化目标寻找任务中产生具备主动感知能力的机器人行为。使用相对关节增量预测优于绝对位置预测，且低分辨率的第一人称视角足以支持可靠的任务完成。

Abstract: We investigate whether behavior cloning is sufficient to produce active perception in a structured object-finding task. A low-cost robot arm equipped with a wrist-mounted egocentric RGB camera must reposition to center a partially visible plant before triggering a grasp signal, requiring actions that improve future observations. The model predicts joint commands directly from low-resolution RGB images under closed-loop control. We show that low-resolution egocentric vision is sufficient for reliable task completion and that predicting relative joint deltas substantially outperforms absolute joint position prediction in our setting. These results demonstrate that visually grounded active perception can emerge from behavior cloning in a reproducible setting.

</details>


### [265] [Motion Planning for Autonomous Vehicles using Optimization over Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14199)
*Matheus Wagner,Antônio Augusto Fröhlich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出利用凸集图（GCS）求解自动驾驶运动规划问题，通过离散化非凸环境并将动力学约束凸化，实现了比传统非线性优化更高效、鲁棒的轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非线性最优控制方法虽然精度高，但计算开销大且对初始化敏感；而几何规划方法虽然扩展性好，但常将路径选择与轨迹优化解耦。需寻找一种既能处理复杂环境、满足实时性需求，又能保证动力学可行性的高效规划方法。

Method: 使用凸集图（GCS）处理非凸几何空间，通过将自由空间划分为一组凸区域的导向图，结合贝塞尔曲线路径参数化与时间缩放多项式；利用简化的动力学自行车模型，将动力学可行性转化为凸约束。

Result: 在CommonRoad场景测试中，该方法生成的轨迹不仅无碰撞且符合动力学一致性，与非线性最优控制的结果高度吻合，且在计算速度和对初始化的依赖性方面表现更优。

Conclusion: 凸集图（GCS）提供了一种针对非线性运动规划问题的结构化近似方法，能在保持凸性的前提下，不仅有效捕捉几何与动态特征，还比传统的非线性规划法具有更高的计算效率和更强的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Motion planning for autonomous vehicles requires generating collision-free and dynamically feasible trajectories in complex environments under real-time constraints. While nonlinear optimal control formulations provide high-fidelity solutions, they are computationally demanding and sensitive to initialization, whereas geometric planning methods scale well but often decouple path selection from trajectory optimization. This paper studies the extent to which optimization over Graphs of Convex Sets (GCS) can approximate solutions of nonlinear optimal control problems in the context of autonomous driving. The free space is represented as a finite union of convex regions organized as a directed graph, allowing nonconvex geometry to be handled through discrete connectivity decisions while maintaining convex trajectory constraints within each region. Vehicle motion is parameterized using Bezier curves for the spatial path and a polynomial time-scaling function for temporal evolution. Under small-slip and linear tire assumptions, a simplified dynamic bicycle model enables approximate enforcement of dynamic feasibility through convex constraints on trajectory derivatives. The approach is evaluated in CommonRoad scenarios involving static obstacle avoidance and lane-changing maneuvers, and is compared against a nonlinear discrete-time optimal control formulation. The results indicate that the GCS-based method generates collision-free and dynamically consistent trajectories that closely match those obtained from the nonlinear program, while exhibiting improved computational efficiency and reduced sensitivity to initialization. These findings suggest that GCS provides a structured approximation of nonlinear motion planning problems, capturing dominant geometric and dynamic effects while preserving convexity in the continuous relaxation.

</details>


### [266] [MAPLE: Latent Multi-Agent Play for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14201)
*Rajeev Yasarla,Deepti Hegde,Hsin-Pai Cheng,Shizhong Han,Yunxiao Shi,Meysam Sadeghigooghari,Hanno Ackermann,Litian Liu,Pranav Desai,Fatih Porikli,Mohammad Ghavamzadeh,Hong Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MAPLE 是一种无需外部模拟器的端到端自动驾驶训练框架，通过在 VLA 模型潜在空间内进行多智能体闭环交互模拟与强化学习微调，显著提升了自动驾驶系统的鲁棒性与规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型采用传统的模仿学习，在闭环场景下表现脆弱；现有的闭环监督方法扩展性差，且难以有效建模动态响应环境；传统的外部模拟器计算成本高且真实感有限。

Method: 提出了 MAPLE 框架，包含两个阶段：1. 基于真实轨迹的潜在空间模拟回放进行监督微调；2. 结合全局与智能体特定奖励（安全、进度、交互真实性）及多样性奖励进行强化学习。通过在潜在空间内独立控制自车及周围智能体实现闭环交互模拟。

Result: 在 Bench2Drive 基准测试中实现了自动驾驶性能的 SOTA，验证了其在闭环多智能体博弈中的可扩展性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MAPLE 通过端到端的潜在空间多智能体回放训练，无需昂贵的外部模拟器即可提升 VLA 模型在闭环驾驶任务中的鲁棒性与泛化能力，达到了 SOTA 表现。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are effective as end-to-end motion planners, but can be brittle when evaluated in closed-loop settings due to being trained under traditional imitation learning framework. Existing closed-loop supervision approaches lack scalability and fail to completely model a reactive environment. We propose MAPLE, a novel framework for reactive, multi-agent rollout of a dynamic driving scenario in the latent space of the VLA model. The ego vehicle and nearby traffic agents are independently controlled over multi-step horizons, while being reactive to other agents in the scene, enabling closed-loop training. MAPLE consists of two training stages: (1) supervised fine-tuning on the latent rollouts based on ground-truth trajectories, followed by (2) reinforcement learning with global and agent -specific rewards that encourage safety, progress, and interaction realism. We further propose diversity rewards that encourage the model to generate planning behaviors that may not be present in logged driving data. Notably, our closed-loop training framework is scalable and does not require external simulators, which can be computationally expensive to run and have limited visual fidelity to the real-world. MAPLE achieves state-of-the-art driving performance on Bench2Drive and demonstrates scalable, closed-loop multi-agent play for robust E2E autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [267] [Reactive Planning based Control for Mobile Robots in Obstacle-Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14232)
*Li Tan,Junlin Xiong,Yan Wang,Wei Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对部分环境信息下移动机器人的反应式规划控制策略（RPCS），通过结合局部路径动态修改（RPS）与自适应轨迹跟踪（ATCS），实现了复杂环境下的高效无碰撞导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决移动机器人在充满障碍物的复杂环境且仅具备部分环境信息的前提下，实现安全、有效的运动控制与避障导航问题。

Method: 提出了一种反应式规划控制策略（RPCS）。该方法包含两个核心模块：一是反应式规划策略（RPS），通过局部修改参考路径实现避障；二是自适应跟踪控制策略（ATCS），利用离散化技术实现对轨迹的鲁棒跟踪。

Result: 通过数值仿真实验验证了所提 RPCS 策略的有效性，证明了该方法在处理障碍物环境下的路径规划与运动控制能力。

Conclusion: RPCS 策略在移动机器人避障运动控制中表现出高效性与优势，能够有效地在局部已知环境下实现从起点到终点的无碰撞轨迹跟踪。

Abstract: This paper addresses the motion control problem for mobile robots in obstacle-cluttered environments. The mobile robot has partial environment information only, and aims to move from an initial position to a target position without collisions. For this purpose, a reactive planning based control strategy (RPCS) is proposed. First, the initial and target positions are connected as a reference trajectory. Then, a reactive planning strategy (RPS) is developed to ensure the collision avoidance by modifying the reference trajectory locally based on the partial environment information. Next, an adaptive tracking control strategy (ATCS) is proposed to track the reference trajectory with potentially local modifications via the discretization techniques. Finally, the RPS and ATCS are combined to establish the RPCS, whose efficacy and advantages are illustrated by numerical examples.

</details>


### [268] [Distill: Uncovering the True Intent behind Human-Robot Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14262)
*Ting Li,David Porfirio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了 Distill 方法，通过简化、泛化和去约束步骤，优化了机器人对模糊用户指令的理解，实现了更精准的人机意图沟通。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人机交互方式（如自然语言和终端用户编程）存在局限性：自然语言往往含糊不清，而终端用户编程则过于受限，两者都难以准确捕捉用户的真实意图。

Method: 提出了 Distill 方法，包含三个核心步骤：1) 剔除任务中不必要的步骤；2) 对单个步骤的含义进行泛化；3) 放宽步骤间的执行顺序约束。作者在 Web 界面上实现了该方法，并进行了众包研究以验证其效能。

Result: 通过众包研究证明，Distill 方法能够成功从用户的初始任务描述中引导并提炼出更准确、更符合用户真实意图的任务规范。

Conclusion: Distill 方法通过简化、泛化和放宽约束，有效地解决了人机交互中自然语言模糊和终端用户编程过于具体的问题，显著增强了机器人对用户真实意图的理解能力。

Abstract: As robots become increasingly integrated into everyday environments, intuitive communication paradigms such as natural language and end-user programming have become indispensable for specifying autonomous robot behavior. However, these mechanisms are ineffective at fully capturing user intent: natural language is imprecise and ambiguous, whereas end-user programming can be overly specific. As a result, understanding what users truly mean when they interact with robots remains a central challenge for human-AI communication systems. To address this issue, we propose the Distill approach for human-robot communication interfaces. Given a task specification provided by the user, Distill (1) removes unnecessary steps; (2) generalizes the meaning behind individual steps; and (3) relaxes ordering constraints between steps. We implemented Distill on a web interface and, through a crowdsourcing study, demonstrated its ability to elicit and refine user intent from initial task specifications.

</details>


### [269] [Energy-Efficient Quadruped Locomotion with Compliant Feet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14411)
*Pramod Pal,Shishir Kolathaya,Ashitava Ghosal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过在强化学习中加入足部柔顺性设计，证明了选择合适的足部弹性刚度可将四足机器人的运动能耗降低约17%，有效提升了 locomotion 效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的四足机器人多采用刚性足，虽简化了控制，但无法吸收冲击力或回收弹性势能，导致运动能效较低。研究旨在探索引入足部柔顺性是否能提升运动效率。

Method: 1. 将足部柔顺性（不同弹簧刚度）引入强化学习（RL）运动控制框架；2. 在仿真中对8种不同刚度的策略进行训练与交叉评估；3. 在实机平台上进行对比实验，测量单位距离的机械能消耗。

Result: 实验表明，采用中等刚度的弹簧足相比过硬或过软的足部，能耗降低约17%，且这一趋势在仿真与实机中保持一致。

Conclusion: 足部柔顺性（compliance）对于提升四足机器人的运动效率具有显著作用。选择合适的弹性刚度能够优化能量回弹与冲击吸收，在不破坏稳定性的前提下显著降低能量消耗。

Abstract: Quadruped robots are often designed with rigid feet to simplify control and maintain stable contact during locomotion. While this approach is straightforward, it limits the ability of the legs to absorb impact forces and reuse stored elastic energy, leading to higher energy expenditure during locomotion. To explore whether compliant feet can provide an advantage, we integrate foot compliance into a reinforcement learning (RL) locomotion controller and study its effect on walking efficiency. In simulation, we train eight policies corresponding to eight different spring stiffness values and then cross-evaluate their performance by measuring mechanical energy consumed per meter traveled. In experiments done on a developed quadruped, the energy consumption for the intermediate stiffness spring is lower by ~ 17% when compared to a very stiff or a very flexible spring incorporated in the feet, with similar trends appearing in the simulation results. These results indicate that selecting an appropriate foot compliance can improve locomotion efficiency without destabilizing the robot during motion.

</details>


### [270] [Before the Body Moves: Learning Anticipatory Joint Intent for Language-Conditioned Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14417)
*Haozhe Jia,Honglei Jin,Yuan Zhang,Youcheng Fan,Shaofeng Liang,Lei Wang,Shuxu Jin,Kuimou Yu,Zinuo Zhang,Jianfei Song,Wenshuo Chen,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为了解决人形机器人语言控制中的动作反应滞后问题，本文提出 DAJI 框架，通过预测性“联合意图”接口实现对未来物理接触和平衡准备的提前规划，显著提升了动作执行的质量与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言控制人形机器人系统通常反应滞后（执行器仅负责被动跟踪）或缺乏对未来物理环境交互（接触变换、重心转移）的明确建模，导致连续动作平滑性和稳定性不足。

Method: 提出 DAJI (Dynamics-Aligned Joint Intent) 分层框架：
1. DAJI-Flow：利用 autoregressive 模型基于指令和历史意图自动生成未来的意图分段（future intent chunks）。
2. DAJI-Act：通过学生驱动的 rollout，将具有预测能力的教师模型提炼为可部署的扩散策略（Diffusion Action Policy），以输出能够主动处理接触点变化和平衡调整的动作。

Result: 在 HumanML3D 数据集达到 94.42% 的 Rollout 成功率，在 BABEL 数据集达到 0.152 的子序列 FID，证明了该方法在 anticipatory latent learning 和指令跟随任务中的优异表现。

Conclusion: DAJI 框架通过引入预测性“联合意图”接口，有效解决了人形机器人语言控制中反应滞后与动作衔接不连贯的问题，在多样化任务指令下展现了高鲁棒性和语义对齐能力。

Abstract: Natural language is an intuitive interface for humanoid robots, yet streaming whole-body control requires control representations that are executable now and anticipatory of future physical transitions. Existing language-conditioned humanoid systems typically generate kinematic references that a low-level tracker must repair reactively, or use latent/action policies whose outputs do not explicitly encode upcoming contact changes, support transfers, and balance preparation. We propose \textbf{DAJI} (\emph{Dynamics-Aligned Joint Intent}), a hierarchical framework that learns an anticipatory joint-intent interface between language generation and closed-loop control. DAJI-Act distills a future-aware teacher into a deployable diffusion action policy through student-driven rollouts, while DAJI-Flow autoregressively generates future intent chunks from language and intent history. Experiments show that DAJI achieves strong results in anticipatory latent learning, single-instruction generation, and streaming instruction following, reaching 94.42\% rollout success on HumanML3D-style generation and 0.152 subsequence FID on BABEL.

</details>


### [271] [SeaVis: Modeling and Control of a Remotely Operated Towed Vehicle for Seabed Visualization and Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14683)
*Abdelhakim Amer,Aske Alstrup,Frederik Rasmussen,Yury Brodskiy,Andriy Sarabakha,Erdal Kayacan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对SeaVis拖曳式水下机器人，提出了基于增益调度LQR的深度与姿态控制方法，经验证，该方法相比传统PID能够显著提升系统的鲁棒性与控制效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率海底测绘任务对水下机器人的定位稳定性与控制精度提出了严苛要求，现有控制手段在复杂干扰环境下的性能有待提升。

Method: 1. 建立SeaVis拖曳式水下机器人（ROTV）的动力学数学模型；2. 设计基于增益调度（Gain-scheduling）的线性二次型调节器（LQR）以控制深度与姿态；3. 在高保真仿真环境中与传统PID控制器进行对比分析。

Result: 与PID控制器相比，所提出的LQR控制器展现出更好的环境干扰鲁棒性、更高的控制效率，并大幅减少了襟翼的冗余动作，有效验证了其在全速度范围内的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过仿真验证，该增益调度LQR控制器在应对复杂海底地形时表现出优于PID控制器的性能，显著增强了ROTV系统的鲁棒性，并有效降低了执行机构（襟翼）的动作频率与能量消耗。该研究为高分辨率海底测绘提供了可靠的控制策略，且相关代码已开源。

Abstract: High-resolution seafloor mapping necessitates stable and precise positioning for underwater robots. This paper introduces a novel mathematical model for SeaVis remotely operated towed vehicles (ROTVs) and develops a gain-scheduled linear-quadratic regulator (LQR) for robust depth and attitude control. We validate the approach in a high-fidelity simulation, benchmarking the LQR against a conventional PID controller over a challenging seabed profile. The presented results demonstrate the LQR's superior performance, with significantly enhanced robustness to disturbances, greater control efficiency, and substantially reduced flap actuation. The gain scheduling also confirms the controller's effectiveness across the full operational velocity range. The complete simulation environment and controller are open-sourced.

</details>


### [272] [IntentVLA: Short-Horizon Intent Modeling for Aliased Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14712)
*Shijie Lian,Bin Yu,Xiaopeng Lin,Zhaolong Shen,Laurence Tianruo Yang,Yurun Jin,Haishan Liu,Changti Wu,Hang Yuan,Cong Huang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IntentVLA 通过引入“短时意图编码”来处理视频动作模仿中的歧义问题，解决了传统 VLA 模型在处理多模态数据时导致的意图抖动和执行不稳问题，并在多个基准测试中展现了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习数据通常具有多模态性（相同观测下对应不同动作），现有的帧条件（frame-conditioned）VLA 策略仅基于当前帧进行预测，在存在观测歧义或部分可观测时，容易导致相邻步骤间意图跳转，造成动作冲突和执行不稳定。

Method: 1. 提出了 IntentVLA 框架：一种历史条件化的视觉语言动作（VLA）模型，利用编码器将近期视觉观测转化为紧凑的短时意图表示，并据此生成动作块。
2. 引入了 AliasBench 基准：一个包含 12 项任务的、专门针对短时视觉歧义设计的评估基准。

Result: 在 AliasBench、SimplerEnv、LIBERO 和 RoboCasa 等多个测试平台上，IntentVLA 表现出更强的执行稳定性，并优于现有的强 VLA 基线模型。

Conclusion: IntentVLA 通过引入历史条件化和意图编码机制，有效解决了多模态模仿学习中的意图歧义问题，显著提升了机器人策略执行的时间一致性和稳定性。

Abstract: Robot imitation data are often multimodal: similar visual-language observations may be followed by different action chunks because human demonstrators act with different short-horizon intents, task phases, or recent context. Existing frame-conditioned VLA policies infer each chunk from the current observation and instruction alone, so under partial observability they may resample different intents across adjacent replanning steps, leading to inter-chunk conflict and unstable execution. We introduce IntentVLA, a history-conditioned VLA framework that encodes recent visual observations into a compact short-horizon intent representation and uses it to condition chunk generation. We further introduce AliasBench, a 12-task ambiguity-aware benchmark on RoboTwin2 with matched training data and evaluation environments that isolate short-horizon observation aliasing. Across AliasBench, SimplerEnv, LIBERO, and RoboCasa, IntentVLA improves rollout stability and outperforms strong VLA baselines

</details>


### [273] [Exploring Bottlenecks in VLM-LLM Navigation: How 3D Scene Understanding Capability Impacts Zero-Shot VLN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14801)
*Ziyi Xia,Chaoran Xiong,Litao Wei,Xinhao Hu,Ling Pei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文揭示了零样本VLN任务中存在“感知饱和”现象，指出盲目追求像素级精度的3D场景理解模型并不利于提升导航效率，提出应优先优化导航相关语义和边界框准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析并弥补现有的VLN系统瓶颈：现有的3D感知模型侧重于像素级精度，这与具身导航对计算效率和实时性的严苛要求相冲突。

Method: 量化3D场景理解能力对VLN性能的影响；基于主流VLM-LLM框架，推导LLM规划器（语义拓扑映射）和反应式导航器（空间坐标与边界框）的统计成功率（SR）上限；利用现代3D感知模型进行验证。

Result: 验证了所提出的性能上限理论；发现了“感知饱和”现象，即感知精度超过一定阈值后，对导航成功率的提升效果变得极不明显。

Conclusion: 针对VLN的3D场景理解研究应当从追求像素级精度转向关注导航相关的核心词汇库构建及精确的边界框比例，过度的感知精度提升对于导航性能的边际效用递减。

Abstract: Zero-shot vision-and-language navigation (VLN) has gained significant attention due to its minimal data collection costs and inherent generalization. This paradigm is typically driven by the integration of pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and Large Language Models (LLMs), where VLMs construct 3D scene graphs while LLMs handle high-level reasoning and decision-making. However, a critical bottleneck exists in this system: current 3D perception models prioritize pixel-level accuracy, directly conflicting with the strict computational limits and real-time efficiency demanded by embodied navigation. To address this gap, this paper quantifies the actual impact of 3D scene understanding capability on VLN performance. Based on typical VLM-LLM frameworks, we propose statistical success rate (SR) upper bounds for two core subsystems: 1) the slow LLM planner, which relies on topological mapping semantics, and 2) the fast reactive navigator, which utilizes spatial coordinates and bounding boxes to execute LLM decisions. Evaluations using state-of-the-art 3D scene understanding models validate our proposed bounds and reveal a perception saturation phenomenon, indicating that improvements in perception accuracy beyond a certain threshold yield diminishing returns in navigation success. Our findings suggest that 3D scene understanding for VLN should pivot away from strict pixel-level precision, prioritizing instead navigation-relevant core vocabularies and accurate bounding box proportions.

</details>


### [274] [Learning Cross-Coupled and Regime Dependent Dynamics for Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14805)
*Rishabh Dev Yadav,Samaksh Ujjawal,Sihao Sun,Spandan Roy,Wei Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对航空机械臂动态建模困难（强耦合、非平稳）的问题，本文提出一种结构化编码器-解码器框架，通过在线贝叶斯自适应实现对残差动态的高效学习与实时补偿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空机械臂存在强耦合、气动延迟及负载/构型变化带来的非平稳动态特性，导致传统的解析模型与纯离线学习模型在部署时因残差动态特性复杂而表现退化。

Method: 提出了一种结构化的编码器-解码器框架：(1) 非线性潜空间编码器（Latent Encoder）捕捉状态-输入历史中的跨变量耦合与时序依赖；(2) 轻量级线性潜空间解码器（Latent Decoder）支持基于贝叶斯推断的实时在线适应与一致性驱动的协方差膨胀（Covariance Inflation）。

Result: 实物平台实验表明，该方法显著提升了残差预测准确度，在负载变化等动态环境下实现了更快的自适应速度，并有效提升了MPC控制下的轨迹跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 对于航空机械臂而言，联合建模耦合时序动态特性与部署阶段的非平稳性对于实现可靠的操控至关重要；该架构在保持高预测精度的同时，具备了极强的适应性与实时控制兼容性。

Abstract: Accurate dynamics models are critical for aerial manipulators operating under complex tasks such as payload transport. However, modeling these systems remains fundamentally challenging due to strong quadrotor-manipulator coupling, delayed aerodynamic interactions, and regime-dependent dynamics variations arising from payload changes and manipulator reconfiguration. These effects produce residual dynamics that are simultaneously cross-coupled, history-dependent, and nonstationary, causing both analytical models and purely offline learned models to degrade during deployment. To address these challenges, we propose a structured encoder-decoder framework for adaptive residual dynamics learning in aerial manipulators. The proposed nonlinear latent encoder captures cross-variable coupling and temporal dependencies from state-input histories, while a lightweight linear latent decoder enables online adaptation under regime-dependent nonstationary dynamics. The linear-in-parameter decoder structure permits closed-form Bayesian adaptation together with consistency-driven covariance inflation, enabling rapid and stable adaptation to both transient and slowly varying dynamics changes while remaining compatible with real-time model predictive control (MPC). Experimental results on a real aerial manipulation platform demonstrate improved residual prediction accuracy, faster adaptation under changing operating conditions, and enhanced MPC-based trajectory tracking performance. These results highlight the importance of jointly modeling coupled temporal dynamics and deployment-time nonstationarity for reliable aerial manipulation.

</details>


### [275] [CaMeRL: Collision-Aware and Memory-Enhanced Reinforcement Learning for UAV Navigation in Multi-Scale Obstacle Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14810)
*Hong Hong,Feiyu Liao,Yongheng Liang,Boning Zhang,Haitao Wang,Hejun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对无人机导航中多尺度障碍物导致的感知性能下降问题，本文提出了 CaMeRL 框架，通过引入碰撞感知表示与时间记忆机制，有效解决了细小障碍物漏检及大障碍物遮挡引起的导航失败挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机避障导航方法通常忽视了障碍物尺度变化的影响，且单纯依赖单帧几何特征提取，导致在多尺度障碍物环境下对细小障碍物敏感度低，且易受大障碍物遮挡造成的空间信息丢失影响。

Method: 提出了 CaMeRL 强化学习框架，包含两个核心模块：1）碰撞感知潜在表示（Collision-aware latent representation），通过编码风险敏感的深度线索来强化对细小障碍物的感知；2）时间记忆模块（Temporal memory module），通过跨帧融合观测信息，解决大障碍物遮挡导致的部分可观测性问题。

Result: 在包含超小和超大障碍物的多尺度实验中，CaMeRL 超过了现有最先进的基准方法，在超小和超大障碍物场景下的成功率分别提升了 0.48 和 0.28。此外，在复杂的户外杂乱环境中也实现了可靠导航。

Conclusion: CaMeRL 在处理复杂的多尺度障碍物环境（尤其是极小和极大障碍物）中表现出色，显著提升了无人机在避障导航任务中的成功率与鲁棒性。

Abstract: In obstacle avoidance navigation of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), variations in obstacle scale have received strangely less attention than obstacle number or density. Existing methods typically extract purely geometric features from single-frame depth observations. Such representations tend to neglect small obstacles and lose spatial context under occlusions caused by large obstacles, leading to noticeable degradation in environments with multi-scale obstacles. To address this issue, we propose CaMeRL, a Collision-aware and Memory-enhanced Reinforcement Learning framework for UAV navigation. The collision-aware latent representation encodes risk-sensitive depth cues to preserve fine-grained obstacle structures, thereby improving sensitivity to small obstacles. The temporal memory module integrates observations across frames, mitigating partial observability caused by large-obstacle occlusions. We evaluate CaMeRL with multi-scale obstacles, including ultra-small and extra-large obstacle settings. Results show that CaMeRL outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across all scales, with success rate gains of 0.48 and 0.28 in the ultra-small and extra-large settings, respectively. More importantly, CaMeRL achieves reliable navigation in cluttered outdoor environments.

</details>


### [276] [Learning Direct Control Policies with Flow Matching for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14832)
*Marcello Ceresini,Federico Pirazzoli,Andrea Bertogalli,Lorenzo Cipelli,Filippo D'Addeo,Anthony Dell'Eva,Alessandro Paolo Capasso,Alberto Broggi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于流匹配的自动驾驶规划器，通过BEV输入和ODE积分实现低延迟控制。该方法在多种新颖和分布外场景下展现了卓越的闭环稳健性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动驾驶规划方法在处理分布外（OOD）场景时往往表现不稳定，且计算复杂。研究旨在开发一种能够快速生成控制序列、且在面对未见过的新场景时具备强泛化能力与闭环稳定性的规划方案。

Method: 提出了一种流匹配（Flow-matching）规划器，能够直接输出加速度和曲率控制轨迹。模型以周围场景的鸟瞰图（BEV）光栅图为条件，通过少量ODE积分步骤生成控制序列，实现低延迟的闭环重规划。训练数据完全来源于城市场景（含真实街道、交叉路口、环岛），并在室内分布及室外分布（如多车道高速公路）场景中进行闭环测试。

Result: 实验表明，该模型能够可靠地泛化到与训练分布差异明显的场景中。其在未见过的城市场景和高速公路场景下均能保持稳定的闭环控制，成功完成任务。这些优异表现归功于BEV表示对几何特征的关注以及流匹配公式生成平滑向量场的特性。

Conclusion: 基于BEV表征的流匹配规划器具备强大的泛化能力。其生成的平滑向量场在分布外（OOD）场景下表现出稳健的闭环控制性能，证明了该方法在自动驾驶避障与避障规划任务中的有效性。

Abstract: We present a flow-matching planner for autonomous driving that directly outputs actionable control trajectories defined by acceleration and curvature profiles. The model is conditioned on a bird's-eye-view (BEV) raster of the surrounding scene and generates control sequences in a small number of Ordinary Differential Equations (ODE) integration steps, enabling low-latency inference suitable for real-time closed-loop re-planning. We train exclusively on urban scenarios (real urban city streets, intersections and roundabouts of the city of Parma, Italy) collected from a 2D traffic simulator with reactive agents, and evaluate in closed-loop on both in-distribution and markedly out-of-distribution environments, including multi-lane highways and unseen urban scenarios. Our results show that the model generalizes reliably to these unseen conditions, maintaining stable closed-loop control and successfully completing scenarios that differ substantially from the training distribution. We attribute this to the BEV representation, which provides a geometry-centric view of the scene that is inherently less sensitive to distributional shifts, and to the flow-matching formulation, which learns a smooth vector field that degrades gracefully under distribution shift. We provide video demonstrations of closed-loop behavior at https://marcelloceresini.github.io/DirectControlFlowMatching.

</details>


### [277] [Chrono-Gymnasium: An Open-Source, Gymnasium-Compatible Distributed Simulation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14911)
*Bocheng Zou,Harry Zhang,Khailanii Slaton,Jingquan Wang,Derrick Ruan,Huzaifa Mustafa Unjhawala,Radu Serban,Dan Negrut*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Chrono-Gymnasium 是一个分布式计算框架，旨在解决高保真物理仿真在强化学习和参数优化中的计算瓶颈，实现高效、准确的机器人系统开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真物理仿真计算开销巨大，难以满足强化学习 (RL) 和全局优化对大规模数据采样的需求，限制了其在真实机器人系统开发中的应用。

Method: 采用基于 Ray 的分布式计算架构，将 Project Chrono 的高保真多体动力学引擎封装为标准化的 Gymnasium 接口，支持大规模集群并行仿真，并内置了同步与消息传递机制。

Result: 在机器人自主导航（RL）和行星着陆器参数优化（贝叶斯优化）两个案例中，显著降低了仿真运行时间（实现高效加速），同时保持了高保真物理模拟的准确性。

Conclusion: Chrono-Gymnasium 为高保真物理仿真与大规模机器学习（RL/全局优化）之间架起了桥梁，证明了利用分布式计算能够显著降低高保真仿真在复杂设计与控制任务中的计算开销。

Abstract: High-fidelity physics simulation is essential for closing the sim-to-real gap in robotics and complex mechanical systems. However, the computational overhead of high-fidelity engines often limits their use in data-intensive tasks like Reinforcement Learning (RL) and global optimization. We introduce Chrono-Gymnasium, a distributed computing framework that scales the high-fidelity multi-body dynamics of Project Chrono across large-scale computing clusters. Built upon the Ray framework, Chrono-Gymnasium provides a standardized Gymnasium interface, enabling seamless integration with modern machine learning libraries while providing built-in synchronization and messaging primitives for distributed execution. We demonstrate the framework's capabilities through two distinct case studies: (1) the training of an RL agent for autonomous robotic navigation in complex terrains, and (2) the Bayesian Optimization of a planetary lander's design parameters to ensure landing stability. Our results show that Chrono-Gymnasium reduces wall-clock time for high-fidelity simulations without sacrificing physical accuracy, offering a scalable path for the design and control of complex robotic systems.

</details>


### [278] [FU-MPC: Frontier- and Uncertainty-Aware Model Predictive Control for Efficient and Accurate UAV Exploration with Motorized LiDAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14920)
*Jianping Li,Pengfei Wan,Zhongyuan Liu,Yi Wang,Yiheng Chen,Xinhang Xu,Rui Jin,Boyu Zhou,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对无人机探索过程中视场与运动耦合导致的效率低、定位不稳问题，本文提出了一种搭载主动旋转LiDAR的系统，通过FU-MPC框架联合优化飞行路径与感知方向，实现更高效、鲁棒的自主探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定式LiDAR的无人机在探索时，传感视场与飞行运动高度耦合，提高探索覆盖率往往需要额外的移动，这不仅降低了效率，还增加了在复杂场景下定位失效的风险。现有研究缺乏对LiDAR扫描自由度作为探索决策变量的利用。

Method: 提出了一种分层探索框架：包括一个基于拓扑感知转化成本的全局规划器，以及一个名为FU-MPC的局部后退视轴控制框架，该控制器能够联合优化UAV的飞行轨迹与LiDAR的旋转角度，并引入轻量级替代模型以实现机载实时计算。

Result: 在复杂环境中进行了实验验证，结果表明该系统在提高探索效率的同时，能够显著增强定位性能，成功解决了探索进度与定位不确定性之间的权衡问题。

Conclusion: 通过引入可独立旋转的激光雷达（LiDAR）并结合FU-MPC框架，该系统有效解耦了UAV的运动与感知视场，在复杂环境下的探索效率和定位鲁棒性方面均优于现有基准方法。

Abstract: Efficient UAV exploration in unknown environments requires rapid coverage expansion while maintaining accurate and reliable localization, since safe navigation in complex scenes depends on consistent mapping and pose estimation. However, for conventional LiDAR-equipped UAVs, the observable region is tightly coupled with the UAV pose and motion. Expanding coverage often requires additional translational or rotational maneuvers, which can reduce exploration efficiency and increase the risk of localization degradation in geometrically challenging environments. Motorized rotating LiDARs provide a promising solution by actively adjusting the sensor viewing direction without changing the UAV motion, thereby introducing an additional sensing degree of freedom. Nevertheless, existing exploration systems rarely exploit this scanning freedom as an explicit decision variable linked to both exploration progress and localization quality. To address this gap, we develop a UAV platform equipped with an independently actuated rotating LiDAR and propose a hierarchical exploration framework. The global planner organizes frontiers into representative viewpoints and sequences them using topology-aware transition costs. Built upon this planner, FU-MPC serves as a local receding-horizon scan controller that optimizes LiDAR rotation along the predicted flight trajectory. The controller jointly considers frontier-aware exploration utility and direction-dependent localization uncertainty, while lightweight surrogate evaluation enables real-time onboard execution. Experiments in complex environments demonstrate that the proposed system improves exploration efficiency while maintaining robust localization performance compared with fixed-pattern scanning and uncertainty-only baselines. The project page can be found at https://kafeiyin00.github.io/FU-MPC/.

</details>


### [279] [Behavioral Data-Driven Optimal Trajectory Generation for Rotary Cranes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14944)
*Iskandar Khemakhem,Manuel Zobel,Johannes Schüle,Oliver Sawodny,Naoki Uchiyama,Abdallah Farrage*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于行为数据驱动的非参数化轨迹生成框架，仅利用少量的输入输出数据及凸优化，即可在旋转吊车系统中实现比传统模型控制更高效、更平稳的负载减振控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对传统模型控制依赖精确建模和专家调参的局限性，以及现有学习方法对海量数据和算力的依赖，旨在解决建筑起重机自动化中负载振荡控制、安全性和运行效率之间的矛盾。

Method: 基于Willems基本引理的非参数化行为数据驱动框架，通过测量输入输出数据构建轨迹，并结合凸优化技术生成平滑、最优的开环回转轨迹。

Result: 在实验室起重机平台上进行了验证，较传统模型方法，负载摇摆减少了35%，跟踪误差降低了43%，运行时间缩短了50%。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了基于行为数据驱动的轨迹生成在旋转吊车控制中具有显著优势，能够有效摆脱对高精度物理建模的依赖，在保证低数据需求的同时，实现了更优的摇摆抑制和效率提升。

Abstract: With the growth of the construction industry and the global shortage of skilled labor, the automation of crane control has become increasingly important for safe and efficient operations. A central challenge in automatic crane control is the reduction of load oscillations during motion, which is primarily addressed through appropriate slewing trajectories. In this context, classical model-based control methods rely on accurate dynamical models and expert tuning, and often struggle to meet safety and precision requirements, while many learning-based approaches require large data sets and significant computational resources. This paper proposes a behavioral data-driven framework for generating open-loop slewing trajectories for rotary cranes that suppress load sway while reducing operation time and energy consumption. The approach builds on Willems' fundamental lemma and its generalizations, to bypass explicit system modeling and operate directly on measured input-output data. A practical workflow is presented in this paper to reduce the need for expert knowledge. Despite the underactuated nature of the crane dynamics, the method identifies a nonparametric representation of the system behavior and generates smooth, optimal trajectories using limited data and convex optimization. The proposed trajectory generation method is validated on a laboratory crane setup and compared against an established model-based approach, achieving up to 35% reduction in load sway, 43% reduction in tracking error, and 50% reduction in travel time.

</details>


### [280] [SOCC-ICP: Semantics-Assisted Odometry based on Occupancy Grids and ICP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15074)
*Johannes Scherer,Sebastian Hirt,Henri Meeß*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SOCC-ICP 是一种将语义占用网格地图与 LiDAR 里程计深度融合的框架，通过统一地图表示，消除了冗余并直接支持下游机器人任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LiDAR 里程计方法（点/面/NDT）与下游任务常用的语义占用网格不兼容，导致地图结构冗余且效率低下。

Method: 提出 SOCC-ICP 框架，采用语义占用网格（Semantic Occupancy Grid）作为统一的地图表示；基于局部平坦度实现自适应点对点或点对面 ICP；通过射线投射（raycasting）实现动态物体过滤。

Result: 在多种场景下达到与顶尖 LiDAR 里程计持平的表现；在几何退化环境中表现鲁棒；利用语义标签可进一步提高精度。

Conclusion: SOCC-ICP 通过统一里程计与语义占用网格地图，不仅消除了冗余的地图结构，还直接为下游任务提供了适配的地图，在保证鲁棒性的同时提升了地图的实用性。

Abstract: Reliable pose estimation in previously unseen environments is a fundamental capability of autonomous systems. Existing LiDAR odometry methods typically employ point-, surfel-, or NDT-based map representations, which are distinct from the semantic occupancy grids commonly used for downstream tasks such as motion planning. We introduce SOCC-ICP, a semantics-assisted odometry framework that jointly performs Semantic OCCupancy grid mapping and LiDAR scan alignment. Each map voxel encodes geometric and semantic statistics, enabling adaptive point-to-point or point-to-plane ICP based on local planarity. Further, the occupancy grid naturally filters dynamic objects through raycasting-based free-space updates. Across diverse evaluation scenarios, SOCC-ICP achieves performance competitive with state-of-the-art LiDAR odometry and remains robust in geometrically degenerate environments, even in the absence of semantic cues. When semantic labels are available, integrating them into map construction, downsampling, and correspondence weighting yields further accuracy gains. By unifying odometry and semantic occupancy grid mapping within a single representation, SOCC-ICP eliminates redundant map structures and directly provides a map suitable for downstream robotic applications.

</details>


### [281] [CLOVER: Closed-Loop Value Estimation \& Ranking for End-to-End Autonomous Driving Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15120)
*Sining Ang,Yuguang Yang,Canyu Chen,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 CLOVER 框架，通过引入闭环价值估计与排序机制，解决了自动驾驶规划中训练目标与评估指标失调的问题，并利用生成器-打分器联合优化实现了自动驾驶规划性能的新突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端规划方法大多基于单轨迹模仿学习，但在评估时使用规则指标，导致训练目标与评价标准不匹配，限制了模型识别高分、可行规划路径的能力。

Method: 提出 CLOVER 框架，采用“生成器-打分器”架构：使用生成器输出多样化轨迹，通过评估器过滤生成伪专家数据；训练过程中执行闭环自蒸馏（Self-distillation），基于评估器指标优化打分器，并利用 Pareto 前沿目标对生成器进行保守的精炼与正则化。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 数据集达到 94.5 PDMS 和 90.4 EPDMS（SOTA）；在 NavHard 困难拆分集上达到 48.3 EPDMS，性能处于领先水平；在 nuScenes 开环测试中表现出最低的 L2 误差和碰撞率。

Conclusion: CLOVER 通过闭环价值估计与排序框架，有效解决了端到端自动驾驶中模仿学习与规则指标之间的训练-评估不匹配问题，在大规模测试集上显著提升了规划性能，树立了新的SOTA。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving planners are commonly trained by imitating a single logged trajectory, yet evaluated by rule-based planning metrics that measure safety, feasibility, progress, and comfort. This creates a training--evaluation mismatch: trajectories close to the logged path may violate planning rules, while alternatives farther from the demonstration can remain valid and high-scoring. The mismatch is especially limiting for proposal-selection planners, whose performance depends on candidate-set coverage and scorer ranking quality. We propose CLOVER, a Closed-LOop Value Estimation and Ranking framework for end-to-end autonomous driving planning. CLOVER follows a lightweight generator--scorer formulation: a generator produces diverse candidate trajectories, and a scorer predicts planning-metric sub-scores to rank them at inference time. To expand proposal support beyond single-trajectory imitation, CLOVER constructs evaluator-filtered pseudo-expert trajectories and trains the generator with set-level coverage supervision. It then performs conservative closed-loop self-distillation: the scorer is fitted to true evaluator sub-scores on generated proposals, while the generator is refined toward teacher-selected top-$k$ and vector-Pareto targets with stability regularization. We analyze when an imperfect scorer can improve the generator, showing that scorer-mediated refinement is reliable when scorer-selected targets are enriched under the true evaluator and updates remain conservative. On NAVSIM, CLOVER achieves 94.5 PDMS and 90.4 EPDMS, establishing a new state of the art. On the more challenging NavHard split, it obtains 48.3 EPDMS, matching the strongest reported result. On supplementary nuScenes open-loop evaluation, CLOVER achieves the lowest L2 error and collision rate among compared methods. Code data will be released at https://github.com/WilliamXuanYu/CLOVER.

</details>


### [282] [Pelican-Unified 1.0: A Unified Embodied Intelligence Model for Understanding, Reasoning, Imagination and Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15153)
*Yi Zhang,Yinda Chen,Che Liu,Zeyuan Ding,Jin Xu,Shilong Zou,Junwei Liao,Jiayu Hu,Xiancong Ren,Xiaopeng Zhang,Yechi Liu,Haoyuan Shi,Zecong Tang,Haosong Sun,Renwen Cui,Kuishu Wu,Wenhai Liu,Yang Xu,Yingji Zhang,Yidong Wang,Senkang Hu,Jinpeng Lu,Nga Teng Chan,Yechen Wu,Yong Dai,Jian Tang,Xiaozhu Ju*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Pelican-Unified 1.0 是首个采用“统一化原则”的具身基础模型，通过单一模型架构整合了视觉理解、逻辑推理、未来场景预演与动作决策能力，并在多个权威基准测试中展现出媲美甚至超越专用模型的强大实力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前具身智能领域普遍存在的理解、推理、想象和动作模块相互孤立的问题，旨在克服多模型协作带来的复杂性与系统割裂感，实现一套具备通用能力的统一化具身模型。

Method: 提出了基于“统一化原则”的具身基础模型：使用单个 VLM 作为统一理解与推理模块，通过单一前向传播生成思维链与潜变量；引入统一未来生成器 (UFG)，在同一个去噪过程中联合生成未来视频与动作；采用多模态联合优化策略，将语言、视频和动作损失通过反向传播同时优化共享表征。

Result: 在三个主要任务上表现优异：VLM 基准测试 (64.7) 表现领先；WorldArena 基准 (66.03) 排名第一；RoboTwin 动作执行任务 (93.5) 达到第二名，验证了统一化架构在多任务上的高性能表现。

Conclusion: Pelican-Unified 1.0 证明了“统一范式”在具身智能领域的有效性，能够在不损失专家系统性能的前提下，通过单一模型实现视觉理解、逻辑推理、未来预测及动作执行的综合能力。

Abstract: We present Pelican-Unified 1.0, the first embodied foundation model trained according to the principle of unification. Pelican-Unified 1.0 uses a single VLM as a unified understanding module, mapping scenes, instructions, visual contexts, and action histories into a shared semantic space. The same VLM also serves as a unified reasoning module, autoregressively producing task-, action-, and future-oriented chains of thought in a single forward pass and projecting the final hidden state into a dense latent variable. A Unified Future Generator (UFG) then conditions on this latent variable and jointly generates future videos and future actions through two modality-specific output heads within the same denoising process. The language, video, and action losses are all backpropagated into the shared representation, enabling the model to jointly optimize understanding, reasoning, imagination, and action during training, rather than training three isolated expert systems.
  Experiments demonstrate that unification does not imply compromise. With a single checkpoint, Pelican-Unified 1.0 achieves strong performance across all three capabilities: 64.7 on eight VLM benchmarks, the best among comparable-scale models; 66.03 on WorldArena, ranking first; and 93.5 on RoboTwin, the second-best average among compared action methods. These results show that the unified paradigm succeeds in preserving specialist strength while bringing understanding, reasoning, imagination, and action into one model.

</details>


### [283] [Hand-in-the-Loop: Improving Dexterous VLA via Seamless Interventional Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15157)
*Zhuohang Li,Liqun Huang,Wei Xu,Zhengming Zhu,Nie Lin,Xiao Ma,Xinjun Sheng,Ruoshi Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对灵巧手操作中人工干预导致的动作抖动问题，HandITL通过融合人机指令平滑接管过程，大幅提升了操作成功率及策略微调效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在多指灵巧手操作中存在累积误差，而传统人工干预（IIL）在接管瞬间常因控制逻辑差异产生“姿态跳变”，破坏操作连贯性。

Method: 提出HandITL（Hand-in-the-Loop）框架，通过在该时刻将人类的修正意图与自动策略进行平滑融合，而非强制完全接管，从而避免了策略切换时产生的动作抖动（Gesture Jumps）。

Result: 相比直接远程接管，HandITL减少了99.8%的接管抖动，抓取失败率降低87.5%，平均完成时间缩短19.1%；利用其数据微调后的策略在长跨度任务上表现提升了19%。

Conclusion: HandITL方法通过平滑人体干预与自动策略的过渡，消除了由于控制指令不匹配导致的“姿态跳变”问题，显著提升了高自由度机器人灵巧手的操作鲁棒性和学习效率，为长跨度灵巧操作提供了一种更有效的交互式模仿学习路径。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are prone to compounding errors in dexterous manipulation, where high-dimensional action spaces and contact-rich dynamics amplify small policy deviations over long horizons. While Interactive Imitation Learning (IIL) can refine policies through human takeover data, applying it to high-degree-of-freedom (DoF) robotic hands remains challenging due to a command mismatch between human teleoperation and policy execution at the takeover moment, which causes abrupt robot-hand configuration changes, or "gesture jumps". We present Hand-in-the-Loop (HandITL), a seamless human-in-the-loop intervention method that blends human corrective intent with autonomous policy execution to avoid gesture jumps during bimanual dexterous manipulation. Compared with direct teleoperation takeover, HandITL reduces takeover jitter by 99.8% and preserves robust post-takeover manipulation, reducing grasp failures by 87.5% and mean completion time by 19.1%. We validate HandITL on tasks requiring bimanual coordination, tool use, and fine-grained long-horizon manipulation. When used to collect intervention data for policy refinement, HandITL yields policies that outperform those trained with standard teleoperation data by 19% on average across three long-horizon dexterous tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [284] [Thinking Ahead: Prospection-Guided Retrieval of Memory with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14177)
*Harshita Chopra,Krishna Kant Chintalapudi,Suman Nath,Ryen W. White,Chirag Shah*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对长程对话中个性化信息检索难题，本论文通过“前瞻（Prospection）”机制，将查询扩展为思维链来检索历史事实，显著提升了记忆召回准确率及对话个性化程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对长程对话中个性化事实检索的难题：传统的RAG和GraphRAG依赖于嵌入向量空间下的语义相似度，常导致隐藏在历史深处但在当前查询向量空间中距离较远的关键事实无法被召回，缺乏对未来需求预测的能力。

Method: 提出“前瞻引导检索（PGR）”方法：不直接用用户查询进行检索，而是利用思维树（ToT）或线性链进行“前线模拟”生成多个潜在步骤作为检索探针，并结合通过这些探针获取的事实进行迭代式的个性化回忆检索。同时构建了MemoryQuest基准测试集。

Result: 在MemoryQuest基准测试中，PGR-TOT的召回率比最强基线提高了近3倍；在1,625个查询的对比评估中，PGR生成的回复在89%-98%的情况下优于基线方案，得到了AI辅助评估及人类盲测的一致认可。

Conclusion: 基于“前瞻（Prospection）”的检索机制能够有效克服长程对话中语义相似度检索的局限，通过模拟未来路径作为检索探针，显著提升了记忆召回率及个性化回复生成的质量。

Abstract: Long-horizon personalization requires dialogue assistants to retrieve user-specific facts from extended interaction histories. In practice, many relevant facts often have low semanticsimilarity to the query under dense retrieval. Standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and GraphRAG systems are still largely retrospective: they rely on embedding similarity to the query or on fixed graph traversals, so they often miss facts that matter for the user's needs but lie far from the query in embedding space. Inspired by prospection, the human ability to use imagined futures as cues for recall, we introduce Prospection-Guided Retrieval (PGR), which decouples retrieval from how memories are stored. Given a user query, PGR first expands the goal into a short Tree-of-Thought (ToT) or linear chain of plausible next steps, and uses these steps as retrieval probes rather than relying on the original query alone. The facts retrieved by these probes are then used to personalize the next round of prospection, enabling PGR to uncover additional memories that become relevant only after the simulation is grounded in the user's history. We also introduce MemoryQuest, a challenging multi-session benchmark in which each query is annotated with 3--5 dated reference facts subject to a low query-reference similarity constraint. Across 1,625 queries spanning 185 user profiles from 3 publicly available datasets, PGR-TOT substantially improves retrieval, including nearly 3x recall on MemoryQuest over the strongest baseline. In pairwise LLM-as-judge comparisons against baselines, PGR-generated responses are preferred on 89--98% of queries, with blinded human annotations on held-out subsets showing the same trend. Overall, the results demonstrate that explicit prospection yields large gains in long-horizon retrieval and response quality relative to similarity-only baselines.

</details>


### [285] [Efficient Generative Retrieval for E-commerce Search with Semantic Cluster IDs and Expert-Guided RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14434)
*Jianbo Zhu,Xing Fang,Jing Wang,Mingmin Jin,Bokang Wang,Guangxin Song,Zhenyu Xie,Junjie Bai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于大规模电商系统的生成式检索框架CQ-SID与EG-GRPO，通过层级化语义聚类与策略优化，解决了生成式检索在工业界的部署难题，显著提升了在线搜索的召回与转化指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生成式检索在工业级电商场景面临商品规模巨大、动态更新频繁、延迟要求极高以及与排序目标不一致等挑战，缺乏在实际生产环境中的可行部署方案。

Method: 1. CQ-SID（分类与查询约束语义ID）：利用类别感知与查询-商品对比学习，配合残差量化VAE（RQ-VAE）生成层级化语义ID，优化束搜索效率；2. EG-GRPO（专家引导的组相对策略优化）：引入强化学习，结合专家样本监督，对齐生成式召回与下游排序目标。

Result: 离线实验显示点击命中率显著提升（达26.76%），在线A/B测试证明GMV与UCTCVR分别提升1.15%与0.40%，且在生产环境贡献了核心买单量。

Conclusion: CQ-SID与EG-GRPO证明了生成式检索在工业级大规模电商搜索场景中的有效性，通过将其定位为召回阶段的补充而非全链路替代，成功实现了GMV与转化率的显著提升。

Abstract: Generative retrieval offers a promising alternative by unifying the fragmented multi-stage retrieval process into a single end-to-end model. However, its practical adoption in industrial e-commerce search remains challenging, given the massive and dynamic product catalogs, strict latency requirements, and the need to align retrieval with downstream ranking goals. In this work, we propose a retrieval framework tailored for real-world recall scenarios, positioning generative retrieval as a recall-stage supplement rather than an end-to-end replacement. Our method, CQ-SID (Category-and-Query constrained Semantic ID), employs category-aware and query-item contrastive learning along with Residual Quantized VAEs to encode items into hierarchical semantic cluster identifiers, significantly reducing beam search complexity. Additionally, we develop EG-GRPO (Expert-Guided Group Relative Policy Optimization), a reinforcement learning approach that aligns generative recall with downstream ranking under sparse rewards by injecting ground-truth samples to stabilize training. Offline experiments on TmallAPP search logs show that CQ-SID achieves up to 26.76% and 11.11% relative gains in semantic and personalized click hitrate over RQ-VAE baselines, while halving beam search size. EG-GRPO further improves multi-objective performance. Online A/B tests confirm gains in GMV (+1.15%) and UCTCVR (+0.40%). The generative recall channel now contributes substantially in production, accounting for over 50.25% of exposures, 58.96% of clicks, and 72.63% of purchases, demonstrating a viable path for deploying generative retrieval in real-world e-commerce systems.

</details>


### [286] [Stop Overthinking: Unlocking Efficient Listwise Reranking with Minimal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14450)
*Danyang Liu,Kan Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM重排序任务中CoT产生冗长推理带来的高成本问题，提出了一种长度正则化自蒸馏框架，通过训练模型生成更精简高效的推理链，显著提升了推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于思维链（CoT）的LLM重排序方法虽然效果好，但存在推理过程冗长导致计算成本过高的问题；研究发现推理长度与排序质量之间存在“过度思考（overthinking）”现象，即过长推理带来的收益递减。

Method: 提出了“长度正则化自蒸馏（Length-Regularized Self-Distillation）”框架：通过教师模型（Rank-K）采样多样化推理路径，结合帕累托优化（Pareto-inspired filter）筛选出兼顾高排序性能与低推理令牌消耗的轨迹，并以此微调学生模型以实现推理过程的精简。

Result: 在TREC Deep Learning和NeuCLIR基准测试中，该方法在保持模型重排序性能的前提下，将推理令牌消耗量降低了34%-37%。

Conclusion: 该研究通过Length-Regularized Self-Distillation框架证明了在LLM排序任务中，通过蒸馏精简的高质量推理链，可以在保留模型性能的同时大幅降低推理成本，为实际应用中部署推理增强型重排序器提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Listwise reranking utilizing Large Language Models (LLMs) has achieved state-of-the-art retrieval effectiveness. Recently, reasoning-enhanced models have further pushed these boundaries by employing Chain-of-Thought (CoT) to perform deep comparative analysis of candidate documents. However, this performance gain comes at a prohibitive computational cost, as models often generate thousands of reasoning tokens before producing a final ranking. In this work, we investigate the relationship between reasoning length and ranking quality, revealing an overthinking phenomenon where extended reasoning yields diminishing returns. To address this, we propose a Length-Regularized Self-Distillation framework. We synthesize a dataset by sampling diverse reasoning traces from a teacher model (Rank-K) and applying a Pareto-inspired filter to select traces that achieve high ranking performance with minimal token usage. By fine-tuning on these concise, high-quality rationales, the student model learns to internalize efficient reasoning patterns, effectively pruning redundant deliberation. Experiments on TREC Deep Learning and NeuCLIR benchmarks demonstrate that our method maintains the teacher's effectiveness while reducing inference token consumption by 34%-37% across different retrieval settings, offering a practical solution for deploying reasoning-enhanced rerankers in latency-sensitive applications.

</details>


### [287] [Asymmetric Generative Recommendation via Multi-Expert Projection and Multi-Faceted Hierarchical Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14512)
*Bin Huang,Xin Wang,Junwei Pan,Yongqi Zhou,Yifeng Zhou,Zhixiang Feng,Shudong Huang,Haijie Gu,Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对生成式推荐模型中的“输入-输出”信息瓶颈，提出了非对称框架AsymRec，通过解耦表示与优化量化策略，实现了推荐性能的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐模型（GenRec）使用统一的离散语义ID作为输入和输出，存在双重信息瓶颈：输入端量化损失导致语义丢失及流行度偏差，输出端离散目标限制了监督质量。

Method: 提出AsymRec框架：1. 多专家语义投影（MSP）：将连续嵌入映射至Transformer隐藏空间，保留语义丰富度；2. 多面分层量化（MHQ）：通过多视角、多层级的量化及语义正则化构建结构化离散目标，避免维度崩塌。

Result: 在多项实验中，AsymRec的表现均优于现有最优的生成式推荐模型，平均性能提升达15.8%。

Conclusion: AsymRec通过解耦输入与输出表示，有效解决了生成式推荐模型中的信息瓶颈问题，在保持Semantic ID架构优势的同时，显著提升了推荐性能。

Abstract: Generative Recommendation (GenRec) models reformulate recommendation as a sequence generation task, representing items as discrete Semantic IDs used symmetrically as both inputs and prediction targets. We identify a critical dual-stage information bottleneck in this design: (1) the Input Bottleneck, where lossy quantization degrades fine-grained semantics, while popularity bias skews the learned representations toward frequent items, and (2) the Output Bottleneck, where imprecise discrete targets limit supervision quality. To address these issues, we propose AsymRec, an asymmetric continuous-discrete framework that decouples input and output representations. Specifically, Multi-expert Semantic Projection (MSP) maps continuous embeddings into the Transformer's hidden space via expert-specialized projections, preserving semantic richness and improving generalization to infrequent items. Multi-faceted Hierarchical Quantization (MHQ) constructs high-capacity, structured discrete targets through multi-view and multi-level quantization with semantic regularization, preventing dimensional collapse while retaining fine-grained distinctions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AsymRec consistently outperforms state-of-the-art generative recommenders by an average of 15.8 %. The code will be released.

</details>


### [288] [Discrimination Is Generation: Unifying Ranking and Retrieval from a Tokenizer Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14853)
*Shuli Wang,Junwei Yin,Changhao Li,Senjie Kou,Chi Wang,Yinqiu Huang,Yinhua Zhu,Haitao Wang,Xingxing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了DIG框架，通过将分词器纳入判别式排序模型的端到端训练过程，将用户-物品交叉特征纳入语义ID构造，有效解决了生成式推荐中个性化信号不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语义ID（SID）分词器与推荐排序目标解耦，导致生成式检索性能长期弱于判别式排序。作者认为排序和检索本质上是在不同粒度下解决同一个寻找“argmax”的问题。

Method: 提出DIG（Discrimination Is Generation）框架，将分词器嵌入判别式排序模型中进行端到端联合训练；利用特征分配分类法（Feature Assignment Taxonomy），将物品静态特征编码至语义ID（SID），用户-物品交叉特征（u2i）引导码本边界，并引入MLP_u2t蒸馏模块以在推理阶段逼近u2i特征。

Result: 在三个公开基准测试和两个工业数据集上，DIG能够同时提升排序、检索以及统一检索-排序任务的性能。

Conclusion: DIG通过将判别式排序模型与分词器端到端联合训练，成功弥合了生成式模型与判别式模型之间的性能鸿沟，实现了排序与检索任务的同步最优。

Abstract: Semantic IDs (SIDs) define the generation space of generative recommendation and directly determine its personalization ceiling. However, existing tokenizers are trained independently with retrieval objectives, leaving personalization signals fully decoupled from the SID construction process -- a fundamental gap that causes generative retrieval to persistently lag behind discriminative ranking. In this paper, we rethink the essence of SIDs: \emph{ranking seeks argmax in item space while retrieval seeks argmax in token space; both are the same problem solved at different granularities.} Based on this insight, we propose \DIG (\textbf{D}iscrimination \textbf{I}s \textbf{G}eneration), which embeds the tokenizer inside a discriminative ranking model for end-to-end training -- the ranker naturally becomes a retrieval model, yielding two models from a single training run. \DIG is organized around a \emph{feature assignment taxonomy}: item-intrinsic static features are encoded into SIDs, user-item cross features (u2i) implicitly drive codebook boundaries toward recommendation decision boundaries during training, and an MLP$_\mathrm{u2t}$ distillation module approximates u2i at the token level for inference. Experiments on three public benchmarks and two industrial datasets demonstrate that \DIG simultaneously improves ranking, retrieval, and unified retrieval-ranking quality.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [289] [Clustering with Locally Bounded Ignorance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13917)
*Jaroslav Garvardt,Christian Komusiewicz*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文研究了相关聚类问题的参数化复杂度。通过将“模糊边图”的结构参数（简并度和闭包度）加入参数集，作者成功证明了该问题在这些参数下存在多项式核，突破了通用情况下的核化限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相关聚类（Correlation Clustering）在一般情况下承认 FPT 算法，但不存在多项式核。因此，研究人员试图通过限制图中权重为0的“模糊边图”的结构因素，寻找是否能使该问题变得“更简单”（即拥有多项式核）。

Method: 利用参数化复杂度分析（Parameterized Complexity Analysis），特别是针对相关聚类问题中权重为0的“模糊边图”引入新的参数（如简并度 d 和闭包度 c），并进行核化分析（Kernelization）与复杂度下界证明。

Result: 证明了相关聚类问题在参数 k+d 和 k+c 下拥有多项式核；同时，证明了即使在模糊边图结构受到严格限制的情况下，该问题在某些其他设置下仍具有计算复杂性。

Conclusion: 通过限制“模糊边图”（权重为0的边组成的图）的结构，可以获得相关聚类问题的多项式核。具体而言，当参数化为 k+d（简并度）或 k+c（闭包度）时，该问题是固定参数可追踪（FPT）且拥有多项式核。

Abstract: In Correlation Clustering, the input is a graph $G=(V,E)$ with weight function $ω: {V \choose 2}\to Z$
  and the task is to partition the vertex set into clusters such that
  the total weight of edges between clusters and missing edges
  inside clusters is minimized. Due to close connections
  between Correlation Clustering and Edge Multicut,
  deciding whether there is a partition with total cost at most $k$ is
  FPT with respect to $k$ but a polynomial kernel is presumably
  impossible. We study the influence of the structure of the fuzzy
  edge graph, that is, the graph induced by the weight-0 edges, on the
  problem complexity. We show in particular that Correlation
  Clustering admits a polynomial problem kernel when parameterized
  by $k+d$, where $d$ is the degeneracy of the fuzzy edge graph, and when
  parameterized by $k+c$, where $c$ is the closure of the fuzzy edge
  graph. We complement these positive results by showing hardness for
  several settings where the graph induced by the edges and nonedges has very restricted structure.

</details>


### [290] [Non-Redundancy of Low-Arity Symmetric Boolean CSPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14007)
*Amatya Sharma,Santhoshini Velusamy*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了对称布尔CSP的非冗余度（NRD），对arity $\le 5$ 的谓词进行了近乎完整的渐近分类，并引入“t-平衡性”理论成功界定了NRD的增长量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非冗余度（Non-redundancy）是衡量CSP实例复杂程度的核心结构参数，但其在特定约束类（如对称布尔谓词）下的渐近增长行为尚不清楚。作者旨在通过数学工具和计算方法，系统性地揭示对称布尔CSP中NRD的复杂性分布。

Method: 1. 理论分析：引入并证明了“t-平衡性（t-balancedness）”与多项式表示的等价性，用于导出NRD的上限（$O(n^t)$）；2. 下限构建：利用Carbonnel（2022）的框架，通过从k-ary OR的归约来确定下限（$\Omega(n^k)$）；3. 计算实验：通过计算方法辅助确定arity为4及部分5的谓词分类。

Result: 1. 成功解析了所有arity不大于4的对称布尔谓词，以及arity为5的绝大多数谓词（仅余两个未完全确定）；2. 证明了t-平衡性是判定NRD为 $O(n^t)$ 的充分条件；3. 将剩余两个arity为5的谓词问题归约为极值集合系统问题。

Conclusion: 本文对arity不大于5的对称布尔约束满足问题（CSP）的非冗余度（NRD）进行了近乎完整的渐近增长分类，并引入了‘t-平衡性’这一理论工具来分析高阶多项式表示与NRD上限之间的关系，为理解CSP的结构复杂性提供了重要依据。

Abstract: Non-redundancy, introduced by Bessiere, Carbonnel, and Katsirelos (AAAI 2020), is a structural parameter for Constraint Satisfaction Problems ($\mathsf{CSPs}$) that governs kernelization, exact and approximate sparsification, and exact streaming complexity. It is the largest size of a $\mathsf{CSP}$ instance admitting no smaller subinstance with the same satisfying assignments.
  We study non-redundancy $\mathsf{NRD}_n(R)$ for Boolean symmetric $\mathsf{CSPs}$ defined by an $r$-ary relation $R$ whose value depends only on Hamming weight. An instance of $\mathsf{CSP}(R)$ has $n$ variables and constraints given by $r$-tuples; a constraint is satisfied exactly when the induced tuple lies in $R$. This class includes natural predicates such as cuts and $k$-SAT clauses. Our main result is a near-complete classification of the asymptotic growth of $\mathsf{NRD}_n(R)$ for symmetric Boolean predicates of arity at most $5$. Using computational experiments and algebraic upper- and lower-bound criteria, we resolve every predicate of arity at most $4$ and all but two predicates of arity $5$.
  For upper bounds, we introduce $t$-balancedness, a lifted, higher-degree version of the balancedness notion of Chen, Jansen, and Pieterse (Algorithmica 2020). We prove that $t$-balancedness is equivalent to the existence of degree-$t$ multilinear polynomials capturing $R$, and hence implies $\mathsf{NRD}_n(R)=O(n^t)$. For lower bounds, we use Carbonnel's (CP 2022) framework: predicates admitting a special reduction from $k$-ary OR inherit OR's lower bound $Ω(n^k)$. The only unresolved arity-$5$ predicates in our framework have bounds $Ω(n^2)$ and $O(n^3)$; we reduce their exact classification to natural extremal set-system questions.

</details>


### [291] [Hybrid Sketching Methods for Dynamic Connectivity on Sparse Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15173)
*Quinten De Man,Gilvir Gill,Michael A. Bender,Laxman Dhulipala,David Tench*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 针对现有图草图算法在真实稀疏图中存储效率低的问题，本文提出了HybridSCALE混合存储方案，通过将高密度核心草图化、低密度外围无损化的组合，结合新型BalloonSketch技术，首次在多尺度图结构上实现了大幅空间优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动态连通性图草图算法（Linear Sketches）虽在理论上空间复杂度优于稠密图，但因单个顶点的草图开销巨大，导致其在实际应用中仅对极端稠密图有效，无法在稀疏的真实世界图中节省空间。

Method: 1. 混合存储策略：针对图的“密集核心”使用线性草图（Sketch），对“稀疏外围”采用精确存储；2. BalloonSketch：一种新型的l0-采样器，可将每个顶点的草图尺寸缩减至原来的1/8；3. HybridSCALE：一个模块化系统，用于整合上述两种存储方式。

Result: 相比于现有的无损存储基准，HybridSCALE在稀疏图上节省了高达15%的空间，在中等密度图上节省了高达92%的空间，在稠密图上节省了高达97%的空间。

Conclusion: HybridSCALE是首个在实际稀疏图和中等密度图上通过混合存储方案实现显著空间节省的动态连通性系统，验证了图草图理论在实际应用中的可行性。

Abstract: Dynamic connectivity is a fundamental dynamic graph problem, and recent algorithmic breakthroughs on dynamic graph sketching have reshaped what is theoretically possible: by encoding the graph as per-vertex linear sketches, these algorithms solve dynamic connectivity in only $Θ(V \log^2 V)$ space, independent of the number of edges,outperforming lossless $Θ(V+E)$-space structures that grow as the graph becomes denser. Prior to this work, no practical dynamic connectivity algorithm has been able to translate these theoretical breakthroughs into space savings on real-world graphs. The main obstacle is that per-vertex sketches cost thousands of bytes per vertex, so sketching only pays off once the graph becomes extremely dense.
  We observe that sparse real-world graphs are often not uniformly sparse, these graphs can contain dense cores on a small subset of vertices that account for a large fraction of edges. We exploit this structure via hybrid sketching: sketch only the dense core, and store the sparse periphery losslessly.
  We design new hybrid algorithms for fully-dynamic and semi-streaming connectivity with space $O(\min\{V+E, V \log V \log(2+E/V)\})$ w.h.p., simultaneously matching the lossless bound on sparse graphs, the sketching bound on dense graphs, and improving on both in an intermediate regime. A key component is BalloonSketch, a new l0-sampler reducing per-vertex sketch sizes by up to 8x. We implement HybridSCALE, a modular system treating the lossless and sketch-based components as subroutines. HybridSCALE is the first sketch-based dynamic connectivity system to save space on common real-world graphs. Compared to the state-of-the-art lossless baseline, HybridSCALE saves up to 15% space on sparse graphs (average degree < 100), up to 92% on intermediate density graphs (average degree ~ 100-1000), and up to 97% on dense graphs (average degree > 1000).

</details>


### [292] [Fast Leaf-to-Ancestor Minimum Query in the Oracle Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14112)
*Aleksey Upirvitskiy,Aleksandr Levin*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 针对有根加权树，该论文提出了一种新的静态数据结构，通过长路径分解和预处理比较，实现了 $O(1)$ 的快速响应叶子到祖先路径最小值查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在仅允许权重比较操作的预言机模型下，如何高效解决有根加权树上的叶子到祖先路径最小值查询（path-minimum queries）问题。

Method: 提出了一个结合四种技术的静态数据结构：(1) 将边权转化为节点权重并结合确定性平局处理以获得全序；(2) 长路径分解（Ladder Decomposition）；(3) 二进制提升（Binary Lifting）；(4) 基于梯形路径数组构建的稀疏表RMQ，并在预处理阶段通过预先确定的比较结果存储索引。

Result: 实现了 $O(n \log h)$ 的预处理时间、空间和预言机调用次数，并在查询时达到了 $O(1)$ 的最坏时间复杂度（且查询时无需进行额外的权重比较操作）。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种渐进最优的查询算法，证明了在确定性比较模型下，该预处理成本界限是紧的（tight）。

Abstract: We study leaf-to-ancestor path-minimum queries on a rooted, weighted tree in the oracle model, where the only allowed value operation is a comparison oracle on edge (or node) weights. We give a static data structure that, after O(n log h) preprocessing time, space, and oracle calls (where $n$ is the number of nodes and $h$ is the tree height), answers any leaf-to-ancestor query in O(1) worst-case time with zero oracle calls at query time. The method combines (I) an edge-to-node weight conversion with a deterministic tie-break to obtain a total order; (II) ladder (longest-path) decomposition; (III) binary lifting; and (IV) sparse-table RMQ built over ladder arrays, storing indices selected via the oracle during preprocessing. We also show that the preprocessing oracle-comparison bound is tight in the deterministic comparison model.

</details>


### [293] [zSort: Stable Distribution Sort using Z-Score Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14419)
*Hriday Jain,Ketan Sabale,Aditya Shastri,Hiren Kumar Thakkar,Ashutosh Londhe*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: zSort 是一种新型稳定排序算法，通过自适应分布排序技术打破了“性能与稳定性不可兼得”的定律，在保持稳定的前提下实现了媲美非稳定算法的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有排序算法存在“稳定性税”问题，即必须在保持相对顺序（稳定性）的高计算代价和追求高性能（但非稳定）之间做出取舍。

Method: 提出了一种名为 zSort 的自适应、基于 z-score 分布的排序算法，该算法规避了随键宽度线性增长的复杂性，并避免了传统稳定排序的“稳定性税”。

Result: 微架构分析显示 zSort 表现出更低的投机失败开销（19.7%）和 1.44 的 IPC。实证显示其稳定排序比现有算法快 3x-4.5x，在处理重复数据和部分有序数据时表现优异，且吞吐量可与高速非稳定排序算法（如 Skasort）媲美。

Conclusion: zSort 成功缩小了稳定排序与非稳定排序之间的性能差距，提供了一种既能保证稳定性又能保持高吞吐量的高效排序选择。

Abstract: Sorting is a foundational primitive in modern data processing, influencing the execution speed of high-performance data pipelines. However, the algorithmic landscape is currently bifurcated by a pervasive "Stability Tax": practitioners must sacrifice either order preservation for high throughput or execution speed for stability. To address these limitations, this paper introduces, zSort, an adaptive z-score based distribution sorting algorithm that guarantees stability while avoiding pass complexity that scales with key-width. The performance of the proposed technique is evaluated using Microarchitectural analysis and experimental results. Microarchitectural analysis shows that zSort achieves a lower bad-speculation overhead (19.7%) than both stable baselines and several high-performance unstable algorithms and sustains a competitive IPC of 1.44. Empirical evaluation across diverse input distributions and datasets of up to 10^7 elements (64 bit) demonstrates that zSort consistently outperforms widely used comparison based stable sorting algorithms, achieving up to 3x-4.5x speedups, and a relatively better performance compared to LSD Radix, with larger gains on duplicate heavy and partially ordered inputs. Despite providing stability, zSort achieves comparable throughput as compared to high-performance unstable algorithms such as Skasort. It also maintains this performance on adaptive workloads where methods like Pdqsort typically excel and doesn't exhibit any extreme worst case. These results indicate that zSort substantially narrows the traditional performance gap between stable and unstable sorting and provides an efficient, stable sorting alternative.

</details>


### [294] [Branch-width of represented matroids in matrix multiplication time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14428)
*Mujin Choi,Tuukka Korhonen,Sang-il Oum*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种更快计算有限域上拟阵分支宽的算法，其时间复杂度为 $O_{k,\mathbb F}(n^2) + O(n^ω)$，优于此前的 $Ω(n^3)$ 算法，并证明了该算法在矩阵乘法指数下的最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的计算拟阵分支宽的算法（如 Hliněný 等人的工作）时间复杂度均不低于 $Ω(n^3)$，作者希望利用矩阵乘法优化算法在寻找拟阵分支分解时的执行效率。

Method: 开发了一种基于算法技巧的拟阵分支分解（branch-decomposition）查找算法。若拟阵以标准型矩阵表示，算法复杂度为 $O_{k,\mathbb F}(n^2)$；若为一般的 $n \times n$ 矩阵，则在该基础上增加 $O(n^ω)$ 的标准正规化预处理时间。算法通过引入矩阵乘法指数 $ω$ 优化了之前的 $Ω(n^3)$ 复杂度。

Result: 提出了一种针对 $n$ 元素拟阵的分支分解算法，时间复杂度为 $O_{k,\mathbb F}(n^2) + O(n^ω)$。该成果被成功推广用于提升有向图秩宽（rank-width）和有限域上拟阵路径宽（path-width）的计算效率，并针对特定条件下无限域拟阵提出了近似算法。

Conclusion: 该算法在计算拟阵分支宽（branch-width）方面达到了当前最优的时间复杂度。由于判定拟阵分支宽是否为0在计算上等价于矩阵奇异性判定，因此该算法在矩阵乘法指数下的时间复杂度是难以进一步优化的。

Abstract: For an $n$-element matroid $M$ given by an $n \times n$ matrix representation over a finite field $\mathbb F$ and an integer $k$, we present an $(O_{k,\mathbb F}(n^2)+O(n^ω))$-time algorithm that either finds a branch-decomposition of $M$ of width at most $k$, or confirms that the branch-width of $M$ is more than $k$, where $ω< 2.3714$ is the matrix multiplication exponent, and the $O_{k,\mathbb F}(\cdot)$-notation hides factors that depend on $k$ and $\mathbb F$ in a computable manner. All previous algorithms including Hliněný and Oum [SIAM J. Comput. (2008)] and Jeong, Kim, and Oum [SIAM J. Discrete Math. (2021)] run in at least $Ω(n^3)$ time. Moreover, if the input matrix representation is given by a standard form, our algorithm runs in $O_{k,\mathbb F}(n^2)$-time, since $O(n^ω)$-time is only needed for finding a standard form of the input matrix. When $M$ is given by an $m \times n$ matrix, the overhead for finding a standard form is $O(mn \min(m,n)^{ω-2})$.
  As corollaries, we obtain faster algorithms for rank-width of directed graphs and path-width of matroids represented over a fixed finite field. Furthermore, we also present an approximation algorithm for finding branch-width that works on infinite fields provided that the input matrix is of a standard form and contains a bounded number of distinct values of entries.
  To suggest that our algorithm is optimal, we observe that for every field $\mathbb F$, deciding whether the branch-width of a matroid represented over $\mathbb F$ is $0$ is as hard as deciding whether a square matrix over $\mathbb F$ is singular. Under the assumption that singularity testing requires $Ω(n^ω)$-time, this implies that the overhead of $O(n^ω)$ is unavoidable. We also show strengthenings of this observation to rule out some approximations under this assumption.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [295] [SemaTune: Semantic-Aware Online OS Tuning with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15026)
*Georgios Liargkovas,Mihir Nitin Joshi,Hubertus Franke,Kostis Kaffes*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: SemaTune 是一种基于大语言模型指导的 OS 调优框架，通过将参数调优转化为具有语义感知的受限决策过程，实现了高效、低风险的系统性能在线优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线操作系统（OS）调优控制器通常将系统参数视为黑盒，忽略了参数间的结构依赖关系，且容易在缺乏应用层指标的情况下将系统推向性能退化区域。

Method: 提出了 SemaTune 框架：采用双循环控制策略（快速循环用于低延迟更新，慢速循环用于策略修正），结合语义感知的决策上下文（模式、遥测、历史记录）和类型安全验证机制，通过大语言模型导向来进行受限的 OS 参数探索。

Result: 在涵盖 5 个基准套件的 13 个工作负载测试中，SemaTune 相比默认设置性能提升 72.5%，相比最强的非 LLM 基线性能提升 153.3%；在仅使用主机级指标的情况下，依然展现出优于已知应用目标的基线性能，且有效避免了严重退化。

Conclusion: SemaTune 通过引入具有语义约束的大语言模型架构，有效地解决了在线 OS 调优中跨参数依赖性及黑盒优化的局限性，实现了在降低性能退化风险的同时显著提升系统性能。

Abstract: Online OS tuning can improve long-running services, but existing controllers are poorly matched to live hosts. They treat scheduler, power, memory, and I/O controls as black-box variables and optimize a scalar reward. This view ignores cross-knob policy structure, breaks down when application metrics are unavailable, and can send a running service into degraded regions that persist after the bad setting is removed. We present SemaTune, a host-side framework for steady-state OS tuning with bounded language-model guidance. SemaTune turns knob schemas, telemetry, current configuration, recent action--response history, and retrieved prior runs into a compact decision context. A fast loop proposes low-latency updates, a slower loop periodically revises the search strategy, and every proposed change passes through typed validation before reaching kernel or sysctl interfaces. This lets the controller reason about OS-control meaning and indirect performance signals while keeping model cost, latency, and authority constrained. We evaluate SemaTune on 13 live workloads from five benchmark suites while tuning up to 41 Linux parameters. Across the suite, SemaTune improves stable-phase performance by 72.5\% over default settings and by 153.3\% relative to the strongest non-LLM baseline. A 30-window session costs about \$0.20 in model calls. With only host-level metrics, SemaTune still outperforms baselines given direct application objectives by 93.7 percentage points, while avoiding severe degraded regions reached by structure-blind exploration.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [296] [From Schema to Signal: Retrieval-Augmented Modeling for Relational Data Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14464)
*Lingze Zeng,Shaofeng Cai,Changshuo Liu,Zhongle Xie,Yuncheng Wu,Beng Chin Ooi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: RAM是一种结合图结构与属性语义的检索增强框架，通过信息检索技术挖掘元组间的深层关系，显著提升了关系型数据库中的表示学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度学习模型在处理单表数据时表现优异，但在处理具有复杂多表关系和规范化结构的关系型数据库时，往往受限于硬性的Schema结构，忽略了元组属性中蕴含的隐式语义信号。
